(3.26)

Step 3
.
Derivation of the modulaltion vector
. In view of (
3.7
), we have

\displaystyle\partial_{s}w_{0}-\frac{\lambda_{s}}{\lambda}\varLambda w_{0}+\varTheta Z{\rm R}w_{0}

(3.27)

\displaystyle=a_{s}\varPhi_{1,0}+b_{s}\varPhi_{0,1}+\sum\partial_{s}(a^{i}b^{j})\varPhi_{i,j}+\sum a^{i}b^{j}b_{s}\partial_{b}\varPhi_{i,j}+2bb_{s}S_{0,2}

\displaystyle-\Big(\frac{\lambda_{s}}{\lambda}+b\Big)\varLambda w_{0}+ab\varLambda\varPhi_{1,0}+b^{2}\varLambda\varPhi_{0,1}+\sum a^{i}b^{j+1}\varLambda\varPhi_{i,j}+b^{3}\varLambda S_{0,2}

\displaystyle+\big(\varTheta_{s}+a\big)Z{\rm R}w_{0}-a^{2}Z{\rm R}\hskip 0.50003pt\varPhi_{1,0}-abZ{\rm R}\hskip 0.50003pt\varPhi_{0,1}-\sum a^{i+1}b^{j}Z{\rm R}\hskip 0.50003pt\varPhi_{i,j}-ab^{2}Z{\rm R}\varPhi_{i,j}

\displaystyle=-\Big(\frac{\lambda_{s}}{\lambda}+b\Big)\varLambda w_{0}+\big(\varTheta_{s}+a\big)Z{\rm R}w_{0}+a_{s}\Big(\varPhi_{1,0}+\sum ia^{i-1}b^{j}\varPhi_{i,j}\Big)

\displaystyle+\big(\partial_{s}b+a^{2}+b^{2}\big)\Big(\varPhi_{0,1}+2b\,S_{0,2}+\sum ja^{i}b^{j-1}\varPhi_{i,j}+\sum a^{i}b^{j}\partial_{b}\varPhi_{i,j}\Big)

\displaystyle+ab\big(\varLambda\varPhi_{1,0}-\varPhi_{1,0}\big)+b^{2}\big(\varLambda\varPhi_{0,1}-\varPhi_{0,1}\big)+b^{3}\big(\varLambda S_{0,2}-2S_{0,2}\big)

\displaystyle+ab(1+Z)\hskip 0.50003pt\varPhi_{1,0}-a^{2}(1+Z)\hskip 0.50003pt\varPhi_{0,1}-2a^{2}b\,S_{0,2}

\displaystyle+\sum a^{i}b^{j}\Big(b\varLambda\varPhi_{i,j}-aZ{\rm R}\varPhi_{i,j}\Big)-(a^{2}+b^{2})\sum\Big(ja^{i}b^{j-1}\varPhi_{i,j}+a^{i}b^{j}\partial_{b}\varPhi_{i,j}\Big).

where (
3.23
) has been applied. The above summations run over the indices
i,j
for
2\leq i+j\leq 3
. By collecting the terms with coefficients
(\varTheta_{s}+a),(\lambda_{s}/\lambda+b),a_{s},(b_{s}+a^{2}+b^{2})
in (
3.17
), (
3.27
), we obtain the modulation vector (
3.8
), and also the error

\displaystyle\varPsi_{0}

\displaystyle={\rm\hat{J}}\hskip 1.00006pt\Bigg\{(\rho_{1}-\rho_{2}{\rm R})\hskip-1.00006pt\begin{bmatrix}\,{\it\mathbb{H}}^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-2.26666pt}{\resizebox{868894}{445645}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.75pt}{$\displaystyle(1)$}}}}}{\raisebox{-2.26666pt}{\resizebox{868894}{445645}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.75pt}{$\textstyle(1)$}}}}}{\raisebox{-2.26665pt}{\resizebox{759247}{445645}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.22499pt}{$\scriptstyle(1)$}}}}}{\raisebox{-2.26666pt}{\resizebox{759248}{445645}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.875pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle(1)$}}}}}}(w_{0}^{2})\,\\
{\it\mathbb{H}}^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-2.26666pt}{\resizebox{868894}{445645}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.75pt}{$\displaystyle(2)$}}}}}{\raisebox{-2.26666pt}{\resizebox{868894}{445645}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.75pt}{$\textstyle(2)$}}}}}{\raisebox{-2.26665pt}{\resizebox{759247}{445645}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.22499pt}{$\scriptstyle(2)$}}}}}{\raisebox{-2.26666pt}{\resizebox{759248}{445645}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.875pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle(2)$}}}}}}(w_{0}^{2})\\
0\end{bmatrix}-2b^{2}(1\hskip-1.00006pt+\hskip-1.00006ptZ)\hskip 1.00006pt\partial_{y}S_{0,2}^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-2.26666pt}{\resizebox{868894}{445645}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.75pt}{$\displaystyle(3)$}}}}}{\raisebox{-2.26666pt}{\resizebox{868894}{445645}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.75pt}{$\textstyle(3)$}}}}}{\raisebox{-2.26665pt}{\resizebox{759247}{445645}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.22499pt}{$\scriptstyle(3)$}}}}}{\raisebox{-2.26666pt}{\resizebox{759248}{445645}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.875pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle(3)$}}}}}}\hskip 1.00006pt\big(\rho_{1}e_{x}-\rho_{2}e_{y}\big)

(3.28)

\displaystyle-\rho_{2}w_{0}\hskip-1.00006pt\wedge\hskip-1.00006pt{\it\mathbb{H}}w_{0}-\rho_{1}b^{2}\Delta S_{0,2}^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-2.26666pt}{\resizebox{868894}{445645}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.75pt}{$\displaystyle(3)$}}}}}{\raisebox{-2.26666pt}{\resizebox{868894}{445645}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.75pt}{$\textstyle(3)$}}}}}{\raisebox{-2.26665pt}{\resizebox{759247}{445645}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.22499pt}{$\scriptstyle(3)$}}}}}{\raisebox{-2.26666pt}{\resizebox{759248}{445645}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.875pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle(3)$}}}}}}\,e_{z}+2\rho_{1}(1\hskip-1.00006pt+\hskip-1.00006ptZ)\sum_{i=1,2}\Big(\partial_{y}\hskip-1.00006pt\hskip-1.00006pt+\hskip-1.00006pt\hskip-1.00006pt\frac{Z}{y}\Big)(w_{0}^{i})^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-2.26666pt}{\resizebox{868894}{445645}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.75pt}{$\displaystyle(1)$}}}}}{\raisebox{-2.26666pt}{\resizebox{868894}{445645}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.75pt}{$\textstyle(1)$}}}}}{\raisebox{-2.26665pt}{\resizebox{759247}{445645}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.22499pt}{$\scriptstyle(1)$}}}}}{\raisebox{-2.26666pt}{\resizebox{759248}{445645}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.875pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle(1)$}}}}}}\hskip 0.50003pte_{z}\Bigg\}

\displaystyle+ab\big(\varLambda\varPhi_{1,0}-\varPhi_{1,0}\big)+b^{2}\big(\varLambda\varPhi_{0,1}-\varPhi_{0,1}\big)+b^{3}\big(\varLambda S_{0,2}-2S_{0,2}\big)

\displaystyle+ab(1+Z)\hskip 0.50003pt\varPhi_{1,0}-a^{2}(1+Z)\hskip 0.50003pt\varPhi_{0,1}-2a^{2}b\,S_{0,2}

\displaystyle+\hskip-6.99997pt\sum_{2\leq i+j\leq 3}\hskip-6.00006pta^{i}b^{j}\Big(b\varLambda\varPhi_{i,j}\hskip-1.00006pt-\hskip-1.00006ptaZ{\rm R}\varPhi_{i,j}\Big)-(a^{2}+b^{2})\hskip-6.99997pt\sum_{2\leq i+j\leq 3}\hskip-5.0pt\Big(ja^{i}b^{j-1}\varPhi_{i,j}+a^{i}b^{j}\partial_{b}\varPhi_{i,j}\Big).

Step 4
.
The
\varSigma_{b}
function
. To create cancellations on the forthcoming growing tails of the second order profiles (see (
3.33
), (
3.34
)), we introduce the radiation
\varSigma_{b}
defined by

H\varSigma_{b}=c_{b}\chi_{\frac{B_{0}}{4}}\varLambda\phi-d_{b}H\Big[(1-\chi_{3B_{0}})\varLambda\phi\Big],

(3.29)

where the coefficients are

\left\{\begin{aligned} &c_{b}=\frac{4}{\int\chi_{\frac{B_{0}}{4}}(\varLambda\phi)^{2}xdx}=\frac{2}{\left|\log b\right|}\bigg(1+O\Big(\frac{1}{\left|\log b\right|}\Big)\bigg),\\
&d_{b}=c_{b}\int\chi_{\frac{B_{0}}{4}}\varLambda\phi\varGamma xdx=O\bigg(\frac{1}{b\left|\log b\right|}\bigg).\end{aligned}\right.

(3.30)

Applying (
2.33
), we see

\displaystyle\varSigma_{b}(y)=-\varGamma\int_{0}^{y}c_{b}\chi_{\frac{B_{0}}{4}}(\varLambda\phi)^{2}+\varLambda\phi\int_{0}^{y}c_{b}\chi_{\frac{B_{0}}{4}}\varLambda\phi\varGamma-d_{b}\big(1-\chi_{3B_{0}}\big)\varLambda\phi,

which implies

\varSigma_{b}=\begin{cases}c_{b}T_{1}&\mbox{for}\;\;y\leq\frac{B_{0}}{4},\\
-4\varGamma&\mbox{for}\;\;y\geq 6B_{0}.\end{cases}

(3.31)

From (
2.31
), (
2.32
), there holds the asymptotics: for
y\geq 6B_{0}
,

\varSigma_{b}(y)=-y+O\bigg(\frac{\log y}{y}\bigg).

while for
1\leq y\leq 6B_{0}
,

\displaystyle\varSigma_{b}(y)

\displaystyle=-c_{b}\bigg(\frac{y}{4}+O\bigg(\frac{\log y}{y}\bigg)\bigg)\int_{0}^{y}\chi_{\frac{B_{0}}{4}}(\varLambda\phi)^{2}xdx

\displaystyle+c_{b}\,\varLambda\phi\int_{1}^{y}O(x)dx+O\bigg(\frac{1_{y\geq 3B_{0}}}{yb\left|\log b\right|}\bigg)

\displaystyle=-y\frac{\int_{0}^{y}\chi_{\frac{B_{0}}{4}}(\varLambda\phi)^{2}xdx}{\int\chi_{\frac{B_{0}}{4}}(\varLambda\phi)^{2}xdx}+O\bigg(\frac{1+y}{\left|\log b\right|}\bigg).

Furthermore, the derivatives of
c_{b},d_{b}
on
b
are

\partial_{b}c_{b}=O\bigg(\frac{1}{b\left|\log b\right|^{2}}\bigg),\quad\partial_{b}d_{b}=O\bigg(\frac{1}{b^{2}\left|\log b\right|}\bigg),

and thus

\displaystyle\partial_{b}\varSigma_{b}

\displaystyle=\partial_{b}c_{b}\,T_{1}\,\mathbf{1}_{y\leq\frac{B_{0}}{4}}+O\bigg(\frac{1}{b^{2}y\left|\log b\right|}\bigg)\mathbf{1}_{\frac{B_{0}}{4}\leq y\leq 6B_{0}}

(3.32)

\displaystyle=O\bigg(\frac{y^{3}}{b\left|\log b\right|^{2}}\,\mathbf{1}_{y\leq 1}+\frac{y}{b\left|\log b\right|}\,\mathbf{1}_{1\leq y\leq 6B_{0}}\bigg).

Step 5
.
Manipulation on the growing tails
. Due to the explicit construction (
3.22
), (
3.25
) based on
T_{1}
, we observe the pattern
\varLambda T_{1}-T_{1}
appears in each term in the third line on the RHS of (
3.28
). By (
2.35
), the asymptotics of
\varLambda T_{1}-T_{1}
at the scale of
y\sim 2B_{1}
is

\varLambda T_{1}-T_{1}=-y+O\bigg(\frac{(\log y)^{2}}{y}\bigg),

(3.33)

suggesting the potential fast growing tails of the second order profiles (see (
3.45
), (
3.46
)), which will make the approximate solution out of control. To fix this, we use
\varSigma_{b}
to create the following cancellation for
6B_{0}\leq y\leq 2B_{1}
:

\varLambda T_{1}-T_{1}-\varSigma_{b}=O\bigg(\frac{(\log y)^{2}}{y}\bigg).

(3.34)

For
1\leq y\leq 6B_{0}
, there holds

\displaystyle\varLambda T_{1}-T_{1}-\varSigma_{b}

\displaystyle=-y\Bigg(1-\frac{\int_{0}^{y}\chi_{\frac{B_{0}}{4}}(\varLambda\phi)^{2}xdx}{\int\chi_{\frac{B_{0}}{4}}(\varLambda\phi)^{2}xdx}\Bigg)+O\bigg(\frac{(\log y)^{2}}{y}\bigg)+O\bigg(\frac{1+y}{\log b}\bigg)

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{y}{\left|\log b\right|}\Big(1+\left|\log(\right.\hskip-1.00006pt\hskip-1.00006pty\sqrt{b}\left.\hskip-1.00006pt\hskip-1.00006pt)\right|\Big).

These with (
2.33
) yield the bound

\big|H^{-1}\big(\varLambda T_{1}\hskip-1.00006pt-\hskip-1.00006ptT_{1}\hskip-1.00006pt-\hskip-1.00006pt\varSigma_{b}\big)\big|=O\bigg(y^{5}\mathbf{1}_{y\leq 1}+\frac{1+y}{b\left|\log b\right|}\mathbf{1}_{1\leq y\leq 2B_{1}}\bigg)\lesssim\frac{1+y}{b\left|\log b\right|}.

(3.35)

Therefore we define the vectorial functions

\varSigma_{1,0}\hskip-1.00006pt=\hskip-1.00006pt\frac{1}{\rho_{1}^{2}+\rho_{2}^{2}}\hskip-1.00006pt\begin{bmatrix}\,\rho_{1}\,\\
\rho_{2}\\
0\end{bmatrix}\hskip-1.00006pt\hskip-1.00006pt\varSigma_{b},\quad\varSigma_{0,1}\hskip-1.00006pt=\hskip-1.00006pt\frac{1}{\rho_{1}^{2}+\rho_{2}^{2}}\hskip-1.00006pt\begin{bmatrix}-\rho_{2}\\
\rho_{1}\\
0\end{bmatrix}\hskip-1.00006pt\hskip-1.00006pt\varSigma_{b},\quad\varSigma_{0,2}\hskip-1.00006pt=\hskip-1.00006pt\frac{1}{\rho_{1}^{2}+\rho_{2}^{2}}T_{1}\varSigma_{b}\,e_{z},

corresponding to the cancellations of
\varLambda\varPhi_{1,0}-\varPhi_{1,0},\varLambda\varPhi_{0,1}-\varPhi_{0,1},\varLambda S_{0,2}-S_{0,2}
respectively. Similar to (
3.23
), we have

\varSigma_{0,1}={\rm R}\varSigma_{1,0},\quad\varSigma_{1,0}=-{\rm R}\varSigma_{0,1}.

Then (
3.28
) can be rewritten as

\displaystyle\varPsi_{0}

\displaystyle={\rm\hat{J}}\hskip 1.00006pt\Bigg\{(\rho_{1}-\rho_{2}{\rm R})\hskip-1.00006pt\begin{bmatrix}\,{\it\mathbb{H}}^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-2.26666pt}{\resizebox{868894}{445645}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.75pt}{$\displaystyle(1)$}}}}}{\raisebox{-2.26666pt}{\resizebox{868894}{445645}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.75pt}{$\textstyle(1)$}}}}}{\raisebox{-2.26665pt}{\resizebox{759247}{445645}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.22499pt}{$\scriptstyle(1)$}}}}}{\raisebox{-2.26666pt}{\resizebox{759248}{445645}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.875pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle(1)$}}}}}}(w_{0}^{2})\,\\
{\it\mathbb{H}}^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-2.26666pt}{\resizebox{868894}{445645}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.75pt}{$\displaystyle(2)$}}}}}{\raisebox{-2.26666pt}{\resizebox{868894}{445645}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.75pt}{$\textstyle(2)$}}}}}{\raisebox{-2.26665pt}{\resizebox{759247}{445645}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.22499pt}{$\scriptstyle(2)$}}}}}{\raisebox{-2.26666pt}{\resizebox{759248}{445645}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.875pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle(2)$}}}}}}(w_{0}^{2})\\
0\end{bmatrix}-2b^{2}(1\hskip-1.00006pt+\hskip-1.00006ptZ)\hskip 1.00006pt\partial_{y}S_{0,2}^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-2.26666pt}{\resizebox{868894}{445645}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.75pt}{$\displaystyle(3)$}}}}}{\raisebox{-2.26666pt}{\resizebox{868894}{445645}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.75pt}{$\textstyle(3)$}}}}}{\raisebox{-2.26665pt}{\resizebox{759247}{445645}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.22499pt}{$\scriptstyle(3)$}}}}}{\raisebox{-2.26666pt}{\resizebox{759248}{445645}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.875pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle(3)$}}}}}}\hskip 1.00006pt\big(\rho_{1}e_{x}-\rho_{2}e_{y}\big)

(3.36)

\displaystyle-\rho_{2}w_{0}\hskip-1.00006pt\wedge\hskip-1.00006pt{\it\mathbb{H}}w_{0}-\rho_{1}b^{2}\Delta S_{0,2}^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-2.26666pt}{\resizebox{868894}{445645}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.75pt}{$\displaystyle(3)$}}}}}{\raisebox{-2.26666pt}{\resizebox{868894}{445645}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.75pt}{$\textstyle(3)$}}}}}{\raisebox{-2.26665pt}{\resizebox{759247}{445645}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.22499pt}{$\scriptstyle(3)$}}}}}{\raisebox{-2.26666pt}{\resizebox{759248}{445645}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.875pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle(3)$}}}}}}\,e_{z}+2\rho_{1}(1\hskip-1.00006pt+\hskip-1.00006ptZ)\sum_{i=1,2}\Big(\partial_{y}\hskip-1.00006pt\hskip-1.00006pt+\hskip-1.00006pt\hskip-1.00006pt\frac{Z}{y}\Big)(w_{0}^{i})^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-2.26666pt}{\resizebox{868894}{445645}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.75pt}{$\displaystyle(1)$}}}}}{\raisebox{-2.26666pt}{\resizebox{868894}{445645}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.75pt}{$\textstyle(1)$}}}}}{\raisebox{-2.26665pt}{\resizebox{759247}{445645}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.22499pt}{$\scriptstyle(1)$}}}}}{\raisebox{-2.26666pt}{\resizebox{759248}{445645}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.875pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle(1)$}}}}}}\hskip 0.50003pte_{z}\Bigg\}

\displaystyle+\sum_{2\leq i+j\leq 4}a^{i}b^{j}E_{i,j}+b^{3}\big(\varLambda S_{0,2}-2S_{0,2}+\varSigma_{0,2}\big)

\displaystyle+ab\varSigma_{1,0}+b^{2}\varSigma_{0,1}-b^{3}\varSigma_{0,2}-2a^{2}b\hskip 1.00006ptS_{0,2}-(a^{2}+b^{2})\hskip-5.0pt\sum_{2\leq i+j\leq 3}\hskip-5.0pta^{i}b^{j}\partial_{b}\varPhi_{i,j}.

where

\displaystyle\sum_{2\leq i+j\leq 4}\hskip-3.99994pta^{i}b^{j}E_{i,j}

\displaystyle=ab(1+Z)\hskip 0.50003pt\varPhi_{1,0}-a^{2}(1+Z)\hskip 0.50003pt\varPhi_{0,1}

(3.37)

\displaystyle+ab\big(\varLambda\varPhi_{1,0}-\varPhi_{1,0}-\varSigma_{1,0}\big)+b^{2}\big(\varLambda\varPhi_{0,1}-\varPhi_{0,1}-\varSigma_{0,1}\big)

\displaystyle+\sum_{2\leq i+j\leq 3}\hskip-3.00003pta^{i}b^{j}\Big(b\varLambda\varPhi_{i,j}-aZ{\rm R}\varPhi_{i,j}\Big)-(a^{2}+b^{2})\hskip-5.0pt\sum_{2\leq i+j\leq 3}\hskip-5.0ptja^{i}b^{j-1}\varPhi_{i,j}.

Step 6
.
The
{\rm\hat{J}}
structure
. From the definition (
2.25
), we have the identity

{\rm\hat{J}}e_{z}=(e_{z}+w_{0})\wedge e_{z}=-(e_{z}+w_{0})\wedge w_{0}=-{\rm\hat{J}}w_{0}.

(3.38)

It implies the smallness of the third component under the action of
{\rm\hat{J}}
, which is helpful in analyzing the terms inside the big brace in (
3.36

3.36

\displaystyle w_{0}\wedge{\it\mathbb{H}}w_{0}

\displaystyle=w_{0}^{1}\wedge{\it\mathbb{H}}w_{0}^{1}+w_{0}^{1}\wedge{\it\mathbb{H}}w_{0}^{2}+b^{2}w_{0}^{1}\wedge{\it\mathbb{H}}S_{0,2}

(3.39)

\displaystyle+w_{0}^{2}\wedge{\it\mathbb{H}}w_{0}^{1}+w_{0}^{2}\wedge{\it\mathbb{H}}w_{0}^{2}+b^{2}w_{0}^{2}\wedge{\it\mathbb{H}}S_{0,2}

\displaystyle+b^{2}S_{0,2}\wedge{\it\mathbb{H}}w_{0}^{1}+b^{2}S_{0,2}\wedge{\it\mathbb{H}}w_{0}^{2}+b^{4}S_{0,2}\wedge{\it\mathbb{H}}S_{0,2}.

From (

), (

\displaystyle w_{0}^{1}\wedge{\it\mathbb{H}}w_{0}^{1}

\displaystyle=w_{0}^{1}\wedge\begin{bmatrix}{\it\mathbb{H}}^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-2.26666pt}{\resizebox{868894}{445645}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.75pt}{$\displaystyle(1)$}}}}}{\raisebox{-2.26666pt}{\resizebox{868894}{445645}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.75pt}{$\textstyle(1)$}}}}}{\raisebox{-2.26665pt}{\resizebox{759247}{445645}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.22499pt}{$\scriptstyle(1)$}}}}}{\raisebox{-2.26666pt}{\resizebox{759248}{445645}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.875pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle(1)$}}}}}}(w_{0}^{1})\\
{\it\mathbb{H}}^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-2.26666pt}{\resizebox{868894}{445645}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.75pt}{$\displaystyle(2)$}}}}}{\raisebox{-2.26666pt}{\resizebox{868894}{445645}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.75pt}{$\textstyle(2)$}}}}}{\raisebox{-2.26665pt}{\resizebox{759247}{445645}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.22499pt}{$\scriptstyle(2)$}}}}}{\raisebox{-2.26666pt}{\resizebox{759248}{445645}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.875pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle(2)$}}}}}}(w_{0}^{1})\\
0\end{bmatrix}+w_{0}^{1}\wedge\begin{bmatrix}0\\
0\\
{\it\mathbb{H}}^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-2.26666pt}{\resizebox{868894}{445645}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.75pt}{$\displaystyle(3)$}}}}}{\raisebox{-2.26666pt}{\resizebox{868894}{445645}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.75pt}{$\textstyle(3)$}}}}}{\raisebox{-2.26665pt}{\resizebox{759247}{445645}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.22499pt}{$\scriptstyle(3)$}}}}}{\raisebox{-2.26666pt}{\resizebox{759248}{445645}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.875pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle(3)$}}}}}}(w_{0}^{1})\end{bmatrix}

\displaystyle=\big(a\hskip 0.50003pt\varPhi_{1,0}+b\hskip 0.50003pt\varPhi_{0,1}\big)\wedge\mathbb{H^{\perp}}\big(a\hskip 0.50003pt\varPhi_{1,0}+b\hskip 0.50003pt\varPhi_{0,1}\big)-{\it\mathbb{H}}^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-2.26666pt}{\resizebox{868894}{445645}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.75pt}{$\displaystyle(3)$}}}}}{\raisebox{-2.26666pt}{\resizebox{868894}{445645}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.75pt}{$\textstyle(3)$}}}}}{\raisebox{-2.26665pt}{\resizebox{759247}{445645}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.22499pt}{$\scriptstyle(3)$}}}}}{\raisebox{-2.26666pt}{\resizebox{759248}{445645}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.875pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle(3)$}}}}}}(w_{0}^{1})\big(e_{z}\wedge w_{0}^{1}\big)

\displaystyle=-2(1\hskip-1.00006pt+\hskip-1.00006ptZ)\Big(\partial_{y}\hskip-1.00006pt\hskip-1.00006pt+\hskip-1.00006pt\hskip-1.00006pt\frac{Z}{y}\Big)(w_{0}^{1})^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-2.26666pt}{\resizebox{868894}{445645}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.75pt}{$\displaystyle(1)$}}}}}{\raisebox{-2.26666pt}{\resizebox{868894}{445645}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.75pt}{$\textstyle(1)$}}}}}{\raisebox{-2.26665pt}{\resizebox{759247}{445645}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.22499pt}{$\scriptstyle(1)$}}}}}{\raisebox{-2.26666pt}{\resizebox{759248}{445645}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.875pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle(1)$}}}}}}\hskip 1.00006pt{\rm R}w_{0}^{1}.

Besides, (

\displaystyle{\rm\hat{J}}\big(w_{0}^{1}\wedge{\it\mathbb{H}}w_{0}^{2}\big)

\displaystyle={\rm\hat{J}}\Bigg\{O\Big(|w_{0}^{1}|\,|{\it\mathbb{H}}w_{0}^{2}|\,|w_{0}|\Big)-2(1\hskip-1.00006pt+\hskip-1.00006ptZ)\Big(\partial_{y}\hskip-1.00006pt\hskip-1.00006pt+\hskip-1.00006pt\hskip-1.00006pt\frac{Z}{y}\Big)(w_{0}^{2})^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-2.26666pt}{\resizebox{868894}{445645}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.75pt}{$\displaystyle(1)$}}}}}{\raisebox{-2.26666pt}{\resizebox{868894}{445645}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.75pt}{$\textstyle(1)$}}}}}{\raisebox{-2.26665pt}{\resizebox{759247}{445645}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.22499pt}{$\scriptstyle(1)$}}}}}{\raisebox{-2.26666pt}{\resizebox{759248}{445645}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.875pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle(1)$}}}}}}\hskip 1.00006pt{\rm R}w_{0}^{1}\Bigg\}.

An analogous identity switching the position of

and

holds for

. Again from (
3.19
),
S_{0,2}=S_{0,2}^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-2.26666pt}{\resizebox{868894}{445645}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.75pt}{$\displaystyle(3)$}}}}}{\raisebox{-2.26666pt}{\resizebox{868894}{445645}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.75pt}{$\textstyle(3)$}}}}}{\raisebox{-2.26665pt}{\resizebox{759247}{445645}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.22499pt}{$\scriptstyle(3)$}}}}}{\raisebox{-2.26666pt}{\resizebox{759248}{445645}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.875pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle(3)$}}}}}}\,e_{z}
, and then direct computations give

\displaystyle{\rm\hat{J}}\Big(b^{2}w_{0}^{1}\wedge{\it\mathbb{H}}

\displaystyle S_{0,2}+b^{2}S_{0,2}\wedge{\it\mathbb{H}}w_{0}^{1}\Big)

\displaystyle={\rm\hat{J}}\Big(b^{2}\Delta S_{0,2}^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-2.26666pt}{\resizebox{868894}{445645}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.75pt}{$\displaystyle(3)$}}}}}{\raisebox{-2.26666pt}{\resizebox{868894}{445645}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.75pt}{$\textstyle(3)$}}}}}{\raisebox{-2.26665pt}{\resizebox{759247}{445645}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.22499pt}{$\scriptstyle(3)$}}}}}{\raisebox{-2.26666pt}{\resizebox{759248}{445645}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.875pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle(3)$}}}}}}\hskip 1.00006pt{\rm R}w_{0}^{1}+2b^{2}(1\hskip-1.00006pt+\hskip-1.00006ptZ)\hskip 0.50003pt\partial_{y}S_{0,2}^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-2.26666pt}{\resizebox{868894}{445645}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.75pt}{$\displaystyle(3)$}}}}}{\raisebox{-2.26666pt}{\resizebox{868894}{445645}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.75pt}{$\textstyle(3)$}}}}}{\raisebox{-2.26665pt}{\resizebox{759247}{445645}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.22499pt}{$\scriptstyle(3)$}}}}}{\raisebox{-2.26666pt}{\resizebox{759248}{445645}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.875pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle(3)$}}}}}}\hskip 1.00006pt(w_{0}^{1})^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-2.26666pt}{\resizebox{868894}{445645}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.75pt}{$\displaystyle(2)$}}}}}{\raisebox{-2.26666pt}{\resizebox{868894}{445645}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.75pt}{$\textstyle(2)$}}}}}{\raisebox{-2.26665pt}{\resizebox{759247}{445645}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.22499pt}{$\scriptstyle(2)$}}}}}{\raisebox{-2.26666pt}{\resizebox{759248}{445645}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.875pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle(2)$}}}}}}\hskip 0.50003ptw_{0}+b^{2}S_{0,2}^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-2.26666pt}{\resizebox{868894}{445645}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.75pt}{$\displaystyle(3)$}}}}}{\raisebox{-2.26666pt}{\resizebox{868894}{445645}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.75pt}{$\textstyle(3)$}}}}}{\raisebox{-2.26665pt}{\resizebox{759247}{445645}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.22499pt}{$\scriptstyle(3)$}}}}}{\raisebox{-2.26666pt}{\resizebox{759248}{445645}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.875pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle(3)$}}}}}}\hskip 1.00006pt{\rm R}{\it\mathbb{H}}w_{0}^{1}\Big).

Combining these, the nonlinear term is actually given by

\displaystyle{\rm\hat{J}}\big(-\hskip-1.00006pt\rho_{2}w_{0}\hskip-1.00006pt\wedge\hskip-1.00006pt{\it\mathbb{H}}w_{0}\big)

\displaystyle={\rm\hat{J}}\Bigg\{\,2\rho_{2}(1\hskip-1.00006pt+\hskip-1.00006ptZ)\sum_{i+j\leq 3}\Big(\partial_{y}\hskip-1.00006pt\hskip-1.00006pt+\hskip-1.00006pt\hskip-1.00006pt\frac{Z}{y}\Big)(w_{0}^{i})^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-2.26666pt}{\resizebox{868894}{445645}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.75pt}{$\displaystyle(1)$}}}}}{\raisebox{-2.26666pt}{\resizebox{868894}{445645}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.75pt}{$\textstyle(1)$}}}}}{\raisebox{-2.26665pt}{\resizebox{759247}{445645}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.22499pt}{$\scriptstyle(1)$}}}}}{\raisebox{-2.26666pt}{\resizebox{759248}{445645}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.875pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle(1)$}}}}}}\hskip 1.00006pt{\rm R}w_{0}^{j}

(3.40)

\displaystyle-\rho_{2}b^{2}\Delta S_{0,2}^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-2.26666pt}{\resizebox{868894}{445645}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.75pt}{$\displaystyle(3)$}}}}}{\raisebox{-2.26666pt}{\resizebox{868894}{445645}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.75pt}{$\textstyle(3)$}}}}}{\raisebox{-2.26665pt}{\resizebox{759247}{445645}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.22499pt}{$\scriptstyle(3)$}}}}}{\raisebox{-2.26666pt}{\resizebox{759248}{445645}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.875pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle(3)$}}}}}}\hskip 1.00006pt{\rm R}w_{0}^{1}-\rho_{2}b^{2}S_{0,2}^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-2.26666pt}{\resizebox{868894}{445645}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.75pt}{$\displaystyle(3)$}}}}}{\raisebox{-2.26666pt}{\resizebox{868894}{445645}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.75pt}{$\textstyle(3)$}}}}}{\raisebox{-2.26665pt}{\resizebox{759247}{445645}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.22499pt}{$\scriptstyle(3)$}}}}}{\raisebox{-2.26666pt}{\resizebox{759248}{445645}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.875pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle(3)$}}}}}}\hskip 1.00006pt{\rm R}{\it\mathbb{H}}w_{0}^{1}+\sum_{4\leq i+j\leq 7}a^{i}b^{j}R_{i,j}\Bigg\},

where

\displaystyle\sum_{4\leq i+j\leq 7}a^{i}b^{j}R_{i,j}

\displaystyle=-\rho_{2}\bigg\{\hskip 0.50003pt2b^{2}(1\hskip-1.00006pt+\hskip-1.00006ptZ)\hskip 1.00006pt\partial_{y}S_{0,2}^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-2.26666pt}{\resizebox{868894}{445645}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.75pt}{$\displaystyle(3)$}}}}}{\raisebox{-2.26666pt}{\resizebox{868894}{445645}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.75pt}{$\textstyle(3)$}}}}}{\raisebox{-2.26665pt}{\resizebox{759247}{445645}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.22499pt}{$\scriptstyle(3)$}}}}}{\raisebox{-2.26666pt}{\resizebox{759248}{445645}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.875pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle(3)$}}}}}}\hskip 1.00006pt(w_{0}^{1})^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-2.26666pt}{\resizebox{868894}{445645}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.75pt}{$\displaystyle(2)$}}}}}{\raisebox{-2.26666pt}{\resizebox{868894}{445645}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.75pt}{$\textstyle(2)$}}}}}{\raisebox{-2.26665pt}{\resizebox{759247}{445645}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.22499pt}{$\scriptstyle(2)$}}}}}{\raisebox{-2.26666pt}{\resizebox{759248}{445645}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.875pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle(2)$}}}}}}\hskip 0.50003ptw_{0}+w_{0}^{2}\wedge{\it\mathbb{H}}w_{0}^{2}

\displaystyle+b^{2}w_{0}^{2}\wedge{\it\mathbb{H}}S_{0,2}+b^{2}S_{0,2}\wedge{\it\mathbb{H}}w_{0}^{2}+b^{4}S_{0,2}\wedge{\it\mathbb{H}}S_{0,2}

\displaystyle+O\Big(|w_{0}^{1}|\,|{\it\mathbb{H}}w_{0}^{2}|\,|w_{0}|\Big)+O\Big(|w_{0}^{2}|\,|{\it\mathbb{H}}w_{0}^{1}|\,|w_{0}|\Big)\bigg\}.

Next, for the last two terms in the brace in (

\displaystyle{\rm\hat{J}}\bigg\{-\rho_{1}b^{2}\Delta S_{0,2}^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-2.26666pt}{\resizebox{868894}{445645}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.75pt}{$\displaystyle(3)$}}}}}{\raisebox{-2.26666pt}{\resizebox{868894}{445645}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.75pt}{$\textstyle(3)$}}}}}{\raisebox{-2.26665pt}{\resizebox{759247}{445645}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.22499pt}{$\scriptstyle(3)$}}}}}{\raisebox{-2.26666pt}{\resizebox{759248}{445645}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.875pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle(3)$}}}}}}\hskip 0.50003pte_{z}

(3.41)

\displaystyle={\rm\hat{J}}\bigg\{\rho_{1}b^{2}\Delta S_{0,2}^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-2.26666pt}{\resizebox{868894}{445645}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.75pt}{$\displaystyle(3)$}}}}}{\raisebox{-2.26666pt}{\resizebox{868894}{445645}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.75pt}{$\textstyle(3)$}}}}}{\raisebox{-2.26665pt}{\resizebox{759247}{445645}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.22499pt}{$\scriptstyle(3)$}}}}}{\raisebox{-2.26666pt}{\resizebox{759248}{445645}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.875pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle(3)$}}}}}}\hskip 0.50003ptw_{0}-2\rho_{1}(1\hskip-1.00006pt+\hskip-1.00006ptZ)\sum_{i=1,2}\Big(\partial_{y}\hskip-1.00006pt\hskip-1.00006pt+\hskip-1.00006pt\hskip-1.00006pt\frac{Z}{y}\Big)(w_{0}^{i})^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-2.26666pt}{\resizebox{868894}{445645}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.75pt}{$\displaystyle(1)$}}}}}{\raisebox{-2.26666pt}{\resizebox{868894}{445645}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.75pt}{$\textstyle(1)$}}}}}{\raisebox{-2.26665pt}{\resizebox{759247}{445645}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.22499pt}{$\scriptstyle(1)$}}}}}{\raisebox{-2.26666pt}{\resizebox{759248}{445645}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.875pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle(1)$}}}}}}\hskip 1.00006ptw_{0}\bigg\}.

Furthermore, for any vector

\displaystyle\sum_{2\leq i+j\leq 4}a^{i}b^{j}E_{i,j}

\displaystyle=\sum_{2\leq i+j\leq 4}a^{i}b^{j}\bigg\{{\rm\hat{J}}\hskip 1.00006pt\big(-\hskip-0.50003pt{\rm R}E_{i,j}\big)+\big(w_{0}\cdot E_{i,j}\big)e_{z}-b^{2}S_{0,2}^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-2.26666pt}{\resizebox{868894}{445645}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.75pt}{$\displaystyle(3)$}}}}}{\raisebox{-2.26666pt}{\resizebox{868894}{445645}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.75pt}{$\textstyle(3)$}}}}}{\raisebox{-2.26665pt}{\resizebox{759247}{445645}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.22499pt}{$\scriptstyle(3)$}}}}}{\raisebox{-2.26666pt}{\resizebox{759248}{445645}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.875pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle(3)$}}}}}}E_{i,j}\bigg\},

(3.42)

where we note that

. Now we use (

into the big brace in (

\displaystyle\varPsi_{0}

\displaystyle={\rm\hat{J}}\hskip 1.00006pt\Bigg\{(\rho_{1}-\rho_{2}{\rm R})\hskip-1.00006pt\hskip-1.00006pt\begin{bmatrix}\,{\it\mathbb{H}}^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-2.26666pt}{\resizebox{868894}{445645}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.75pt}{$\displaystyle(1)$}}}}}{\raisebox{-2.26666pt}{\resizebox{868894}{445645}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.75pt}{$\textstyle(1)$}}}}}{\raisebox{-2.26665pt}{\resizebox{759247}{445645}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.22499pt}{$\scriptstyle(1)$}}}}}{\raisebox{-2.26666pt}{\resizebox{759248}{445645}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.875pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle(1)$}}}}}}(w_{0}^{2})\,\\
{\it\mathbb{H}}^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-2.26666pt}{\resizebox{868894}{445645}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.75pt}{$\displaystyle(2)$}}}}}{\raisebox{-2.26666pt}{\resizebox{868894}{445645}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.75pt}{$\textstyle(2)$}}}}}{\raisebox{-2.26665pt}{\resizebox{759247}{445645}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.22499pt}{$\scriptstyle(2)$}}}}}{\raisebox{-2.26666pt}{\resizebox{759248}{445645}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.875pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle(2)$}}}}}}(w_{0}^{2})\\
0\end{bmatrix}\ +\sum_{2\leq i+j\leq 7}\hskip-3.99994pta^{i}b^{j}\tilde{E}_{i,j}\Bigg\}

(3.43)

\displaystyle+ab\varSigma_{1,0}+b^{2}\varSigma_{0,1}-b^{3}\varSigma_{0,2}-2a^{2}b\hskip 1.00006ptS_{0,2}-(a^{2}+b^{2})\hskip-5.0pt\sum_{2\leq i+j\leq 3}\hskip-5.0pta^{i}b^{j}\partial_{b}\varPhi_{i,j}

\displaystyle+\sum_{2\leq i+j\leq 4}a^{i}b^{j}\Big[\big(w_{0}\cdot E_{i,j}\big)e_{z}-b^{2}S_{0,2}^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-2.26666pt}{\resizebox{868894}{445645}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.75pt}{$\displaystyle(3)$}}}}}{\raisebox{-2.26666pt}{\resizebox{868894}{445645}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.75pt}{$\textstyle(3)$}}}}}{\raisebox{-2.26665pt}{\resizebox{759247}{445645}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.22499pt}{$\scriptstyle(3)$}}}}}{\raisebox{-2.26666pt}{\resizebox{759248}{445645}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.875pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle(3)$}}}}}}E_{i,j}\Big]+b^{3}\big(\varLambda S_{0,2}-2S_{0,2}+\varSigma_{0,2}\big),

where

Step 7
.
Construction of
\varPhi_{i,j}
. We choose suitable
\varPhi_{i,j}
by eliminating the error (
3.44
). More precisely, we let

(\rho_{1}-\rho_{2}{\rm R})\hskip-1.00006pt\hskip-1.00006pt\begin{bmatrix}\,{\it\mathbb{H}}^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-2.26666pt}{\resizebox{868894}{445645}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.75pt}{$\displaystyle(1)$}}}}}{\raisebox{-2.26666pt}{\resizebox{868894}{445645}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.75pt}{$\textstyle(1)$}}}}}{\raisebox{-2.26665pt}{\resizebox{759247}{445645}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.22499pt}{$\scriptstyle(1)$}}}}}{\raisebox{-2.26666pt}{\resizebox{759248}{445645}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.875pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle(1)$}}}}}}(w_{0}^{2})\,\\
{\it\mathbb{H}}^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-2.26666pt}{\resizebox{868894}{445645}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.75pt}{$\displaystyle(2)$}}}}}{\raisebox{-2.26666pt}{\resizebox{868894}{445645}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.75pt}{$\textstyle(2)$}}}}}{\raisebox{-2.26665pt}{\resizebox{759247}{445645}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.22499pt}{$\scriptstyle(2)$}}}}}{\raisebox{-2.26666pt}{\resizebox{759248}{445645}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.875pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle(2)$}}}}}}(w_{0}^{2})\\
0\end{bmatrix}+\sum_{2\leq i+j\leq 3}\hskip-3.99994pta^{i}b^{j}\tilde{E}_{i,j}=0,

which, in view of (
3.19
), yields the following linear system

\begin{bmatrix}\rho_{1}&\rho_{2}\\
-\rho_{2}&\rho_{1}\end{bmatrix}\left[\begin{aligned} \,H\varPhi_{i,j}^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-2.26666pt}{\resizebox{868894}{445645}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.75pt}{$\displaystyle(1)$}}}}}{\raisebox{-2.26666pt}{\resizebox{868894}{445645}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.75pt}{$\textstyle(1)$}}}}}{\raisebox{-2.26665pt}{\resizebox{759247}{445645}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.22499pt}{$\scriptstyle(1)$}}}}}{\raisebox{-2.26666pt}{\resizebox{759248}{445645}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.875pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle(1)$}}}}}}\,\\
\,H\varPhi_{i,j}^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-2.26666pt}{\resizebox{868894}{445645}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.75pt}{$\displaystyle(2)$}}}}}{\raisebox{-2.26666pt}{\resizebox{868894}{445645}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.75pt}{$\textstyle(2)$}}}}}{\raisebox{-2.26665pt}{\resizebox{759247}{445645}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.22499pt}{$\scriptstyle(2)$}}}}}{\raisebox{-2.26666pt}{\resizebox{759248}{445645}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.875pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle(2)$}}}}}}\,\end{aligned}\right]=-\left[\begin{aligned} \,\tilde{E}_{i,j}^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-2.26666pt}{\resizebox{868894}{445645}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.75pt}{$\displaystyle(1)$}}}}}{\raisebox{-2.26666pt}{\resizebox{868894}{445645}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.75pt}{$\textstyle(1)$}}}}}{\raisebox{-2.26665pt}{\resizebox{759247}{445645}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.22499pt}{$\scriptstyle(1)$}}}}}{\raisebox{-2.26666pt}{\resizebox{759248}{445645}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.875pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle(1)$}}}}}}\,\\
\,\tilde{E}_{i,j}^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-2.26666pt}{\resizebox{868894}{445645}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.75pt}{$\displaystyle(2)$}}}}}{\raisebox{-2.26666pt}{\resizebox{868894}{445645}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.75pt}{$\textstyle(2)$}}}}}{\raisebox{-2.26665pt}{\resizebox{759247}{445645}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.22499pt}{$\scriptstyle(2)$}}}}}{\raisebox{-2.26666pt}{\resizebox{759248}{445645}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.875pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle(2)$}}}}}}\,\end{aligned}\right],\quad\mbox{for}\quad 2\leq i+j\leq 3.

(3.45)

It can also be solved using (
2.33
), and then the constructions of
\varPhi_{i,j}
are obtained. We consider first the case
i+j=2
. From (
3.37
), (
3.44
), we see

\displaystyle-\sum_{i+j=2}\hskip-1.99997pta^{i}b^{j}\tilde{E}_{i,j}

\displaystyle=a^{2}\bigg\{\hskip-1.00006pt(1\hskip-1.00006pt+\hskip-1.00006ptZ)\varPhi_{1,0}+2\rho_{1}(1+Z)\Big(\partial_{y}\hskip-1.00006pt\hskip-1.00006pt+\hskip-1.00006pt\hskip-1.00006pt\frac{Z}{y}\Big)\varPhi_{1,0}^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-2.26666pt}{\resizebox{868894}{445645}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.75pt}{$\displaystyle(1)$}}}}}{\raisebox{-2.26666pt}{\resizebox{868894}{445645}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.75pt}{$\textstyle(1)$}}}}}{\raisebox{-2.26665pt}{\resizebox{759247}{445645}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.22499pt}{$\scriptstyle(1)$}}}}}{\raisebox{-2.26666pt}{\resizebox{759248}{445645}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.875pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle(1)$}}}}}}\big(\rho_{1}\hskip-1.00006pt-\hskip-1.00006pt\rho_{2}{\rm R}\big)\varPhi_{1,0}^{\phantom{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-2.26666pt}{\resizebox{868894}{445645}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.75pt}{$\displaystyle(1)$}}}}}{\raisebox{-2.26666pt}{\resizebox{868894}{445645}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.75pt}{$\textstyle(1)$}}}}}{\raisebox{-2.26665pt}{\resizebox{759247}{445645}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.22499pt}{$\scriptstyle(1)$}}}}}{\raisebox{-2.26666pt}{\resizebox{759248}{445645}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.875pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle(1)$}}}}}}}\bigg\}

(3.46)

\displaystyle+ab\bigg\{(1\hskip-1.00006pt+\hskip-1.00006ptZ)\varPhi_{0,1}+\big(\varLambda\varPhi_{0,1}-\varPhi_{0,1}-\varSigma_{0,1}\big)

\displaystyle+2\rho_{1}(1\hskip-1.00006pt+\hskip-1.00006ptZ)\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}i+k=1\\
j+l=1\end{subarray}}\Big(\partial_{y}\hskip-1.00006pt\hskip-1.00006pt+\hskip-1.00006pt\hskip-1.00006pt\frac{Z}{y}\Big)\varPhi_{i,j}^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-2.26666pt}{\resizebox{868894}{445645}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.75pt}{$\displaystyle(1)$}}}}}{\raisebox{-2.26666pt}{\resizebox{868894}{445645}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.75pt}{$\textstyle(1)$}}}}}{\raisebox{-2.26665pt}{\resizebox{759247}{445645}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.22499pt}{$\scriptstyle(1)$}}}}}{\raisebox{-2.26666pt}{\resizebox{759248}{445645}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.875pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle(1)$}}}}}}\big(\rho_{1}\hskip-1.00006pt-\hskip-1.00006pt\rho_{2}{\rm R}\big)\varPhi_{k,l}^{\phantom{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-2.26666pt}{\resizebox{868894}{445645}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.75pt}{$\displaystyle(1)$}}}}}{\raisebox{-2.26666pt}{\resizebox{868894}{445645}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.75pt}{$\textstyle(1)$}}}}}{\raisebox{-2.26665pt}{\resizebox{759247}{445645}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.22499pt}{$\scriptstyle(1)$}}}}}{\raisebox{-2.26666pt}{\resizebox{759248}{445645}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.875pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle(1)$}}}}}}}\bigg\}

\displaystyle+b^{2}\bigg\{2(1\hskip-1.00006pt+\hskip-1.00006ptZ)\partial_{y}S_{0,2}^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-2.26666pt}{\resizebox{868894}{445645}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.75pt}{$\displaystyle(3)$}}}}}{\raisebox{-2.26666pt}{\resizebox{868894}{445645}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.75pt}{$\textstyle(3)$}}}}}{\raisebox{-2.26665pt}{\resizebox{759247}{445645}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.22499pt}{$\scriptstyle(3)$}}}}}{\raisebox{-2.26666pt}{\resizebox{759248}{445645}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.875pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle(3)$}}}}}}\big(\rho_{1}e_{x}\hskip-1.00006pt-\hskip-1.00006pt\rho_{2}e_{y}\big)-\big(\varLambda\varPhi_{1,0}-\varPhi_{1,0}-\varSigma_{1,0}\big)

\displaystyle+2\rho_{1}(1\hskip-1.00006pt+\hskip-1.00006ptZ)\Big(\partial_{y}\hskip-1.00006pt\hskip-1.00006pt+\hskip-1.00006pt\hskip-1.00006pt\frac{Z}{y}\Big)\varPhi_{0,1}^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-2.26666pt}{\resizebox{868894}{445645}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.75pt}{$\displaystyle(1)$}}}}}{\raisebox{-2.26666pt}{\resizebox{868894}{445645}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.75pt}{$\textstyle(1)$}}}}}{\raisebox{-2.26665pt}{\resizebox{759247}{445645}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.22499pt}{$\scriptstyle(1)$}}}}}{\raisebox{-2.26666pt}{\resizebox{759248}{445645}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.875pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle(1)$}}}}}}\big(\rho_{1}\hskip-1.00006pt-\hskip-1.00006pt\rho_{2}{\rm R}\big)\varPhi_{0,1}^{\phantom{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-2.26666pt}{\resizebox{868894}{445645}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.75pt}{$\displaystyle(1)$}}}}}{\raisebox{-2.26666pt}{\resizebox{868894}{445645}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.75pt}{$\textstyle(1)$}}}}}{\raisebox{-2.26665pt}{\resizebox{759247}{445645}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.22499pt}{$\scriptstyle(1)$}}}}}{\raisebox{-2.26666pt}{\resizebox{759248}{445645}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.875pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle(1)$}}}}}}}\bigg\}.

This together with the asymptotics for
\varPhi_{1,0},\varPhi_{0,1},S_{0,2}
and (
3.35
) implies the bound

\varPhi_{i,j}=O\bigg(y^{5}\mathbf{1}_{y\leq 1}+\frac{1+y}{b\left|\log b\right|}\mathbf{1}_{y\geq 1}\bigg)\lesssim\frac{1+y}{b\left|\log b\right|},

(3.47)

and also the crude bound

|\varPhi_{i,j}|\lesssim 1+y^{3}.

Moreover, from (
3.32
), we have the estimate for the derivatives on
b
:

\partial_{b}\varPhi_{i,j}=O\bigg(\frac{y^{5}}{b\left|\log b\right|^{2}}\,\mathbf{1}_{y\leq 1}+\frac{y^{3}}{b\left|\log b\right|}\,\mathbf{1}_{1\leq y\leq 6B_{0}}\bigg).

Next for
i+j=3
, from (
3.37
), (
3.44
) again, we have

\displaystyle-\sum_{i+j=3}\hskip-1.99997pta^{i}b^{j}\tilde{E}_{i,j}

\displaystyle=\sum_{i+j=2}\hskip-3.00003pta^{i}b^{j}\Big(b\varLambda{\rm R}\varPhi_{i,j}+aZ\varPhi_{i,j}\Big)+(a^{2}+b^{2})\hskip-3.00003pt\sum_{i+j=2}\hskip-3.00003ptja^{i}b^{j-1}{\rm R}\varPhi_{i,j}

\displaystyle-2(1\hskip-1.00006pt+\hskip-1.00006ptZ)\hskip-1.00006pt\hskip-1.00006pt\sum_{i+j=3}\hskip-3.00003pt\Big(\partial_{y}\hskip-1.00006pt\hskip-1.00006pt+\hskip-1.00006pt\hskip-1.00006pt\frac{Z}{y}\Big)(w_{0}^{i})^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-2.26666pt}{\resizebox{868894}{445645}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.75pt}{$\displaystyle(1)$}}}}}{\raisebox{-2.26666pt}{\resizebox{868894}{445645}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.75pt}{$\textstyle(1)$}}}}}{\raisebox{-2.26665pt}{\resizebox{759247}{445645}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.22499pt}{$\scriptstyle(1)$}}}}}{\raisebox{-2.26666pt}{\resizebox{759248}{445645}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.875pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle(1)$}}}}}}\big(\rho_{1}\hskip-1.00006pt-\hskip-1.00006pt\rho_{2}{\rm R}\big)w_{0}^{j}

\displaystyle-\rho_{2}b^{2}S_{0,2}^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-2.26666pt}{\resizebox{868894}{445645}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.75pt}{$\displaystyle(3)$}}}}}{\raisebox{-2.26666pt}{\resizebox{868894}{445645}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.75pt}{$\textstyle(3)$}}}}}{\raisebox{-2.26665pt}{\resizebox{759247}{445645}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.22499pt}{$\scriptstyle(3)$}}}}}{\raisebox{-2.26666pt}{\resizebox{759248}{445645}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.875pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle(3)$}}}}}}\hskip 1.00006pt{\rm R}{\it\mathbb{H}}w_{0}^{1}+b^{2}\Delta S_{0,2}^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-2.26666pt}{\resizebox{868894}{445645}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.75pt}{$\displaystyle(3)$}}}}}{\raisebox{-2.26666pt}{\resizebox{868894}{445645}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.75pt}{$\textstyle(3)$}}}}}{\raisebox{-2.26665pt}{\resizebox{759247}{445645}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.22499pt}{$\scriptstyle(3)$}}}}}{\raisebox{-2.26666pt}{\resizebox{759248}{445645}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.875pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle(3)$}}}}}}\big(\rho_{1}\hskip-1.00006pt-\hskip-1.00006pt\rho_{2}{\rm R}\big)w_{0}^{1}.

This together with (
3.22
), (
3.25
), (
3.47
) gives for
i+j=3
that

\varPhi_{i,j}=O\bigg(y^{7}\mathbf{1}_{y\leq 1}+\frac{1+y^{3}}{b\left|\log b\right|}\mathbf{1}_{y\geq 1}\bigg)\lesssim\frac{1+y^{3}}{b\left|\log b\right|},

(3.48)

the crude bound

|\varPhi_{i,j}|\lesssim 1+y^{5},

and the derivatives

\partial_{b}\varPhi_{i,j}=O\bigg(\frac{y^{7}}{b\left|\log b\right|^{2}}\,\mathbf{1}_{y\leq 1}+\frac{y^{5}}{b\left|\log b\right|}\,\mathbf{1}_{1\leq y\leq 6B_{0}}\bigg).

Step 8
.
Estimates on the error
. Due to the choice of
\varPhi_{i,j}
, the error (
3.43
) boils down to

\displaystyle\varPsi_{0}

\displaystyle=ab\varSigma_{1,0}+b^{2}\varSigma_{0,1}-b^{3}\varSigma_{0,2}-2a^{2}b\hskip 1.00006ptS_{0,2}-(a^{2}+b^{2})\hskip-5.0pt\sum_{2\leq i+j\leq 3}\hskip-5.0pta^{i}b^{j}\partial_{b}\varPhi_{i,j}

(3.49)

\displaystyle+\sum_{2\leq i+j\leq 4}a^{i}b^{j}\big(w_{0}\cdot E_{i,j}\big)e_{z}+b^{3}\big(\varLambda S_{0,2}-2S_{0,2}+\varSigma_{0,2}\big)

\displaystyle-b^{2}\hskip 0.50003ptS_{0,2}^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-2.26666pt}{\resizebox{868894}{445645}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.75pt}{$\displaystyle(3)$}}}}}{\raisebox{-2.26666pt}{\resizebox{868894}{445645}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.75pt}{$\textstyle(3)$}}}}}{\raisebox{-2.26665pt}{\resizebox{759247}{445645}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.22499pt}{$\scriptstyle(3)$}}}}}{\raisebox{-2.26666pt}{\resizebox{759248}{445645}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.875pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle(3)$}}}}}}\hskip-1.00006pt\sum_{2\leq i+j\leq 4}a^{i}b^{j}E_{i,j}+{\rm\hat{J}}\hskip 1.00006pt\bigg(\sum_{4\leq i+j\leq 7}\hskip-1.00006pt\hskip-1.00006pta^{i}b^{j}\tilde{E}_{i,j}\bigg).

For each line of the RHS of (
3.49
), we estimate them in the region
[0,2B_{1}]
by previous asymptotics. From (
3.31
), (
3.26
), there holds

\displaystyle ab\varSigma_{1,0}+b^{2}\varSigma_{0,1}-b^{3}\varSigma_{0,2}-2a^{2}b\hskip 1.00006ptS_{0,2}

(3.50)

\displaystyle=O\big(b^{2}\varSigma_{b}\big)(e_{x}+e_{y})+O\big(b^{3}T_{1}\varSigma_{b}\big)e_{z}+O\big(ab^{2}S_{0,2}\big)

\displaystyle=b^{2}\,O\bigg(\frac{y^{3}}{\left|\log b\right|}\mathbf{1}_{y\leq 1}+\frac{y\log y}{\left|\log b\right|}\mathbf{1}_{1\leq y\leq\frac{B_{0}}{4}}+y\mathbf{1}_{y\geq\frac{B_{0}}{4}}\bigg)\big(e_{x}+e_{y}\big)

\displaystyle+b^{2}\,O\bigg(\frac{y^{6}}{\left|\log b\right|}\mathbf{1}_{y\leq 1}+\frac{y^{2}(\log y)^{2}}{\left|\log b\right|}\mathbf{1}_{1\leq y\leq\frac{B_{0}}{4}}+y^{2}\log y\mathbf{1}_{y\geq\frac{B_{0}}{4}}\bigg)e_{z}

\displaystyle+a^{2}b\,O\Big(y^{2}(\log y)^{2}\mathbf{1}_{y\geq 1}\Big).

By the
b
derivatives estimates, we have

(a^{2}+b^{2})\hskip-3.00003pt\sum_{2\leq i+j\leq 3}a^{i}b^{j}\partial_{b}\varPhi_{i,j}=O\bigg(\frac{b^{3}y^{5}}{\left|\log b\right|^{2}}\mathbf{1}_{y\leq 1}+\frac{b^{3}y^{3}}{\left|\log b\right|}\mathbf{1}_{1\leq y\leq 6B_{0}}\bigg).

For the second line, (
3.25
) induces the cancellation:

\Big(b\big(\varLambda\varPhi_{0,1}\hskip-1.00006pt-\hskip-1.00006pt\varPhi_{0,1}\hskip-1.00006pt-\hskip-1.00006pt\varSigma_{0,1}\big)\cdot b\,\varPhi_{0,1}\Big)\hskip 0.50003pte_{z}+b^{3}\big(\varLambda S_{0,2}-2S_{0,2}+\varSigma_{0,2}\big)=0,

which together with explicit formula (
3.37
) gives

\displaystyle\sum_{2\leq i+j\leq 4}a^{i}b^{j}\big(w_{0}\cdot E_{i,j}\big)e_{z}+b^{3}\big(\varLambda S_{0,2}-2S_{0,2}+\varSigma_{0,2}\big)

\displaystyle=ab^{2}\,O\bigg(y^{6}\mathbf{1}_{y\leq 1}+\frac{y^{2}\log y}{\left|\log b\right|}\Big(\hskip-1.00006pt1\hskip-1.00006pt+\hskip-1.00006pt\left|\log(\right.\hskip-1.00006pt\hskip-1.00006pty\sqrt{b}\left.\hskip-1.00006pt\hskip-1.00006pt)\right|\hskip-1.00006pt\Big)\mathbf{1}_{1\leq y\leq 6B_{0}}+\frac{y^{2}\log y}{\left|\log b\right|}\mathbf{1}_{y\geq 6B_{0}}\bigg)\,e_{z}.

For the third line, from (
3.37
), (
3.44
), and the crude bounds of
\varPhi_{i,j}
, we have

\Big|b^{2}\hskip 0.50003ptS_{0,2}^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-2.26666pt}{\resizebox{868894}{445645}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.75pt}{$\displaystyle(3)$}}}}}{\raisebox{-2.26666pt}{\resizebox{868894}{445645}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.75pt}{$\textstyle(3)$}}}}}{\raisebox{-2.26665pt}{\resizebox{759247}{445645}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.22499pt}{$\scriptstyle(3)$}}}}}{\raisebox{-2.26666pt}{\resizebox{759248}{445645}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.875pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle(3)$}}}}}}\hskip-1.00006pt\sum_{2\leq i+j\leq 4}a^{i}b^{j}E_{i,j}\Big|+\Big|\sum_{4\leq i+j\leq 7}a^{i}b^{j}\tilde{E}_{i,j}\Big|\lesssim b^{4}\Big(y^{9}\mathbf{1}_{y\leq 1}+y^{5}(\log y)^{2}\mathbf{1}_{y\geq 1}\Big).

Collecting these estimates, we see (
3.50
) is the dominate part of
\varPsi_{0}
, which yields the bound

\int_{y\leq 2B_{1}}\frac{|\varPsi_{0}^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-2.26666pt}{\resizebox{868894}{445645}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.75pt}{$\displaystyle(1)$}}}}}{\raisebox{-2.26666pt}{\resizebox{868894}{445645}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.75pt}{$\textstyle(1)$}}}}}{\raisebox{-2.26665pt}{\resizebox{759247}{445645}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.22499pt}{$\scriptstyle(1)$}}}}}{\raisebox{-2.26666pt}{\resizebox{759248}{445645}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.875pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle(1)$}}}}}}|^{2}+|\varPsi_{0}^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-2.26666pt}{\resizebox{868894}{445645}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.75pt}{$\displaystyle(2)$}}}}}{\raisebox{-2.26666pt}{\resizebox{868894}{445645}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.75pt}{$\textstyle(2)$}}}}}{\raisebox{-2.26665pt}{\resizebox{759247}{445645}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.22499pt}{$\scriptstyle(2)$}}}}}{\raisebox{-2.26666pt}{\resizebox{759248}{445645}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.875pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle(2)$}}}}}}|^{2}}{y^{6}}\lesssim\frac{b^{4}}{\left|\log b\right|^{2}},\quad\int_{y\leq 2B_{1}}\frac{|\varPsi_{0}^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-2.26666pt}{\resizebox{868894}{445645}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.75pt}{$\displaystyle(3)$}}}}}{\raisebox{-2.26666pt}{\resizebox{868894}{445645}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.75pt}{$\textstyle(3)$}}}}}{\raisebox{-2.26665pt}{\resizebox{759247}{445645}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.22499pt}{$\scriptstyle(3)$}}}}}{\raisebox{-2.26666pt}{\resizebox{759248}{445645}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.875pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle(3)$}}}}}}|^{2}}{y^{8}}\lesssim\frac{b^{6}}{\left|\log b\right|^{2}}.

(3.51)

This yields (
3.9
), (
3.10
) with
i=0
. The cases
i\geq 1
are similar. After the action of
H
, we have

\displaystyle abH\varSigma_{1,0}+b^{2}H\varSigma_{0,1}=O\big(b^{2}\varSigma_{b}\big)(e_{x}+e_{y})

\displaystyle=b^{2}\,O\bigg(\frac{y}{\left|\log b\right|}\mathbf{1}_{y\leq 1}+\frac{1}{y\left|\log b\right|}\mathbf{1}_{1\leq y\leq 6B_{0}}\bigg)\big(e_{x}+e_{y}\big),

which still leads to the same bounds as (
3.51
). So do most of the other terms, except the
b^{4}{\rm R}\varLambda\varPhi_{0,3}
coming from the last term of (
3.44
), which, from (
3.48
), can be bounded by

\int_{y\leq 2B_{1}}\hskip-1.00006pt\hskip-1.00006pt\hskip-1.00006pt|H\big(b^{4}{\rm R}\varLambda\varPhi_{0,3}\big)|^{2}\lesssim b^{8}\bigg(\int_{y\leq 1}\hskip-1.00006pt\hskip-1.00006pt|H(y^{7})|^{2}+\int_{1\leq y\leq 2B_{1}}\hskip-1.00006pt\frac{|H(y^{3})|^{2}}{b^{2}\left|\log b\right|^{2}}\bigg)\lesssim b^{4}\left|\log b\right|^{2},

and thus (
3.11
) follows. The estimates (
3.12
)–(
3.14
) are obtained similarly. The details are left to readers. Finally, for the flux computations, we observe from (
3.29
) that

\displaystyle(\mathbb{H^{\perp}}\varPsi_{0},\varPhi_{M})

\displaystyle=\big(ab\mathbb{H^{\perp}}\varSigma_{1,0}+b^{2}\mathbb{H^{\perp}}\varSigma_{0,1},\varPhi_{M}\big)+C(M)\frac{b^{6}}{\left|\log b\right|^{2}}

\displaystyle=\frac{ab\hskip 0.50003ptc_{b}\big(\varLambda\phi,\varPhi_{M}\big)}{\rho_{1}^{2}+\rho_{2}^{2}}\begin{bmatrix}\rho_{1}\\
\rho_{2}\\
0\end{bmatrix}+\frac{b^{2}\hskip 0.50003ptc_{b}\big(\varLambda\phi,\varPhi_{M}\big)}{\rho_{1}^{2}+\rho_{2}^{2}}\begin{bmatrix}-\rho_{2}\\
\rho_{1}\\
0\end{bmatrix}+C(M)\frac{b^{6}}{\left|\log b\right|^{2}},

which together with (
3.30
) yields the desired (
3.15
). This concludes the proof.

\square

Remark 3.1

The construction of
S_{0,2}
(
3.25
) may seem to be unnatural, but it actually corresponds to the constraint (
2.8
), ensuring that LL flow maps from
\mathbb{R}^{2}
to
\mathbb{S}^{2}
. On the contrary, if we simply let
w_{0}^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-2.26666pt}{\resizebox{868894}{445645}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.75pt}{$\displaystyle(3)$}}}}}{\raisebox{-2.26666pt}{\resizebox{868894}{445645}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.75pt}{$\textstyle(3)$}}}}}{\raisebox{-2.26665pt}{\resizebox{759247}{445645}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.22499pt}{$\scriptstyle(3)$}}}}}{\raisebox{-2.26666pt}{\resizebox{759248}{445645}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.875pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle(3)$}}}}}}=S_{0,2}^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-2.26666pt}{\resizebox{868894}{445645}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.75pt}{$\displaystyle(3)$}}}}}{\raisebox{-2.26666pt}{\resizebox{868894}{445645}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.75pt}{$\textstyle(3)$}}}}}{\raisebox{-2.26665pt}{\resizebox{759247}{445645}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.22499pt}{$\scriptstyle(3)$}}}}}{\raisebox{-2.26666pt}{\resizebox{759248}{445645}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.875pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle(3)$}}}}}}=0
, the error arising therefrom will make
w^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-2.26666pt}{\resizebox{868894}{445645}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.75pt}{$\displaystyle(3)$}}}}}{\raisebox{-2.26666pt}{\resizebox{868894}{445645}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.75pt}{$\textstyle(3)$}}}}}{\raisebox{-2.26665pt}{\resizebox{759247}{445645}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.22499pt}{$\scriptstyle(3)$}}}}}{\raisebox{-2.26666pt}{\resizebox{759248}{445645}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.875pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle(3)$}}}}}}=\gamma

(
see (
4.2
), (
4.3
)
)
out of control, and destroy the corresponding bounds in Lemma
A.4
.

3.1
Localization of the profiles

The aim of this subsection is to localize the approximate solution constructed in Lemma
3.1
within the spacial scale
y\leq 2B_{1}
.

Lemma 3.2
(Localization)

Under the assumption of Lemma
3.1
, we define the localized profile

\tilde{w}_{0}=\chi_{B_{1}}w_{0},

(3.52)

and
\tilde{\varPhi}_{i,j}=\chi_{B_{1}}\varPhi_{i,j}
accordingly. Then (
3.52
) is an approximate solution to (
2.28
) in the sense that

\displaystyle\tilde{\varPsi}_{0}+\widetilde{{\rm Mod}}(t)=\partial_{s}\tilde{w}_{0}-\frac{\lambda_{s}}{\lambda}\varLambda\tilde{w}_{0}+\varTheta_{s}Z{\rm R}\tilde{w}_{0}+{\rm\tilde{J}}\Big(\,\rho_{1}{\it\mathbb{H}}\tilde{w}_{0}-\rho_{2}{\rm\tilde{J}}{\it\mathbb{H}}\tilde{w}_{0}+\hat{p}\,\varLambda\phi\,\Big),

(3.53)

where
{\rm\tilde{J}}:=\big(e_{z}+\tilde{w}_{0}\big)\wedge
. The localized modulation vector
\widetilde{{\rm Mod}}
is given by

\displaystyle\widetilde{{\rm Mod}}

\displaystyle=\chi_{B_{1}}{\rm Mod}+\big(b_{s}+b^{2}+a^{2}\big)\,O\Big(\frac{w_{0}}{b}\Big)\mathbf{1}_{y\sim B_{1}}-\Big(\frac{\lambda_{s}}{\lambda}+b\Big)O(w_{0})\mathbf{1}_{y\sim B_{1}}

(3.54)

\displaystyle+(\varTheta_{s}+a)\varLambda\phi\hskip 0.50003pt\big(e_{y}+O(\tilde{w}_{0})\big)\mathbf{1}_{y\gtrsim B_{1}}-\Big(\frac{\lambda_{s}}{\lambda}+b\Big)\varLambda\phi\hskip 0.50003pt\big(e_{x}+O(\tilde{w}_{0})\big)\mathbf{1}_{y\gtrsim B_{1}},

while the localized error
\tilde{\varPsi}_{0}
satisfies the following estimates:

\displaystyle\int_{y\leq 2B_{1}}\frac{|\partial_{y}^{i}\tilde{\varPsi}_{0}^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-2.26666pt}{\resizebox{868894}{445645}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.75pt}{$\displaystyle(1)$}}}}}{\raisebox{-2.26666pt}{\resizebox{868894}{445645}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.75pt}{$\textstyle(1)$}}}}}{\raisebox{-2.26665pt}{\resizebox{759247}{445645}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.22499pt}{$\scriptstyle(1)$}}}}}{\raisebox{-2.26666pt}{\resizebox{759248}{445645}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.875pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle(1)$}}}}}}|^{2}+|\partial_{y}^{i}\tilde{\varPsi}_{0}^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-2.26666pt}{\resizebox{868894}{445645}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.75pt}{$\displaystyle(2)$}}}}}{\raisebox{-2.26666pt}{\resizebox{868894}{445645}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.75pt}{$\textstyle(2)$}}}}}{\raisebox{-2.26665pt}{\resizebox{759247}{445645}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.22499pt}{$\scriptstyle(2)$}}}}}{\raisebox{-2.26666pt}{\resizebox{759248}{445645}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.875pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle(2)$}}}}}}|^{2}}{y^{6-2i}}\lesssim\frac{b^{4}}{\left|\log b\right|^{2}},\quad 0\leq i\leq 3,

(3.55)

\displaystyle\int_{y\leq 2B_{1}}\frac{|\partial_{y}^{i}\tilde{\varPsi}_{0}^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-2.26666pt}{\resizebox{868894}{445645}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.75pt}{$\displaystyle(3)$}}}}}{\raisebox{-2.26666pt}{\resizebox{868894}{445645}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.75pt}{$\textstyle(3)$}}}}}{\raisebox{-2.26665pt}{\resizebox{759247}{445645}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.22499pt}{$\scriptstyle(3)$}}}}}{\raisebox{-2.26666pt}{\resizebox{759248}{445645}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.875pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle(3)$}}}}}}|^{2}}{y^{8-2i}}\lesssim\frac{b^{6}}{\left|\log b\right|^{2}},\quad 0\leq i\leq 4,

(3.56)

\displaystyle\int_{y\leq 2B_{1}}|H\tilde{\varPsi}_{0}^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-2.26666pt}{\resizebox{868894}{445645}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.75pt}{$\displaystyle(1)$}}}}}{\raisebox{-2.26666pt}{\resizebox{868894}{445645}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.75pt}{$\textstyle(1)$}}}}}{\raisebox{-2.26665pt}{\resizebox{759247}{445645}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.22499pt}{$\scriptstyle(1)$}}}}}{\raisebox{-2.26666pt}{\resizebox{759248}{445645}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.875pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle(1)$}}}}}}|^{2}+|H\tilde{\varPsi}_{0}^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-2.26666pt}{\resizebox{868894}{445645}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.75pt}{$\displaystyle(2)$}}}}}{\raisebox{-2.26666pt}{\resizebox{868894}{445645}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.75pt}{$\textstyle(2)$}}}}}{\raisebox{-2.26665pt}{\resizebox{759247}{445645}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.22499pt}{$\scriptstyle(2)$}}}}}{\raisebox{-2.26666pt}{\resizebox{759248}{445645}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.875pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle(2)$}}}}}}|^{2}\lesssim b^{4}\left|\log b\right|^{2},

(3.57)

\displaystyle\int_{y\leq 2B_{1}}\frac{|\partial_{y}^{i}H\tilde{\varPsi}_{0}^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-2.26666pt}{\resizebox{868894}{445645}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.75pt}{$\displaystyle(1)$}}}}}{\raisebox{-2.26666pt}{\resizebox{868894}{445645}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.75pt}{$\textstyle(1)$}}}}}{\raisebox{-2.26665pt}{\resizebox{759247}{445645}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.22499pt}{$\scriptstyle(1)$}}}}}{\raisebox{-2.26666pt}{\resizebox{759248}{445645}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.875pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle(1)$}}}}}}|^{2}+|\partial_{y}^{i}H\tilde{\varPsi}_{0}^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-2.26666pt}{\resizebox{868894}{445645}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.75pt}{$\displaystyle(2)$}}}}}{\raisebox{-2.26666pt}{\resizebox{868894}{445645}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.75pt}{$\textstyle(2)$}}}}}{\raisebox{-2.26665pt}{\resizebox{759247}{445645}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.22499pt}{$\scriptstyle(2)$}}}}}{\raisebox{-2.26666pt}{\resizebox{759248}{445645}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.875pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle(2)$}}}}}}|^{2}}{y^{2-2i}}\lesssim\frac{b^{4}}{\left|\log b\right|^{2}},\quad 0\leq i\leq 1,

(3.58)

\displaystyle\int_{y\leq 2B_{1}}|AH\tilde{\varPsi}_{0}^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-2.26666pt}{\resizebox{868894}{445645}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.75pt}{$\displaystyle(1)$}}}}}{\raisebox{-2.26666pt}{\resizebox{868894}{445645}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.75pt}{$\textstyle(1)$}}}}}{\raisebox{-2.26665pt}{\resizebox{759247}{445645}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.22499pt}{$\scriptstyle(1)$}}}}}{\raisebox{-2.26666pt}{\resizebox{759248}{445645}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.875pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle(1)$}}}}}}|^{2}+|AH\tilde{\varPsi}_{0}^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-2.26666pt}{\resizebox{868894}{445645}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.75pt}{$\displaystyle(2)$}}}}}{\raisebox{-2.26666pt}{\resizebox{868894}{445645}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.75pt}{$\textstyle(2)$}}}}}{\raisebox{-2.26665pt}{\resizebox{759247}{445645}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.22499pt}{$\scriptstyle(2)$}}}}}{\raisebox{-2.26666pt}{\resizebox{759248}{445645}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.875pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle(2)$}}}}}}|^{2}\lesssim b^{5},

(3.59)

\displaystyle\int_{y\leq 2B_{1}}|H^{2}\tilde{\varPsi}_{0}^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-2.26666pt}{\resizebox{868894}{445645}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.75pt}{$\displaystyle(1)$}}}}}{\raisebox{-2.26666pt}{\resizebox{868894}{445645}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.75pt}{$\textstyle(1)$}}}}}{\raisebox{-2.26665pt}{\resizebox{759247}{445645}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.22499pt}{$\scriptstyle(1)$}}}}}{\raisebox{-2.26666pt}{\resizebox{759248}{445645}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.875pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle(1)$}}}}}}|^{2}+|H^{2}\tilde{\varPsi}_{0}^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-2.26666pt}{\resizebox{868894}{445645}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.75pt}{$\displaystyle(2)$}}}}}{\raisebox{-2.26666pt}{\resizebox{868894}{445645}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.75pt}{$\textstyle(2)$}}}}}{\raisebox{-2.26665pt}{\resizebox{759247}{445645}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.22499pt}{$\scriptstyle(2)$}}}}}{\raisebox{-2.26666pt}{\resizebox{759248}{445645}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.875pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle(2)$}}}}}}|^{2}\lesssim\frac{b^{6}}{\left|\log b\right|^{2}}.

(3.60)

\displaystyle\frac{(H\tilde{\varPsi}_{0}^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-2.26666pt}{\resizebox{868894}{445645}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.75pt}{$\displaystyle(1)$}}}}}{\raisebox{-2.26666pt}{\resizebox{868894}{445645}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.75pt}{$\textstyle(1)$}}}}}{\raisebox{-2.26665pt}{\resizebox{759247}{445645}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.22499pt}{$\scriptstyle(1)$}}}}}{\raisebox{-2.26666pt}{\resizebox{759248}{445645}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.875pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle(1)$}}}}}},\varPhi_{M})}{(\varLambda\phi,\varPhi_{M})}=\frac{2(\rho_{1}ab-\rho_{2}b^{2})}{(\rho_{1}^{2}+\rho_{2}^{2})\left|\log b\right|}\bigg(1+O\Big(\frac{1}{\left|\log b\right|}\Big)\bigg),

(3.61)

\displaystyle\frac{(H\tilde{\varPsi}_{0}^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-2.26666pt}{\resizebox{868894}{445645}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.75pt}{$\displaystyle(2)$}}}}}{\raisebox{-2.26666pt}{\resizebox{868894}{445645}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.75pt}{$\textstyle(2)$}}}}}{\raisebox{-2.26665pt}{\resizebox{759247}{445645}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.22499pt}{$\scriptstyle(2)$}}}}}{\raisebox{-2.26666pt}{\resizebox{759248}{445645}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.875pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle(2)$}}}}}},\varPhi_{M})}{(\varLambda\phi,\varPhi_{M})}=\frac{2(\rho_{1}b^{2}+\rho_{2}ab)}{(\rho_{1}^{2}+\rho_{2}^{2})\left|\log b\right|}\bigg(1+O\Big(\frac{1}{\left|\log b\right|}\Big)\bigg).

(3.62)

Proof of Lemma
3.2
.

From the localization (
3.52
) and the error of the approximate solution (
3.7
), we compute:

\displaystyle\partial_{s}\tilde{w}_{0}-\frac{\lambda_{s}}{\lambda}\varLambda\tilde{w}_{0}+\varTheta_{s}Z{\rm R}\tilde{w}_{0}+{\rm\tilde{J}}\Big(\,\rho_{1}{\it\mathbb{H}}\tilde{w}_{0}-\rho_{2}{\rm\tilde{J}}{\it\mathbb{H}}\tilde{w}_{0}+\hat{p}\hskip 0.50003pt\varLambda\phi\,\Big)

(3.63)

\displaystyle=\partial_{s}\chi_{B_{1}}w_{0}-\frac{\lambda_{s}}{\lambda}(\varLambda\chi)_{B_{1}}w_{0}+\chi_{B_{1}}\Big(\partial_{s}w_{0}-\frac{\lambda_{s}}{\lambda}\varLambda w_{0}+\varTheta_{s}ZRw_{0}\Big)

\displaystyle+\Big((e_{z}\hskip-1.00006pt\hskip-1.00006pt+\hskip-1.00006pt\hskip-1.00006ptw_{0})\hskip-1.00006pt-\hskip-1.00006pt(1\hskip-1.00006pt\hskip-1.00006pt-\hskip-1.00006pt\hskip-1.00006pt\chi_{B_{1}})w_{0}\Big)\wedge\Big(\rho_{1}{\it\mathbb{H}}(\chi_{B_{1}}w_{0})\hskip-1.00006pt-\hskip-1.00006pt\rho_{2}(e_{z}\hskip-1.00006pt+\hskip-1.00006pt\tilde{w}_{0})\hskip-1.00006pt\wedge\hskip-1.00006pt{\it\mathbb{H}}(\chi_{B_{1}}w_{0})\hskip-1.00006pt+\hskip-1.00006pt\hat{p}\hskip 0.50003pt\varLambda\phi\Big)

\displaystyle=\chi_{B_{1}}\big(\varPsi_{0}+{\rm Mod}\big)

\displaystyle+\partial_{s}\chi_{B_{1}}w_{0}-\hskip-1.00006pt\frac{\lambda_{s}}{\lambda}(\varLambda\chi)_{B_{1}}w_{0}+(1-\chi_{B_{1}}){\rm\tilde{J}}\big(\hat{p}\hskip 0.50003pt\varLambda\phi\big)

\displaystyle-\chi_{B_{1}}(1-\chi_{B_{1}})w_{0}\wedge\Big(\rho_{1}{\it\mathbb{H}}w_{0}-\rho_{2}(e_{z}+w_{0})\wedge{\it\mathbb{H}}w_{0}+\hat{p}\hskip 0.50003pt\varLambda\phi\Big)

\displaystyle+\rho_{2}\chi_{B_{1}}(1-\chi_{B_{1}}){\rm\tilde{J}}\big(w_{0}\wedge{\it\mathbb{H}}w_{0}\big)

\displaystyle-{\rm\tilde{J}}\Big\{\hskip-1.00006pt\big(\rho_{1}\hskip-1.00006pt-\hskip-1.00006pt\rho_{2}(e_{z}+\tilde{w}_{0})\hskip-1.00006pt\wedge\hskip-1.00006pt\hskip-1.00006pt\big)\Big(2\partial_{y}\chi_{B_{1}}\partial_{y}w_{0}+\Delta_{y}w_{0}+2(1+Z)\partial_{y}\chi_{B_{1}}\,e_{y}\wedge w_{0}\hskip-1.00006pt\Big)\hskip-1.00006pt\Big\}.

For the second line of RHS of (
3.63
), we reformulate them according to the anticipated modulation equations

\displaystyle\partial_{s}\chi_{B_{1}}w_{0}-\frac{\lambda_{s}}{\lambda}(\varLambda\chi)_{B_{1}}w_{0}+(1-\chi_{B_{1}})\hskip 0.50003pt{\rm\tilde{J}}\big(\hat{p}\hskip 0.50003pt\varLambda\phi\big)

\displaystyle=\big(b_{s}+b^{2}+a^{2}\big)\,O\Big(\frac{w_{0}}{b}\Big)\mathbf{1}_{y\sim B_{1}}-\Big(\frac{\lambda_{s}}{\lambda}+b\Big)O(w_{0})\mathbf{1}_{y\sim B_{1}}

\displaystyle+O\big(b^{2}y\log y\big)(e_{x}+e_{y})\mathbf{1}_{y\sim B_{1}}+O\big(b^{3}y^{2}(\log y)^{2}\big)e_{z}\mathbf{1}_{y\sim B_{1}}+O\big(b\hskip 0.50003pty^{-1}\big)(e_{x}+e_{y})\mathbf{1}_{y\gtrsim B_{1}}

\displaystyle+(\varTheta_{s}+a)\varLambda\phi\big(e_{y}+O(\tilde{w}_{0})\big)\hskip 0.50003pt\mathbf{1}_{y\gtrsim B_{1}}-\Big(\frac{\lambda_{s}}{\lambda}+b\Big)\varLambda\phi\big(e_{x}+O(\tilde{w}_{0})\big)\hskip 0.50003pt\mathbf{1}_{y\gtrsim B_{1}}.

To treat the third line of the RHS of (
3.63
), from the cancellation (
3.21
), which is

\big(\rho_{1}-\rho_{2}{\rm R}\big)\begin{bmatrix}\,{\it\mathbb{H}}^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-2.26666pt}{\resizebox{868894}{445645}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.75pt}{$\displaystyle(1)$}}}}}{\raisebox{-2.26666pt}{\resizebox{868894}{445645}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.75pt}{$\textstyle(1)$}}}}}{\raisebox{-2.26665pt}{\resizebox{759247}{445645}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.22499pt}{$\scriptstyle(1)$}}}}}{\raisebox{-2.26666pt}{\resizebox{759248}{445645}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.875pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle(1)$}}}}}}(w_{0}^{1})\,\\
{\it\mathbb{H}}^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-2.26666pt}{\resizebox{868894}{445645}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.75pt}{$\displaystyle(2)$}}}}}{\raisebox{-2.26666pt}{\resizebox{868894}{445645}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.75pt}{$\textstyle(2)$}}}}}{\raisebox{-2.26665pt}{\resizebox{759247}{445645}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.22499pt}{$\scriptstyle(2)$}}}}}{\raisebox{-2.26666pt}{\resizebox{759248}{445645}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.875pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle(2)$}}}}}}(w_{0}^{1})\\
0\end{bmatrix}-\begin{bmatrix}\,a\,\\
b\\
0\end{bmatrix}\varLambda\phi=0,

we have the estimate

\displaystyle-\chi_{B_{1}}(1\hskip-1.00006pt-\hskip-1.00006pt\chi_{B_{1}})w_{0}\wedge\Big(\rho_{1}{\it\mathbb{H}}w_{0}-\rho_{2}(e_{z}+w_{0})\wedge{\it\mathbb{H}}w_{0}+\varLambda\phi\,\hat{p}\Big)

\displaystyle=O\Big(|w_{0}|\cdot\big(|{\it\mathbb{H}}(w_{0}^{2})|+b^{2}|{\it\mathbb{H}}S_{0,2}|+|w_{0}|\,|{\it\mathbb{H}}w_{0}|\big)\Big)\mathbf{1}_{y\sim B_{1}}

\displaystyle-\big(\varTheta_{s}+a\big)\varLambda\phi\hskip 1.00006pt\big(e_{x}\wedge w_{0}\big)\mathbf{1}_{y\sim B_{1}}-\Big(\frac{\lambda_{s}}{\lambda}+b\Big)\varLambda\phi\hskip 1.00006pt\big(e_{y}\wedge w_{0}\big)\mathbf{1}_{y\sim B_{1}}

\displaystyle=O\big(b^{3}\hskip 0.50003pty^{2}\log y\big)\mathbf{1}_{y\sim B_{1}}-\big(\varTheta_{s}+a\big)\varLambda\phi\hskip 1.00006ptO(w_{0})\mathbf{1}_{y\sim B_{1}}-\Big(\frac{\lambda_{s}}{\lambda}+b\Big)\varLambda\phi\hskip 1.00006ptO(w_{0})\mathbf{1}_{y\sim B_{1}}.

Moreover, the fourth line of (
3.63
) is in fact

\displaystyle\rho_{2}\chi_{B_{1}}(1\hskip-1.00006pt-\hskip-1.00006pt\chi_{B_{1}}){\rm\tilde{J}}\big(w_{0}\hskip-1.00006pt\wedge\hskip-1.00006pt{\it\mathbb{H}}w_{0}\big)

\displaystyle=O\Big(|w_{0}|\hskip 0.50003pt|{\it\mathbb{H}}w_{0}|+|w_{0}|^{2}\hskip 0.50003pt|{\it\mathbb{H}}w_{0}|\Big)\mathbf{1}_{y\sim B_{1}}=O\big(b^{2}(\log y)^{2}\big)\hskip 0.50003pt\mathbf{1}_{y\sim B_{1}}.

Finally for the last line of (
3.63
), we estimate it by brute force

\displaystyle-{\rm\tilde{J}}\Big\{\hskip-1.00006pt\big(\rho_{1}\hskip-1.00006pt-\hskip-1.00006pt\rho_{2}(e_{z}+\tilde{w}_{0})\hskip-1.00006pt\wedge\hskip-1.00006pt\big)\Big(2\partial_{y}\chi_{B_{1}}\partial_{y}w_{0}+\Delta_{y}w_{0}+2(1\hskip-1.00006pt+\hskip-1.00006ptZ)\partial_{y}\chi_{B_{1}}\hskip 0.50003pte_{y}\hskip-1.00006pt\wedge\hskip-1.00006ptw_{0}\Big)\hskip-1.00006pt\Big\}

\displaystyle=O\bigg(\frac{1}{B_{1}}|\partial_{y}w_{0}|+\frac{1}{B_{1}^{2}}|w_{0}|+\frac{1}{B_{1}(1+y^{2})}|w_{0}|\bigg)\,\mathbf{1}_{y\sim B_{1}}=O\bigg(\frac{b^{\frac{3}{2}}\log y}{\left|\log b\right|}\bigg)\hskip 0.50003pt\mathbf{1}_{y\sim B_{1}}.

Injecting these computations into (
3.63
), we obtain the localized error

\displaystyle\tilde{\varPsi}_{0}

\displaystyle=\chi_{B_{1}}\varPsi_{0}+O\big(b^{2}y\log y\big)(e_{x}+e_{y})\mathbf{1}_{y\sim B_{1}}

(3.64)

\displaystyle+O\big(b^{3}y^{2}(\log y)^{2}\big)e_{z}\mathbf{1}_{y\sim B_{1}}+O\big(b\hskip 0.50003pty^{-1}\big)(e_{x}+e_{y})\mathbf{1}_{y\gtrsim B_{1}}

\displaystyle+O\big(b^{3}\hskip 0.50003pty^{2}\log y\big)\mathbf{1}_{y\sim B_{1}}+O\big(b^{2}(\log y)^{2}\big)\hskip 0.50003pt\mathbf{1}_{y\sim B_{1}}+O\bigg(\frac{b^{\frac{3}{2}}\log y}{\left|\log b\right|}\bigg)\hskip 0.50003pt\mathbf{1}_{y\sim B_{1}}

\displaystyle=\chi_{B_{1}}\varPsi_{0}+O\big(b^{2}y\log y\big)(e_{x}+e_{y})\mathbf{1}_{y\sim B_{1}}

\displaystyle+O\big(b^{3}y^{2}(\log y)^{2}\big)e_{z}\mathbf{1}_{y\sim B_{1}}+O\big(b\hskip 0.50003pty^{-1}\big)(e_{x}+e_{y})\mathbf{1}_{y\gtrsim B_{1}},

and also the explicit formula of the localized modulation vector (
3.54
). Note from (
3.64
) that most of the additional errors are supported in the region
[B_{1},2B_{1}]
(labelled by
\mathbf{1}_{y\sim B_{1}}
), and thus we can easily check they do not perturb the estimate in Lemma
3.1
, in particular thanks to the choice
B_{1}
. The details are left to readers.

\square

4
The Trapped Regime

We now aim at seeking for an actual blowup solution to (
1.1
). To this end, we in this section study the solution of the form (
4.2
). More precisely, we set up the bootstrap regime, give the orthogonality condition, and compute the modulation equations of the geometrical parameters.

4.1
Bootstrap setup and orthogonality conditions

In this subsection, we describe the set of initial data leading to the blowup scenario of Theorem
1.1
, and give the corresponding bootstrap statement. For any initial data
u_{0}\in\dot{H}^{1}
with

\|\nabla u_{0}-\nabla Q\|_{L^{2}}\ll 1,

(4.1)

we may decompose the corresponding solution
u(t)
in a small time interval
[0,t_{1}]
by

u(t)=e^{\varTheta{\rm R}}\big(Q+\hat{v}\big)_{\lambda},\quad\mbox{with}\quad\left\{\begin{matrix}\hat{v}=\big[e_{r},e_{\tau},Q\big]\hat{w},\\
\hskip-20.00003pt\hat{w}=\tilde{w}_{0}+w,\end{matrix}\right.

(4.2)

where
\tilde{w}_{0}
is the localized approximate solution, and
w
is a correction term called the radiation. The existence of this decomposition at any fixed time follows from the variational characterization of
Q
, see for example
[
31
,
36
]
. Then by the standard modulation theory, the solution can be modulated such that there exists the geometrical parameter maps
\lambda,\varTheta,a,b\in\mathcal{C}^{1}([0,t_{1}],\mathbb{R})
, and the radiation

w=\begin{bmatrix}\alpha\\
\beta\\
\gamma\end{bmatrix}

(4.3)

satisfying the orthogonality conditions

(\alpha,\varPhi_{M})=(\alpha,H\varPhi_{M})=(\beta,\varPhi_{M})=(\beta,H\varPhi_{M})=0.

(4.4)

Here
\varPhi_{M}
is a localized replacement of
\varLambda\phi
defined, for sufficiently large universal constant
M\gg 1
, by

\varPhi_{M}=\chi_{M}\varLambda\phi-c_{M}H\big(\chi_{M}\varLambda\phi\big),

(4.5)

with

c_{M}=\frac{(\chi_{M}\varLambda\phi,\,T_{1})}{(H(\chi_{M}\varLambda\phi),\,T_{1})}\sim c_{\chi}M^{2}\big(1+o(1)\big).

It admits the following non-degenerate and orthogonal properties

\left\{\begin{aligned} &\,\|\varPhi_{M}\|_{L^{2}}=4\log M\big(1+o(1)\big),\\
&\,(\varLambda\phi,\varPhi_{M})=(T_{1},H\varPhi_{M})=4\log M\big(1+o(1)\big),\\
&\,(T_{1},\varPhi_{M})=(\varLambda\phi,H\varPhi_{M})=0.\end{aligned}\right.

(4.6)

Indeed, to study the orthogonality (
4.4
), we define the vectorial function

\mathcal{F}(\lambda,\varTheta,a,b,u)=\Big[(\alpha,\varPhi_{M}),\,(\beta,\varPhi_{M}),\,(\alpha,H\varPhi_{M}),\,(\beta,H\varPhi_{M})\Big].

Using the explicit construction of
\tilde{w}_{0}
in Lemma
3.2
, we compute the following derivatives at point
P
where
(\lambda,\varTheta,a,b,u)=(1,0,0,0,Q)

\begin{aligned} &\,\frac{\partial}{\partial\lambda}\Big|_{P}\Big(e^{-\varTheta R}u_{\frac{1}{\lambda}}\Big)=\varLambda\phi\,e_{r},\\
&\,\frac{\partial}{\partial\varTheta}\Big|_{P}\Big(e^{-\varTheta R}u_{\frac{1}{\lambda}}\Big)=-\varLambda\phi\,e_{\tau},\end{aligned}\quad\quad\begin{aligned} &\,\frac{\partial}{\partial a}\Big|_{P}\big(\tilde{w}_{0}\big)=\tilde{\varPhi}_{1,0},\\
&\,\frac{\partial}{\partial b}\Big|_{P}\big(\tilde{w}_{0}\big)=\tilde{\varPhi}_{0,1},\end{aligned}

from which follows the non-degeneracy of the Jacobian of
\mathcal{F}
at
P
:

\left|\begin{matrix}(\varLambda\phi,\varPhi_{M})&0&(\varLambda\phi,H\varPhi_{M})&0\\
0&(\varLambda\phi,\varPhi_{M})&0&(\varLambda\phi,H\varPhi_{M})\\[5.0pt]
\big(\varPhi_{1,0}^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-2.26666pt}{\resizebox{868894}{445645}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.75pt}{$\displaystyle(1)$}}}}}{\raisebox{-2.26666pt}{\resizebox{868894}{445645}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.75pt}{$\textstyle(1)$}}}}}{\raisebox{-2.26665pt}{\resizebox{759247}{445645}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.22499pt}{$\scriptstyle(1)$}}}}}{\raisebox{-2.26666pt}{\resizebox{759248}{445645}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.875pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle(1)$}}}}}},\varPhi_{M}\big)&\big(\varPhi_{1,0}^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-2.26666pt}{\resizebox{868894}{445645}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.75pt}{$\displaystyle(2)$}}}}}{\raisebox{-2.26666pt}{\resizebox{868894}{445645}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.75pt}{$\textstyle(2)$}}}}}{\raisebox{-2.26665pt}{\resizebox{759247}{445645}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.22499pt}{$\scriptstyle(2)$}}}}}{\raisebox{-2.26666pt}{\resizebox{759248}{445645}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.875pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle(2)$}}}}}},\varPhi_{M}\big)&\big(\varPhi_{1,0}^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-2.26666pt}{\resizebox{868894}{445645}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.75pt}{$\displaystyle(1)$}}}}}{\raisebox{-2.26666pt}{\resizebox{868894}{445645}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.75pt}{$\textstyle(1)$}}}}}{\raisebox{-2.26665pt}{\resizebox{759247}{445645}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.22499pt}{$\scriptstyle(1)$}}}}}{\raisebox{-2.26666pt}{\resizebox{759248}{445645}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.875pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle(1)$}}}}}},H\varPhi_{M}\big)&\big(\varPhi_{1,0}^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-2.26666pt}{\resizebox{868894}{445645}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.75pt}{$\displaystyle(2)$}}}}}{\raisebox{-2.26666pt}{\resizebox{868894}{445645}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.75pt}{$\textstyle(2)$}}}}}{\raisebox{-2.26665pt}{\resizebox{759247}{445645}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.22499pt}{$\scriptstyle(2)$}}}}}{\raisebox{-2.26666pt}{\resizebox{759248}{445645}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.875pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle(2)$}}}}}},H\varPhi_{M}\big)\\[5.0pt]
\big(\varPhi_{0,1}^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-2.26666pt}{\resizebox{868894}{445645}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.75pt}{$\displaystyle(1)$}}}}}{\raisebox{-2.26666pt}{\resizebox{868894}{445645}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.75pt}{$\textstyle(1)$}}}}}{\raisebox{-2.26665pt}{\resizebox{759247}{445645}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.22499pt}{$\scriptstyle(1)$}}}}}{\raisebox{-2.26666pt}{\resizebox{759248}{445645}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.875pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle(1)$}}}}}},\varPhi_{M}\big)&\big(\varPhi_{0,1}^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-2.26666pt}{\resizebox{868894}{445645}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.75pt}{$\displaystyle(2)$}}}}}{\raisebox{-2.26666pt}{\resizebox{868894}{445645}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.75pt}{$\textstyle(2)$}}}}}{\raisebox{-2.26665pt}{\resizebox{759247}{445645}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.22499pt}{$\scriptstyle(2)$}}}}}{\raisebox{-2.26666pt}{\resizebox{759248}{445645}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.875pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle(2)$}}}}}},\varPhi_{M}\big)&\big(\varPhi_{0,1}^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-2.26666pt}{\resizebox{868894}{445645}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.75pt}{$\displaystyle(1)$}}}}}{\raisebox{-2.26666pt}{\resizebox{868894}{445645}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.75pt}{$\textstyle(1)$}}}}}{\raisebox{-2.26665pt}{\resizebox{759247}{445645}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.22499pt}{$\scriptstyle(1)$}}}}}{\raisebox{-2.26666pt}{\resizebox{759248}{445645}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.875pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle(1)$}}}}}},H\varPhi_{M}\big)&\big(\varPhi_{0,1}^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-2.26666pt}{\resizebox{868894}{445645}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.75pt}{$\displaystyle(2)$}}}}}{\raisebox{-2.26666pt}{\resizebox{868894}{445645}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.75pt}{$\textstyle(2)$}}}}}{\raisebox{-2.26665pt}{\resizebox{759247}{445645}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.22499pt}{$\scriptstyle(2)$}}}}}{\raisebox{-2.26666pt}{\resizebox{759248}{445645}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.875pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle(2)$}}}}}},H\varPhi_{M}\big)\end{matrix}\right|=\frac{\big(1\hskip-1.00006pt+\hskip-1.00006pto(1)\big)}{\rho_{1}^{2}+\rho_{2}^{2}}(\varLambda\phi,\varPhi_{M})^{4}\neq 0.

According to the implicit function theorem, there exist a constant
\delta>0
, a neighborhood
V_{P}
of the point
P
, and a unique
\mathcal{C}^{1}
geometrical parameter map

(\lambda,\varTheta,a,b):\big\{u\in\dot{H}^{1}:\|u-Q\|_{\dot{H}^{1}}<\delta\big\}\to V_{P},

such that
\mathcal{F}(\lambda,\varTheta,a,b,u)=0
. In view of (
4.2
), this ensures the orthogonality of the radiation (
4.4
). Moveover, by the regularity of LL flow, the map
(\lambda,\varTheta,a,b)
is
\mathcal{C}^{1}
function of
t
, concluding our claim. Then we may measure the regularity of the
w
by the following Sobolev norms associated to the linear operator (
2.36
): the energy norm

\mathcal{E}_{1}=\int\big|\nabla w\big|^{2}+\Big|\frac{w}{y}\Big|^{2},

(4.7)

and higher-order energy norm

\mathcal{E}_{2}=\int|\mathbb{H^{\perp}}w|^{2},\quad\mathcal{E}_{4}=\int|(\mathbb{H^{\perp}})^{2}w|^{2}.

(4.8)

We now make the following assumptions on the initial data
u_{0}
, which describe a codimension one set (see Proposition
4.1
and its proof in subsection
5.4
) close to the ground state
Q
in
\dot{H}^{1}
:

•

Initial bound on the modulation parameters:

0<b(0)<b^{\ast}(M)\ll 1,\quad a(0)\leq\dfrac{b(0)}{4\left|\log b(0)\right|}.

(4.9)

•

Initial data of the scaling parameter: (Up to a fixed scaling, we can always assume this; thus this assumption is not compulsory)

\lambda(0)=1.

(4.10)

•

Initial energy bounds:

\left\{\begin{aligned} &0<\mathcal{E}_{1}(0)<\delta(b^{\ast})\ll 1,\\
&0<\mathcal{E}_{2}(0)+\mathcal{E}_{4}(0)<b(0)^{10}.\end{aligned}\right.

(4.11)

where
\delta(b^{\ast})
denotes a generic constant related to
b^{\ast}
which satisfies

\delta(b^{\ast})\to 0,\;\;\mbox{as}\;\;b^{\ast}\to 0.

(4.12)

The propagation of regularity of
u(t)
ensures that these bounds can be preserved in a small time interval
[0,t_{1})
. This suggests given a universal large constant
K
, independent of
M
in Lemma
3.1
, we may assume on
[0,t_{1}]
the following bounds:

•

Pointwise bounds on the modulation parameters:

0<b(t)\leq Kb^{\ast}(M),\quad a(t)\leq\dfrac{b(t)}{\left|\log b(t)\right|}.

(4.13)

•

Pointwise energy bounds:

\displaystyle\mathcal{E}_{1}(t)\leq K\hskip 0.50003pt\delta(b^{\ast}),

(4.14)

\displaystyle\mathcal{E}_{2}(t)\leq K\hskip 0.50003ptb(t)^{2}\left|\log b(t)\right|^{6},

(4.15)

\displaystyle\mathcal{E}_{4}(t)\leq K\hskip 0.50003pt\dfrac{b(t)^{4}}{\left|\log b(t)\right|^{2}}.

(4.16)

The core of our analysis is the following proposition, which yields the contraction of the blowup regime.

Proposition 4.1
(Trapped regime)

Assume that
K
in (
4.13
)–(
4.16
) has been chosen large enough, independent of
M
. Then for any large enough rescaled initial time
s_{0}
, and any initial data
\big(w,\lambda,\varTheta,b\big)(0)
satisfying (
4.9
), (
4.10
), (
4.11
), there exists

a(0)=a\Big(b(0),w(0)\Big)\in\left[-\frac{b(0)}{4\left|\log b(0)\right|},\,\frac{b(0)}{4\left|\log b(0)\right|}\right],

(4.17)

such that (
4.2
) the corresponding solution to (
1.1
) satisfies for
t\in[0,t_{1}]
that:

•

Refined bounds on the modulation parameters:

0<b(t)\leq\dfrac{K}{2}\hskip 0.50003ptb^{\ast}(M),\quad a(t)\leq\dfrac{b(t)}{2\left|\log b(t)\right|}.

(4.18)

•

Refined energy bounds:

\displaystyle\mathcal{E}_{1}(t)\leq\dfrac{K}{2}\delta(b^{\ast}),

(4.19)

\displaystyle\mathcal{E}_{2}(t)\leq\dfrac{K}{2}b(t)^{2}\left|\log b(t)\right|^{6},

(4.20)

\displaystyle\mathcal{E}_{4}(t)\leq K(1-\eta)\dfrac{b(t)^{4}}{\left|\log b(t)\right|^{2}},

(4.21)

where
\eta\in(0,1)
is a universal constant independent of
M
.

This proposition implies the solution
u
is trapped in the regime (
4.18
), (
4.19
)–(
4.21
) , and thus the bounds can be maintained within the lifespan of
u
. The proof is given in subsection
5.4
.

4.2
Equation for the radiation

Recall from (
4.2
) that we have the decomposition

\hat{w}=\tilde{w}_{0}+w,

Injecting this into the vectorial formula (
2.28
), and applying (
3.53
), we obtain the equation for the radiation

\partial_{s}w-\frac{\lambda_{s}}{\lambda}\varLambda w+\rho_{1}{\rm\hat{J}}{\it\mathbb{H}}w-\rho_{2}{\rm\hat{J}}^{2}{\it\mathbb{H}}w+f=0,

(4.22)

where

f=\widetilde{{\rm Mod}}+\tilde{\varPsi}_{0}+\mathcal{R}.

(4.23)

The modulation vector
\widetilde{{\rm Mod}}
, the error
\tilde{\varPsi}_{0}
are given by (
3.53
), and the term
\mathcal{R}
contains the residue involving the phase derivative and cross terms of
\tilde{w}_{0}
and
w
:

\mathcal{R}=\varTheta_{s}Z{\rm R}w-\rho_{2}{\rm\hat{J}}\left(w\wedge{\it\mathbb{H}}\tilde{w}_{0}\right)+w\wedge\Big(\rho_{1}{\it\mathbb{H}}\tilde{w}_{0}-\rho_{2}{\rm\tilde{J}}{\it\mathbb{H}}\tilde{w}_{0}+\varLambda\phi\,\hat{p}\Big).

(4.24)

We rewrite
w
in terms of the original coordinate
(r,t)
by

W(t,r)=w(s,y),

(4.25)

and then (
4.22
) is equivalent to

\partial_{t}W+\rho_{1}{\rm\hat{J}}_{\lambda}\mathbb{H_{\lambda}}W-\rho_{2}{\rm\hat{J}}_{\lambda}^{2}\mathbb{H_{\lambda}}W+F=0,

(4.26)

where

{\rm\hat{J}}_{\lambda}=\big(e_{z}+\widehat{W}\big)\wedge,\quad F=\frac{1}{\lambda^{2}}\,f_{\lambda}.

(4.27)

Here the rescaled vectorial Hamiltonian
\mathbb{H_{\lambda}}
is defined by

\mathbb{H_{\lambda}}\begin{bmatrix}\alpha_{\lambda}\\
\beta_{\lambda}\\
\gamma_{\lambda}\end{bmatrix}=\begin{bmatrix}H_{\lambda}\alpha_{\lambda}\\
H_{\lambda}\beta_{\lambda}\\
-\Delta_{r}\gamma_{\lambda}\end{bmatrix}+\frac{2(1+Z_{\lambda})}{\lambda}\begin{bmatrix}-\partial_{r}\gamma_{\lambda}\\
0\\
\partial_{r}\alpha_{\lambda}+\frac{Z_{\lambda}}{r}\alpha_{\lambda}\end{bmatrix},

(4.28)

where the rescaled scalar Hamiltonian is
H_{\lambda}=-\Delta_{r}+V_{\lambda}/r^{2}
. The notation of
\lambda
subscript is given by (
2.10
), from which
Z_{\lambda}(r)=Z(r/\lambda)
,
\alpha_{\lambda}(t,r)=\alpha(t,r/\lambda)
, and so forth. Similar to (
2.29
),
H_{\lambda}
admits the factorization
H_{\lambda}=A_{\lambda}^{\ast}A_{\lambda}
with

A_{\lambda}=-\partial_{r}+\frac{Z_{\lambda}}{r},\quad A_{\lambda}^{\ast}=\partial_{r}+\frac{1+Z_{\lambda}}{r}.

(4.29)

In the forthcoming analysis, we define the dominating part of
\mathbb{H_{\lambda}}
by

\mathbb{H_{\lambda}^{\perp}}\begin{bmatrix}\alpha_{\lambda}\\
\beta_{\lambda}\\
\gamma_{\lambda}\end{bmatrix}=\begin{bmatrix}H_{\lambda}\alpha_{\lambda}\\
H_{\lambda}\beta_{\lambda}\\
0\end{bmatrix},

(4.30)

which, from (
4.29
), admits the factorization
\mathbb{H_{\lambda}^{\perp}}=\mathbb{A_{\lambda}^{\ast}}\mathbb{A_{\lambda}}
with

\mathbb{A_{\lambda}}\begin{bmatrix}\alpha_{\lambda}\\
\beta_{\lambda}\\
\gamma_{\lambda}\end{bmatrix}=\begin{bmatrix}A_{\lambda}\alpha_{\lambda}\\
A_{\lambda}\beta_{\lambda}\\
0\end{bmatrix},\quad\mathbb{A_{\lambda}^{\ast}}\begin{bmatrix}\alpha_{\lambda}\\
\beta_{\lambda}\\
\gamma_{\lambda}\end{bmatrix}=\begin{bmatrix}A_{\lambda}^{\ast}\alpha_{\lambda}\\
A_{\lambda}^{\ast}\beta_{\lambda}\\
0\end{bmatrix}.

(4.31)

In view of the scaling
y/r=1/\lambda
, there holds

{\it\mathbb{H}}w=\lambda^{2}\mathbb{H_{\lambda}}W,\quad\mathbb{H^{\perp}}w=\lambda^{2}\mathbb{H_{\lambda}^{\perp}}W,

(4.32)

and from (
2.38
), we have

{\rm R}\mathbb{H_{\lambda}^{\perp}}=\mathbb{H_{\lambda}^{\perp}}{\rm R},\quad{\rm R}\mathbb{H_{\lambda}}{\rm R}=-\mathbb{H_{\lambda}^{\perp}}.

(4.33)

4.3
Modulation equations

This subsection is devoted to derivation of the modulation equations of
(\lambda,\varTheta,a,b)
, which constitute a ODE system that determines the blowup dynamics. We will use the equation (
4.22
) and the orthogonality conditions (
4.4
).

Proposition 4.2
(Modulation equations)

Assume (
4.13
)–(
4.16
). Then there hold the following estimates on the modulation parameters:

\left\{\begin{aligned} &\,\Big|a_{s}+\frac{2ab}{\left|\log b\right|}\Big|+\bigg|b_{s}+b^{2}\bigg(1+\frac{2}{\left|\log b\right|}\bigg)\bigg|\lesssim\frac{1}{\sqrt{\log M}}\bigg(\sqrt{\mathcal{E}_{4}}+\frac{b^{2}}{\left|\log b\right|}\bigg),\\
&\,\big|a+\varTheta_{s}\big|+\Big|b+\frac{\lambda_{s}}{\lambda}\Big|\lesssim C(M)\,b^{3}.\end{aligned}\right.

(4.34)

Remark 4.1

(i)
The expression in companion with
b_{s}
in the first identity is not
a^{2}+b^{2}
, different from what is suggested by
\widetilde{{\rm Mod}}
. This is a direct result the smallness of
a
(
4.13
) and the flux computations (
3.61
), (
3.62
).
(ii)
The
\sqrt{\log M}
on the denominator is crucial in the following analysis, especially when closing the bootstrap bound of
\mathcal{E}_{4}
and specifying an appropriate initial data of
a
.

Proof of Proposition
4.2
.

We project (
4.22
) onto
\{e_{x},e_{y}\}
to obtain the equations for
\alpha,\beta
, and then take their
L^{2}
inner product with
H\varPhi_{M}
,
\varPhi_{M}
defined by (
4.6
) respectively. Computing each term in the resulting formulas by the interpolation bounds in Appendix
A
, (
4.13
)–(
4.16
), and also (
3.54
), (
3.61
), (
3.62
), we obtain

\begin{gathered}\rho_{1}\hskip 0.50003pta_{s}-\rho_{2}\big(b_{s}\hskip-1.00006pt+\hskip-1.00006ptb^{2}\hskip-1.00006pt+\hskip-1.00006pta^{2}\big)+\frac{2}{\left|\log b\right|}\big(\rho_{1}ab-\rho_{2}b^{2}\big)\lesssim\frac{1}{\sqrt{\log M}}\bigg(\sqrt{\mathcal{E}_{4}}+\frac{b^{2}}{\left|\log b\right|^{2}}\bigg),\\
\rho_{2}\hskip 0.50003pta_{s}+\rho_{1}\big(b_{s}\hskip-1.00006pt+\hskip-1.00006ptb^{2}\hskip-1.00006pt+\hskip-1.00006pta^{2}\big)+\frac{2}{\left|\log b\right|}\big(\rho_{1}ab+\rho_{2}b^{2}\big)\lesssim\frac{1}{\sqrt{\log M}}\bigg(\sqrt{\mathcal{E}_{4}}+\frac{b^{2}}{\left|\log b\right|^{2}}\bigg),\\
\big|a+\varTheta_{s}\big|+\Big|b+\frac{\lambda_{s}}{\lambda}\Big|=C(M)\,b^{3}.\end{gathered}

(4.35)

Then (
4.34
) follows by simple cancellations. For more details, reader can refer to
[
34
]
. Here we only compute the first line in (
4.35
). We first define

U(t)=|a_{s}|+\big|b_{s}\hskip-1.00006pt+\hskip-1.00006ptb^{2}\hskip-1.00006pt+\hskip-1.00006pta^{2}\big|+\big|a+\varTheta_{s}\big|+\Big|b+\frac{\lambda_{s}}{\lambda}\Big|.

(4.36)

Then taking the inner product of the first component of (
4.22
) with
H\varPhi_{M}
, we obtain the system

\displaystyle 0

\displaystyle=\big(\partial_{s}\alpha,H\varPhi_{M}\big)-\frac{\lambda_{s}}{\lambda}\big(\varLambda\alpha,H\varPhi_{M}\big)

(4.37)
