\displaystyle+\rho_{1}\Big(({\rm\hat{J}}{\it\mathbb{H}}w)^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-2.26666pt}{\resizebox{868894}{445645}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.75pt}{$\displaystyle(1)$}}}}}{\raisebox{-2.26666pt}{\resizebox{868894}{445645}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.75pt}{$\textstyle(1)$}}}}}{\raisebox{-2.26665pt}{\resizebox{759247}{445645}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.22499pt}{$\scriptstyle(1)$}}}}}{\raisebox{-2.26666pt}{\resizebox{759248}{445645}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.875pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle(1)$}}}}}},H\varPhi_{M}\Big)-\rho_{2}\Big(({\rm\hat{J}}^{2}{\it\mathbb{H}}w)^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-2.26666pt}{\resizebox{868894}{445645}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.75pt}{$\displaystyle(1)$}}}}}{\raisebox{-2.26666pt}{\resizebox{868894}{445645}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.75pt}{$\textstyle(1)$}}}}}{\raisebox{-2.26665pt}{\resizebox{759247}{445645}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.22499pt}{$\scriptstyle(1)$}}}}}{\raisebox{-2.26666pt}{\resizebox{759248}{445645}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.875pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle(1)$}}}}}},H\varPhi_{M}\Big)

\displaystyle+\big(\mathcal{R},H\varPhi_{M}\big)+\big(\widetilde{{\rm Mod}},H\varPhi_{M}\big)+\big(\tilde{\varPsi}_{0},H\varPhi_{M}\big).

The time derivative term vanishes thanks to (
4.4
). For the second term, by commuting
H,\varLambda
, we split it into

\big(\varLambda\alpha,H\varPhi_{M}\big)=\big(\varLambda H\alpha,\varPhi_{M}\big)+2\big(H\alpha,\varPhi_{M}\big)-\bigg(\frac{\varLambda V}{y^{2}}\alpha,\varPhi_{M}\bigg).

(4.38)

Again using (
4.4
), the second term vanishes. From (
A.3
), (
4.16
), we have

\big|\big(\varLambda H\alpha,\varPhi_{M}\big)\big|\lesssim C(M)\bigg(\int\frac{\left|\partial_{y}H\alpha\right|^{2}}{1+y^{4}}\bigg)^{\frac{1}{2}}\lesssim C(M)\sqrt{\mathcal{E}_{4}},

where
C(M)
is a universal constant appearing in (
A.3
) induced by the coercivity (
A.2
). The constants in the following formulas are similar. For the last term in (
4.38
), an analogous estimate holds, giving the bound

\Big|\frac{\lambda_{s}}{\lambda}\Big|\,\big|\big(\varLambda\alpha,H\varPhi_{M}\big)\big|\lesssim C(M)\hskip 0.50003ptb\sqrt{\mathcal{E}_{4}}.

(4.39)

For the third term in (
4.37
), from (
2.26
),

\displaystyle\Big|\Big(({\rm\hat{J}}{\it\mathbb{H}}w)^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-2.26666pt}{\resizebox{868894}{445645}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.75pt}{$\displaystyle(1)$}}}}}{\raisebox{-2.26666pt}{\resizebox{868894}{445645}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.75pt}{$\textstyle(1)$}}}}}{\raisebox{-2.26665pt}{\resizebox{759247}{445645}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.22499pt}{$\scriptstyle(1)$}}}}}{\raisebox{-2.26666pt}{\resizebox{759248}{445645}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.875pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle(1)$}}}}}},H\varPhi_{M}\Big)\Big|

\displaystyle\lesssim\Big|\Big((1+\hat{\gamma})H\beta,H\varPhi_{M}\Big)\Big|

(4.40)

\displaystyle+\bigg|\bigg(\hat{\beta}\Big(\hskip-1.00006pt-\hskip-1.00006pt\Delta\gamma+2(1\hskip-1.00006pt+\hskip-1.00006ptZ)\Big(\partial_{y}\hskip-1.00006pt+\hskip-1.00006pt\frac{Z}{y}\Big)\alpha\Big),H\varPhi_{M}\bigg)\bigg|,

where by commuting
H
, we split the first one into

\displaystyle\Big|\Big((1+\hat{\gamma})H\beta,H\varPhi_{M}\Big)\Big|

\displaystyle\lesssim\Big|\Big((1+\hat{\gamma})H^{2}\beta,\varPhi_{M}\Big)\Big|

\displaystyle+\Big|\Big(\partial_{y}\hat{\gamma}\hskip 0.50003pt\partial_{y}H\beta,\varPhi_{M}\Big)\Big|+\Big|\Big(\Delta\hat{\gamma}H\beta,\varPhi_{M}\Big)\Big|,

Using (
A.20
), (
A.22
), we have

\Big|\Big((1+\hat{\gamma})H^{2}\beta,\varPhi_{M}\Big)\Big|\lesssim\big\|1\hskip-1.00006pt+\hskip-1.00006pt\hat{\gamma}\big\|_{L^{\infty}}\big\|H^{2}\beta\big\|_{L^{2}}\|\varPhi_{M}\|_{L^{2}}\lesssim\sqrt{\log M}\sqrt{\mathcal{E}_{4}}.

For the remaining, by (
A.3
), (
A.16
), there holds the better bound

\displaystyle\Big|\Big(\partial_{y}\hat{\gamma}\hskip 0.50003pt\partial_{y}H\beta,\varPhi_{M}\Big)\Big|+\Big|\Big(\Delta\hat{\gamma}H\beta,\varPhi_{M}\Big)\Big|

\displaystyle\lesssim C(M)\hskip 0.50003pt\bigg\{\Big\|\frac{\partial_{y}\hat{\gamma}}{y}\Big\|_{L^{\infty}}\Big\|\frac{\partial_{y}H\beta}{y(1+\left|\log y\right|)}\Big\|_{L^{2}}

\displaystyle+\Big\|\frac{\Delta\hat{\gamma}}{y(1+y)(1+\left|\log y\right|)}\Big\|_{L^{2}}\,\Big\|\frac{H\beta}{y(1+y)(1+\left|\log y\right|)}\Big\|_{L^{2}}\bigg\}\lesssim C(M)b\sqrt{\mathcal{E}_{4}}.

The other term on the RHS of (
4.40
) can be treated similarly. Recall from the constraint (
2.8
) that
|e_{z}+\hat{w}|=1
, which implies
|{\rm\hat{J}}v|\leq|v|
for any vector
v
under the Frenet basis. Thus the fourth term in (
4.37
) shares the same bound as above, so that

\Big|\Big(({\rm\hat{J}}{\it\mathbb{H}}w)^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-2.26666pt}{\resizebox{868894}{445645}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.75pt}{$\displaystyle(1)$}}}}}{\raisebox{-2.26666pt}{\resizebox{868894}{445645}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.75pt}{$\textstyle(1)$}}}}}{\raisebox{-2.26665pt}{\resizebox{759247}{445645}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.22499pt}{$\scriptstyle(1)$}}}}}{\raisebox{-2.26666pt}{\resizebox{759248}{445645}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.875pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle(1)$}}}}}},H\varPhi_{M}\Big)\Big|+\Big|\Big(({\rm\hat{J}}^{2}{\it\mathbb{H}}w)^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-2.26666pt}{\resizebox{868894}{445645}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.75pt}{$\displaystyle(1)$}}}}}{\raisebox{-2.26666pt}{\resizebox{868894}{445645}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.75pt}{$\textstyle(1)$}}}}}{\raisebox{-2.26665pt}{\resizebox{759247}{445645}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.22499pt}{$\scriptstyle(1)$}}}}}{\raisebox{-2.26666pt}{\resizebox{759248}{445645}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.875pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle(1)$}}}}}},H\varPhi_{M}\Big)\Big|\lesssim\Big(\sqrt{\log M}+C(M)b\Big)\sqrt{\mathcal{E}_{4}}.

(4.41)

We consider the term involving
\mathcal{R}
in (
4.37
). From (
4.24
) and Cauchy-Schwartz, we get

\displaystyle\big|\big(\mathcal{R},H\varPhi_{M}\big)\big|

\displaystyle\lesssim C(M)\bigg\{\big\|\varTheta_{s}Z{\rm R}w\big\|_{L^{2}(y\leq 2M)}+\big\|{\rm\hat{J}}\big(w\wedge{\it\mathbb{H}}\tilde{w}_{0}\big)\big\|_{L^{2}(y\leq 2M)}

(4.42)

\displaystyle+\Big\|w\wedge\Big(\rho_{1}{\it\mathbb{H}}\tilde{w}_{0}-\rho_{2}{\rm\tilde{J}}{\it\mathbb{H}}\tilde{w}_{0}+\varLambda\phi\hskip 0.50003pt\hat{p}\Big)\Big\|_{L^{2}(y\leq 2M)}\bigg\}.

By (
A.3
), the phase derivative term is bounded by

\big\|\varTheta_{s}Z{\rm R}w\big\|_{L^{2}(y\leq 2M)}\lesssim C(M)\big(b+U(t)\big)\sqrt{\mathcal{E}_{4}}.

Similarly, from the construction of
\tilde{w}_{0}
, the rest of the terms in (
4.42
) are bounded by

\displaystyle\big\|{\rm\hat{J}}\big(w\wedge{\it\mathbb{H}}\tilde{w}_{0}\big)\big\|_{L^{2}(y\leq 2M)}+\Big\|w\wedge\Big(\rho_{1}{\it\mathbb{H}}\tilde{w}_{0}-\rho_{2}{\rm\tilde{J}}{\it\mathbb{H}}\tilde{w}_{0}+\varLambda\phi\,\hat{p}\Big)\Big\|_{L^{2}(y\leq 2M)}

\displaystyle\lesssim C(M)\sqrt{\mathcal{E}_{4}}\,\bigg(\big\|{\it\mathbb{H}}\tilde{w}_{0}\big\|_{L^{\infty}(y\leq 2M)}+\big\|\rho_{1}{\it\mathbb{H}}\tilde{w}_{0}-\rho_{2}{\rm\tilde{J}}{\it\mathbb{H}}\tilde{w}_{0}+\varLambda\phi\hskip 0.50003pt\hat{p}\big\|_{L^{\infty}(y\leq 2M)}\bigg)

\displaystyle\lesssim C(M)\big(b+U(t)\big)\sqrt{\mathcal{E}_{4}},

and hence

\big|\big(\mathcal{R},H\varPhi_{M}\big)\big|\lesssim C(M)\big(b+U(t)\big)\sqrt{\mathcal{E}_{4}}.

(4.43)

It remains to consider the contributions of
\widetilde{{\rm Mod}}
and
\tilde{\varPsi}_{0}
in (
4.37
). From (
3.54
), we observe on the support of
\varPhi_{M}
, which is
\{y\leq 2M\}
, that
\widetilde{{\rm Mod}}
is given by

\displaystyle\widetilde{{\rm Mod}}

\displaystyle=a_{s}\hskip 0.50003pt\varPhi_{1,0}+\big(b_{s}\hskip-1.00006pt+\hskip-1.00006pta^{2}\hskip-1.00006pt+\hskip-1.00006ptb^{2}\big)\varPhi_{0,1}

\displaystyle-\Big(b+\frac{\lambda_{s}}{\lambda}\Big)\varLambda\phi\,e_{x}+(a+\varTheta_{s})\varLambda\phi\,e_{y}+C(M)b\,U(t),

which together with (
4.6
) yields that

\displaystyle\big(\widetilde{{\rm Mod}}^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-2.26666pt}{\resizebox{868894}{445645}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.75pt}{$\displaystyle(1)$}}}}}{\raisebox{-2.26666pt}{\resizebox{868894}{445645}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.75pt}{$\textstyle(1)$}}}}}{\raisebox{-2.26665pt}{\resizebox{759247}{445645}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.22499pt}{$\scriptstyle(1)$}}}}}{\raisebox{-2.26666pt}{\resizebox{759248}{445645}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.875pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle(1)$}}}}}}\hskip-1.00006pt\hskip-1.00006pt\hskip-1.00006pt,H\varPhi_{M}\big)

\displaystyle=a_{s}\big(\varPhi_{1,0}^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-2.26666pt}{\resizebox{868894}{445645}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.75pt}{$\displaystyle(1)$}}}}}{\raisebox{-2.26666pt}{\resizebox{868894}{445645}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.75pt}{$\textstyle(1)$}}}}}{\raisebox{-2.26665pt}{\resizebox{759247}{445645}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.22499pt}{$\scriptstyle(1)$}}}}}{\raisebox{-2.26666pt}{\resizebox{759248}{445645}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.875pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle(1)$}}}}}},H\varPhi_{M}\big)+\big(b_{s}\hskip-1.00006pt+\hskip-1.00006pta^{2}\hskip-1.00006pt+\hskip-1.00006ptb^{2}\big)\big(\varPhi_{0,1}^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-2.26666pt}{\resizebox{868894}{445645}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.75pt}{$\displaystyle(1)$}}}}}{\raisebox{-2.26666pt}{\resizebox{868894}{445645}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.75pt}{$\textstyle(1)$}}}}}{\raisebox{-2.26665pt}{\resizebox{759247}{445645}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.22499pt}{$\scriptstyle(1)$}}}}}{\raisebox{-2.26666pt}{\resizebox{759248}{445645}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.875pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle(1)$}}}}}},H\varPhi_{M}\big)+C(M)b\,U(t)

(4.44)

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{\rho_{1}^{2}+\rho_{2}^{2}}\Big[\rho_{1}\hskip 0.50003pta_{s}-\rho_{2}\big(b_{s}\hskip-1.00006pt+\hskip-1.00006pta^{2}\hskip-1.00006pt+\hskip-1.00006ptb^{2}\big)\Big]\big(\varLambda\phi,\varPhi_{M}\big)+C(M)b\,U(t).

Injecting (
4.39
), (
4.40
), (
4.41
), (
4.43
), (
4.44
) into (
4.37
), applying the flux computation (
3.15
) and also the non-degeneracy (
4.6
), we obtain

\displaystyle\frac{1}{\rho_{1}^{2}+\rho_{2}^{2}}\Big[\rho_{1}\hskip 0.50003pta_{s}-\rho_{2}\big(b_{s}\hskip-1.00006pt+\hskip-1.00006pta^{2}\hskip-1.00006pt+\hskip-1.00006ptb^{2}\big)+\frac{2}{\left|\log b\right|}\big(\rho_{1}ab\hskip-1.00006pt-\hskip-1.00006pt\rho_{2}b^{2}\big)\Big]

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{(\varLambda\phi,\varPhi_{M})}\bigg[\Big(\sqrt{\log M}+C(M)\big(b\hskip-1.00006pt+\hskip-1.00006ptU(t)\big)\Big)\sqrt{\mathcal{E}_{4}}+\frac{b^{2}}{\left|\log b\right|^{2}}\bigg],

where the term involving
b+U(t)
is negligible due to the smallness of
b
and the absorption to the LHS. This yields the desired first line in (
4.35
). The other two identities can be obtained by similar computations.

\square

Remark 4.2

The modulation equations (
4.34
) build up a closed ODE system for the geometrical parameters
(\lambda,\varTheta,a,b)
, determining their asymptotic behavior as the rescaled time
s\to+\infty
, see (
5.89
). However, a direct integration on (
4.34
) can only gives

b(s)\sim\frac{1}{s},\quad a(s)\sim(\log s)^{2}+C_{1},\quad\varTheta(s)\leq\int_{s_{0}}^{s}|a|\,d\sigma+C_{2},

(4.45)

insufficient to ensure the smallness of
a(s)
and the convergence of the phase (
1.9
). To overcome this, a refined bound for
a(s)
will be derived in Section
6
.

5
Energy method

In this section, we consider the fourth order energy
\mathcal{E}_{4}
of
w
, and derive the mixed energy identity/Morawetz estimate, which is the core of our analysis. It actually helps us close the refined bounds in Proposition
4.1
. The overall strategy is called the energy method, see
[
34
]
for related arguments.

5.1
The energy identity

In this subsection, we study the evolution of
\mathcal{E}_{4}
by investigating its equivalent
\int|{\rm\hat{J}}_{\lambda}\mathbb{H_{\lambda}}W_{2}|^{2}
(see (
5.10
)). First, we define the suitable second derivative of
W
:

W_{2}={\rm\hat{J}}_{\lambda}\mathbb{H_{\lambda}}W.

(5.1)

Recalling (
4.25
), we let

w_{2}={\rm\hat{J}}{\it\mathbb{H}}w=\lambda^{2}W_{2}

(5.2)

be its equivalent acting on the rescaled variable
(s,y)
. Moreover, to ease the notation, we denote the rotation with respect to
\hat{w}
by

{\rm R}_{\hat{w}}=\hat{w}\wedge=\widehat{W}\wedge,

(5.3)

and as a consequence,

{\rm\hat{J}}_{\lambda}={\rm R}+{\rm R}_{\hat{w}}={\rm\hat{J}}.

(5.4)

Using (
5.4
), (
4.26
), we compute the equation for
W_{2}
:

\left\{\begin{aligned} \partial_{t}W_{2}&=-\rho_{1}{\rm\hat{J}}_{\lambda}\mathbb{H_{\lambda}}W_{2}+\rho_{2}{\rm\hat{J}}_{\lambda}\mathbb{H_{\lambda}}{\rm\hat{J}}_{\lambda}W_{2}-{\rm\hat{J}}_{\lambda}\mathbb{H_{\lambda}}F+{\rm R}\big[\partial_{t},\mathbb{H_{\lambda}}\big]W+\mathcal{Q}_{1},\\
\mathcal{Q}_{1}&=\partial_{t}\widehat{W}\wedge\mathbb{H_{\lambda}}W+{\rm R}_{\hat{w}}\big[\partial_{t},\mathbb{H_{\lambda}}\big]W.\end{aligned}\right.

(5.5)

In consideration of the quadratic nature of the third component, the dominate part of
W
is actually

W^{\perp}=-{\rm R}^{2}W=\big[\alpha_{\lambda},\beta_{\lambda},0\big]^{T},\quad W^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-2.26666pt}{\resizebox{868894}{445645}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.75pt}{$\displaystyle(3)$}}}}}{\raisebox{-2.26666pt}{\resizebox{868894}{445645}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.75pt}{$\textstyle(3)$}}}}}{\raisebox{-2.26665pt}{\resizebox{759247}{445645}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.22499pt}{$\scriptstyle(3)$}}}}}{\raisebox{-2.26666pt}{\resizebox{759248}{445645}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.875pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle(3)$}}}}}}=W-W^{\perp}=\gamma_{\lambda}\hskip 0.50003pte_{z}.

Similarly, we decompose
W_{2}
into

W_{2}^{0}={\rm R}\mathbb{H_{\lambda}}W^{\perp},\quad W_{2}^{1}=W_{2}-W_{2}^{0},

(5.6)

and also
w_{2}^{0}={\rm R}{\it\mathbb{H}}w^{\perp},w_{2}^{1}=w_{2}-w_{2}^{0}
accordingly. From (
4.26
),
W^{\perp}
satisfies the equation

\left\{\begin{aligned} \partial_{t}W^{\perp}&=-\rho_{1}W_{2}^{0}+\rho_{2}{\rm R}W_{2}^{0}-F^{\perp}+\mathcal{Q}_{2}^{1},\\
\mathcal{Q}_{2}^{1}&=\rho_{1}{\rm R}^{2}W_{2}^{1}-\rho_{2}{\rm R}^{2}{\rm R}_{\hat{w}}W_{2}^{0}-\rho_{2}{\rm R}^{2}{\rm\hat{J}}_{\lambda}W_{2}^{1},\end{aligned}\right.

(5.7)

while
W_{2}^{0}
satisfies the equation

\left\{\begin{aligned} \partial_{t}W_{2}^{0}&=-\rho_{1}{\rm R}\mathbb{H_{\lambda}}W_{2}^{0}-\rho_{2}\mathbb{H_{\lambda}}W_{2}^{0}-{\rm R}\mathbb{H_{\lambda}}F^{\perp}+\mathcal{Q}_{2}^{2},\\
\mathcal{Q}_{2}^{2}&={\rm R}\big[\partial_{t},\mathbb{H_{\lambda}}\big]W^{\perp}+{\rm R}\mathbb{H_{\lambda}}\mathcal{Q}_{2}^{1}.\end{aligned}\right.

(5.8)

We define two more functions, whose behaviors play importance roles on the forthcoming energy estimate:

G(t,r)=\frac{b(\varLambda V)_{\lambda}}{\lambda^{2}r^{2}},\quad L(t,r)=\frac{b(\varLambda Z)_{\lambda}}{\lambda^{2}r}.

(5.9)

As we shall see later,
G
appears in an unsigned quadratic term, and
L
, containing the information on the structure of
\mathbb{A},\mathbb{A^{\ast}}
, will be used to obtain the mixed energy identity. We now derive the plain energy identity at the level of
\mathcal{E}_{4}
.

Lemma 5.1
(Plain energy identity)

Under the above definitions, there holds

\displaystyle\frac{1}{2}\frac{d}{dt}\int\big|{\rm\hat{J}}_{\lambda}\mathbb{H_{\lambda}}W_{2}\big|^{2}=

\displaystyle-\rho_{1}\hskip-1.00006pt\int{\rm\hat{J}}_{\lambda}\mathbb{H_{\lambda}}W_{2}\cdot\big({\rm\hat{J}}_{\lambda}\mathbb{H_{\lambda}}\big)^{2}W_{2}

(5.10)

\displaystyle+\rho_{2}\hskip-1.00006pt\int{\rm\hat{J}}_{\lambda}\mathbb{H_{\lambda}}W_{2}\cdot\big({\rm\hat{J}}_{\lambda}\mathbb{H_{\lambda}}\big)^{2}{\rm\hat{J}}_{\lambda}W_{2}

\displaystyle+\int{\rm\hat{J}}_{\lambda}\mathbb{H_{\lambda}}W_{2}\cdot\big({\rm\hat{J}}_{\lambda}\mathbb{H_{\lambda}}\big)\mathcal{Q}_{1}-\hskip-1.00006pt\int{\rm\hat{J}}_{\lambda}\mathbb{H_{\lambda}}W_{2}\cdot\big({\rm\hat{J}}_{\lambda}\mathbb{H_{\lambda}}\big)^{2}F

\displaystyle+\mathcal{Q}_{3}+\hskip-1.00006pt\int{\rm R}\mathbb{H_{\lambda}}W_{2}^{0}\cdot\Big[-\mathbb{H_{\lambda}^{\perp}}\big(GW^{\perp}\big)+G{\rm R}W_{2}^{0}\Big],

where

\displaystyle\mathcal{Q}_{3}=

\displaystyle-\Big(b+\frac{\lambda_{s}}{\lambda}\Big)\int{\rm R}\mathbb{H_{\lambda}}W_{2}^{0}\cdot\bigg[-\mathbb{H_{\lambda}^{\perp}}\bigg(\frac{(\varLambda V)_{\lambda}}{\lambda^{2}r^{2}}W^{\perp}\bigg)+\frac{(\varLambda V)_{\lambda}}{\lambda^{2}r^{2}}{\rm R}W_{2}^{0}\,\bigg]

(5.11)

\displaystyle+\int{\rm R}\mathbb{H_{\lambda}}W_{2}^{0}\cdot\bigg({\rm R}\mathbb{H_{\lambda}}{\rm R}\big[\partial_{t},\mathbb{H_{\lambda}}\big]\gamma\,e_{z}+{\rm R}_{\hat{w}}\mathbb{H_{\lambda}}{\rm R}\big[\partial_{t},\mathbb{H_{\lambda}}\big]W

\displaystyle+\partial_{t}\widehat{W}\wedge\mathbb{H_{\lambda}}W_{2}^{0}+{\rm R}_{\hat{w}}\big[\partial_{t},\mathbb{H_{\lambda}}\big]W_{2}^{0}+\big[\partial_{t},{\rm\hat{J}}_{\lambda}\mathbb{H_{\lambda}}\big]W_{2}^{1}\bigg)

\displaystyle+\int{\rm R}\mathbb{H_{\lambda}}W_{2}^{1}\cdot\Big({\rm\hat{J}}_{\lambda}\mathbb{H_{\lambda}}{\rm R}\big[\partial_{t},\mathbb{H_{\lambda}}\big]W+\big[\partial_{t},{\rm\hat{J}}_{\lambda}\mathbb{H_{\lambda}}\big]W_{2}\Big)

\displaystyle+\int{\rm R}_{\hat{w}}\mathbb{H_{\lambda}}W_{2}\cdot\Big({\rm\hat{J}}_{\lambda}\mathbb{H_{\lambda}}{\rm R}\big[\partial_{t},\mathbb{H_{\lambda}}\big]W+\big[\partial_{t},{\rm\hat{J}}_{\lambda}\mathbb{H_{\lambda}}\big]W_{2}\Big).

Remark 5.1

(i)

\mathcal{Q}_{3}
will be proved to be small errors in Proposition
5.1
.

(ii)
The majority of the RHS of (
5.10
) can be estimated by the interpolation bounds listed in Lemma
A
directly, but the last quadratic term is unsigned, and can only be controlled via (
5.39
), (
A.3
), (
A.29
), (
A.31
), by the following rough bound

\displaystyle\int{\rm R}\mathbb{H_{\lambda}}W_{2}^{0}\cdot\Big[-\mathbb{H_{\lambda}^{\perp}}\big(GW^{\perp}\big)+G{\rm R}W_{2}^{0}\,\Big]

(5.12)

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\frac{b}{\lambda^{8}}\bigg(\int\big|{\rm R}{\it\mathbb{H}}w_{2}^{0}\big|^{2}\bigg)^{\frac{1}{2}}\bigg(\int\Big|\mathbb{H^{\perp}}\Big(\frac{w^{\perp}}{1+y^{4}}\Big)\Big|^{2}+\frac{|w_{2}^{0}|^{2}}{1+y^{8}}\bigg)^{\frac{1}{2}}\lesssim\frac{b}{\lambda^{8}}\bigg(\mathcal{E}_{4}+\frac{b^{4}}{\left|\log b\right|^{2}}\bigg).

It is of the same size as the leading terms in (
5.10
) (the first two lines on the RHS), and the implicit constant in (
5.12
) will deteriorate the forthcoming energy estimate. To treat this, we introduce the Morawetz type formula in the next subsection.

Proof of Lemma
5.1
.

Injecting (
5.5
) into the time derivative of
\int|{\rm\hat{J}}_{\lambda}\mathbb{H_{\lambda}}W_{2}|^{2}
, we obtain an analogue of the RHS of (
5.10
). The last line of the latter is given by (
5.13
) as below, different from that of (
5.10
). Thus it remains to do some algebraic computation. Using (
5.4
), (
5.6
), we compute

\displaystyle\int{\rm\hat{J}}_{\lambda}\mathbb{H_{\lambda}}W_{2}\cdot\Big({\rm\hat{J}}_{\lambda}\mathbb{H_{\lambda}}{\rm R}\big[\partial_{t},\mathbb{H_{\lambda}}\big]W+\big[\partial_{t},{\rm\hat{J}}_{\lambda}\mathbb{H_{\lambda}}\big]W_{2}\Big)

(5.13)

\displaystyle=\int{\rm R}\mathbb{H_{\lambda}}W_{2}^{0}\cdot\Big({\rm\hat{J}}_{\lambda}\mathbb{H_{\lambda}}{\rm R}\big[\partial_{t},\mathbb{H_{\lambda}}\big]W+\big[\partial_{t},{\rm\hat{J}}_{\lambda}\mathbb{H_{\lambda}}\big]W_{2}\Big)

\displaystyle+\int{\rm R}\mathbb{H_{\lambda}}W_{2}^{1}\cdot\Big({\rm\hat{J}}_{\lambda}\mathbb{H_{\lambda}}{\rm R}\big[\partial_{t},\mathbb{H_{\lambda}}\big]W+\big[\partial_{t},{\rm\hat{J}}_{\lambda}\mathbb{H_{\lambda}}\big]W_{2}\Big)

\displaystyle+\int{\rm R}_{\hat{w}}{\it\mathbb{H}}W_{2}\cdot\Big({\rm\hat{J}}_{\lambda}\mathbb{H_{\lambda}}{\rm R}\big[\partial_{t},\mathbb{H_{\lambda}}\big]W+\big[\partial_{t},{\rm\hat{J}}_{\lambda}\mathbb{H_{\lambda}}\big]W_{2}\Big).

The first term on the RHS of (
5.13
) can be further splited into

\displaystyle\int{\rm R}\mathbb{H_{\lambda}}W_{2}^{0}\cdot\Big({\rm\hat{J}}_{\lambda}\mathbb{H_{\lambda}}{\rm R}\big[\partial_{t},\mathbb{H_{\lambda}}\big]W+\big[\partial_{t},{\rm\hat{J}}_{\lambda}\mathbb{H_{\lambda}}\big]W_{2}\Big)

(5.14)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\int{\rm R}\mathbb{H_{\lambda}}W_{2}^{0}\cdot\Big({\rm R}\mathbb{H_{\lambda}}{\rm R}\big[\partial_{t},\mathbb{H_{\lambda}}\big]W+\big[\partial_{t},{\rm\hat{J}}_{\lambda}\mathbb{H_{\lambda}}\big]W_{2}^{0}\Big)

\displaystyle+\int{\rm R}\mathbb{H_{\lambda}}W_{2}^{0}\cdot\Big({\rm R}_{\hat{w}}\mathbb{H_{\lambda}}{\rm R}\big[\partial_{t},\mathbb{H_{\lambda}}\big]W+\big[\partial_{t},{\rm\hat{J}}_{\lambda}\mathbb{H_{\lambda}}\big]W_{2}^{1}\Big),

where the involved commutators are

\big[\partial_{t},{\rm\hat{J}}_{\lambda}\mathbb{H_{\lambda}}\big]W={\rm R}\big[\partial_{t},\mathbb{H_{\lambda}}\big]W+\partial_{t}\widehat{W}\wedge\mathbb{H_{\lambda}}W+{\rm R}_{\hat{w}}\big[\partial_{t},\mathbb{H_{\lambda}}\big]W,

and

\displaystyle\big[\partial_{t},\mathbb{H_{\lambda}}\big]W

\displaystyle=-\hskip-1.00006pt\frac{\lambda_{s}}{\lambda^{3}}\Bigg\{\bigg[\frac{(\varLambda V)_{\lambda}}{r^{2}}\alpha_{\lambda}-\frac{2}{\lambda}\Big(\hskip-1.00006pt1\hskip-1.00006pt\hskip-1.00006pt+\hskip-1.00006pt\hskip-1.00006ptZ\hskip-1.00006pt\hskip-1.00006pt+\hskip-1.00006pt\hskip-1.00006pt\varLambda Z\hskip-1.00006pt\Big)_{\lambda}\partial_{r}\gamma_{\lambda}\,\bigg]e_{x}+\bigg[\frac{(\varLambda V)_{\lambda}}{r^{2}}\beta_{\lambda}\bigg]e_{y}

(5.15)

\displaystyle+\hskip-1.00006pt\bigg[\frac{2}{\lambda}\Big(\hskip-1.00006pt1\hskip-1.00006pt\hskip-1.00006pt+\hskip-1.00006pt\hskip-1.00006ptZ\hskip-1.00006pt\hskip-1.00006pt+\hskip-1.00006pt\hskip-1.00006pt\varLambda Z\hskip-1.00006pt\Big)_{\lambda}\partial_{r}\alpha_{\lambda}+\hskip-1.00006pt\frac{2}{\lambda r}\Big(\hskip-1.00006ptZ\hskip-1.00006pt\hskip-1.00006pt+\hskip-1.00006pt\hskip-1.00006ptZ^{2}\hskip-1.00006pt\hskip-1.00006pt+\hskip-1.00006pt\hskip-1.00006pt\varLambda Z\big(1\hskip-1.00006pt+\hskip-1.00006pt2Z\big)\hskip-1.00006pt\Big)_{\lambda}\hskip-1.00006pt\hskip-1.00006pt\alpha_{\lambda}\hskip-1.00006pt\bigg]e_{z}\hskip-1.00006pt\Bigg\}.

From (
4.33
), (
5.15
), we have

\left\{\begin{aligned} &\,{\rm R}\mathbb{H_{\lambda}}{\rm R}\big[\partial_{t},\mathbb{H_{\lambda}}\big]W^{\perp}=\frac{\lambda_{s}}{\lambda}\,\mathbb{H_{\lambda}^{\perp}}\bigg(\frac{(\varLambda V)_{\lambda}}{\lambda^{2}r^{2}}W^{\perp}\bigg)\approx-\mathbb{H_{\lambda}^{\perp}}\big(GW^{\perp}\big),\\
&\,{\rm R}\big[\partial_{t},\mathbb{H_{\lambda}}\big]W_{2}^{0}=-\frac{\lambda_{s}}{\lambda}\,\frac{(\varLambda V)_{\lambda}}{\lambda^{2}r^{2}}{\rm R}W_{2}^{0}\approx G{\rm R}W_{2}^{0}.\end{aligned}\right.

(5.16)

Using these, we rewrite the first term on the RHS of (
5.14
) as

\displaystyle\int{\rm R}{\it\mathbb{H}}_{\lambda}W_{2}^{0}\cdot\Big({\rm R}\mathbb{H_{\lambda}}{\rm R}\big[\partial_{t},\mathbb{H_{\lambda}}\big]W+\big[\partial_{t},{\rm\hat{J}}_{\lambda}\mathbb{H_{\lambda}}\big]W_{2}^{0}\Big)

(5.17)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\int{\rm R}\mathbb{H_{\lambda}}W_{2}^{0}\cdot\Big[-\mathbb{H_{\lambda}^{\perp}}\big(GW^{\perp}\big)+G{\rm R}W_{2}^{0}\,\Big]

\displaystyle-\Big(b+\frac{\lambda_{s}}{\lambda}\Big)\int{\rm R}\mathbb{H_{\lambda}}W_{2}^{0}\cdot\bigg[-\mathbb{H_{\lambda}^{\perp}}\bigg(\frac{(\varLambda V)_{\lambda}}{\lambda^{2}r^{2}}W^{\perp}\bigg)+\frac{(\varLambda V)_{\lambda}}{\lambda^{2}r^{2}}{\rm R}W_{2}^{0}\bigg]

\displaystyle+\int{\rm R}\mathbb{H_{\lambda}}W_{2}^{0}\cdot\Big({\rm R}\mathbb{H_{\lambda}}{\rm R}\big[\partial_{t},\mathbb{H_{\lambda}}\big]\gamma\,e_{z}+\partial_{t}\widehat{W}\wedge\mathbb{H_{\lambda}}W_{2}^{0}+{\rm R}_{\hat{w}}\big[\partial_{t},\mathbb{H_{\lambda}}\big]W_{2}^{0}\Big).

Inserting this into (
5.14
) yields the last line of (
5.10
). By collecting the rest, we obtain (
5.11
), and thus complete the proof.

\square

5.2
Morewatz correction

The aim of this subsection is to modify the plain energy (
5.10
), by adding an extra Morawetz term to the energy functional. The resulting formula is called the mixed energy/Morawetz identity, which, containing no uncontrollable terms, is appropriate to close the bootstrap. It is notable that the newly-added Morawetz term heavily rely on the coefficients
\rho_{1},\rho_{2}
in (
1.1
).

Indeed, to characterize the relative size of the absolute values of
\rho_{1},\rho_{2}
, we define their ratio by

\rho=\bigg(\frac{\rho_{1}}{\rho_{2}}\bigg)^{2}.

Recall that
\rho_{1}\in\mathbb{R}
and
\rho_{2}>0
, we see
\rho
is well-defined and takes value in
[0,+\infty)
. We further introduce the following conditional parameters depending on
\rho
:

k_{1}(\rho)=\begin{cases}1,\quad\rho\geq 1,\\
0,\quad\rho<1,\end{cases}\quad k_{2}(\rho)=1-k_{1}(\rho),\quad\Delta k(\rho)=k_{1}(\rho)-k_{2}(\rho).

(5.18)

It is easy to see
k_{2}\in\{0,1\}
and
\Delta k\in\{-1,1\}
. Now we introduce the extra term, and derive the Morawetz type formula. The results are collected in the following lemma.

Lemma 5.2
(Morawetz correction)

The desired Morawetz term is

\displaystyle\mathbf{M}(t)=

\displaystyle c_{1}\hskip-1.00006pt\int\mathbb{H_{\lambda}}W_{2}^{0}\cdot GW^{\perp}+c_{2}\hskip-1.00006pt\int{\rm R}\mathbb{A_{\lambda}}W_{2}^{0}\cdot LW_{2}^{0}

(5.19)

\displaystyle+c_{3}\hskip-1.00006pt\int{\rm R}\mathbb{H_{\lambda}}W_{2}^{0}\cdot GW^{\perp}-c_{4}\hskip-1.00006pt\int\mathbb{A_{\lambda}}W_{2}^{0}\cdot LW_{2}^{0},

where the coefficient in front of the integrals are

\displaystyle\big(c_{1},c_{2},

\displaystyle c_{3},c_{4}\big)=

(5.20)

\displaystyle\bigg(\frac{\rho_{1}}{\rho_{1}^{2}+\rho_{2}^{2}},\,\frac{2\rho_{1}(\rho_{1}^{2}-\rho_{2}^{2})}{(\rho_{1}^{2}+\rho_{2}^{2})(\Delta k\rho_{1}^{2}+\rho_{2}^{2})},\,\frac{\rho_{2}}{\rho_{1}^{2}+\rho_{2}^{2}},\,\frac{2\rho_{1}^{2}\rho_{2}(1+\Delta k)}{(\rho_{1}^{2}+\rho_{2}^{2})(\Delta k\rho_{1}^{2}+\rho_{2}^{2})}\bigg).

Moreover, there holds the boundedness

\big|\mathbf{M}(t)\big|\lesssim\frac{\delta(b^{\ast})}{\lambda^{6}}\bigg(\mathcal{E}_{4}+\frac{b^{4}}{\left|\log b\right|^{2}}\bigg).

(5.21)

Its time derivative (Morawetz type identity) is given by

\displaystyle\frac{d}{dt}\,\mathbf{M}(t)

\displaystyle=\int{\rm R}\mathbb{H_{\lambda}}W_{2}^{0}\cdot\Big[-\mathbb{H_{\lambda}^{\perp}}\big(GW^{\perp}\big)+G{\rm R}W_{2}^{0}\,\Big]+\frac{b}{\lambda^{8}}\,O\bigg(\frac{\mathcal{E}_{4}}{\sqrt{\log M}}+\frac{b^{4}}{\left|\log b\right|^{2}}\bigg)

(5.22)

\displaystyle-2\rho_{1}c_{2}k_{1}\int\mathbb{H_{\lambda}}W_{2}^{0}\cdot L\mathbb{A_{\lambda}}W_{2}^{0}+2\rho_{1}c_{2}k_{2}\int\mathbb{A_{\lambda}}\mathbb{H_{\lambda}}W_{2}^{0}\cdot LW_{2}^{0}.

Remark 5.2

(i)
The first term on the RHS of (
5.22
) is just the uncontrollable quadratic term (
5.12
).

(ii)
The coefficients
c_{i}
in (
5.20
) are well-defined. Indeed, by the positivity of the Gilbert damping
\rho_{2}>0
, we see
\rho_{1}^{2}+\rho_{2}^{2}>0
. Moreover, the vanishing
\Delta k\rho_{1}^{2}+\rho_{2}^{2}=0
implies
\Delta k=-1
and
\rho_{1}^{2}=\rho_{2}^{2}
, from which
\rho=\rho_{1}^{2}/\rho_{2}^{2}=1
, and thus
\Delta k=1
, contradiction. This ensures the denominators in the definitions of
c_{i}
are non-zero.

Assume Lemma
5.2
holds for now. We subtract (
5.22
) from the plain energy (
5.10
), and obtain the following mixed energy/Morawetz identity

\displaystyle\frac{d}{dt}\bigg\{\frac{1}{2}\int\big|{\rm\hat{J}}_{\lambda}\mathbb{H_{\lambda}}W_{2}\big|^{2}-\mathbf{M}(t)\bigg\}

(5.23)

\displaystyle=-\rho_{1}\int{\rm\hat{J}}_{\lambda}\mathbb{H_{\lambda}}W_{2}\cdot\big({\rm\hat{J}}_{\lambda}\mathbb{H_{\lambda}}\big)^{2}W_{2}+\rho_{2}\int{\rm\hat{J}}_{\lambda}\mathbb{H_{\lambda}}W_{2}\cdot\big({\rm\hat{J}}_{\lambda}\mathbb{H_{\lambda}}\big)^{2}{\rm\hat{J}}_{\lambda}W_{2}

\displaystyle+2\rho_{1}c_{2}k_{1}\int\mathbb{H_{\lambda}}W_{2}^{0}\cdot L\mathbb{A_{\lambda}}W_{2}^{0}-2\rho_{1}c_{2}k_{2}\int\mathbb{A_{\lambda}}\mathbb{H_{\lambda}}W_{2}^{0}\cdot LW_{2}^{0}

\displaystyle+\mathcal{Q}_{3}+\int{\rm\hat{J}}_{\lambda}\mathbb{H_{\lambda}}W_{2}\cdot\big({\rm\hat{J}}_{\lambda}\mathbb{H_{\lambda}}\big)\mathcal{Q}_{1}-\int{\rm\hat{J}}_{\lambda}\mathbb{H_{\lambda}}W_{2}\cdot\big({\rm\hat{J}}_{\lambda}\mathbb{H_{\lambda}}\big)^{2}F

\displaystyle+\frac{b}{\lambda^{8}}\,O\bigg(\frac{\mathcal{E}_{4}}{\sqrt{\log M}}+\frac{b^{4}}{\left|\log b\right|^{2}}\bigg).

Note that the uncontrollable term has been cancelled. The identity (
5.23
) is the key to derive the mixed energy/Morawetz estimate. Now before giving a proof for Lemma
5.2
, we introduce the following brief lemma on the structure of
\mathbb{A},\mathbb{A^{\ast}}
, and their relation with the functions
G,L
(
5.9
).

Lemma 5.3
(Structure of
\mathbb{A},\mathbb{A^{\ast}}
)

There holds the identities

\displaystyle\int\mathbb{A_{\lambda}}\mathbb{H_{\lambda}}W_{2}^{0}\cdot LW_{2}^{0}+\int\mathbb{H_{\lambda}}W_{2}^{0}\cdot L\mathbb{A_{\lambda}}W_{2}^{0}=\int\mathbb{H_{\lambda}}W_{2}^{0}\cdot GW_{2}^{0},

(5.24)

\displaystyle\int{\rm R}\mathbb{A_{\lambda}}\mathbb{H_{\lambda}}W_{2}^{0}\cdot LW_{2}^{0}=\int{\rm R}\mathbb{H_{\lambda}}W_{2}^{0}\cdot GW_{2}^{0},

(5.25)

\displaystyle\int{\rm R}\mathbb{H_{\lambda}}W_{2}^{0}\cdot L\mathbb{A_{\lambda}}W_{2}^{0}=0,

(5.26)

and the non-positivity

\int\mathbb{H_{\lambda}}W_{2}^{0}\cdot L\mathbb{A_{\lambda}}W_{2}^{0}\leq 0.

(5.27)

Proof of Lemma
5.3
.

Recalling the function
V
(
2.18
), we see
\varLambda V=\varLambda(\varLambda Z+Z^{2})
. Applying (
4.31
), we compute for any radially symmetric function
f(r)
with
r=\lambda y
that

\displaystyle\mathbb{A_{\lambda}^{\ast}}(Lf)

\displaystyle+L\mathbb{A_{\lambda}}f=\bigg(\partial_{r}L+\frac{2Z_{\lambda}+1}{r}L\bigg)f

(5.28)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{b}{\lambda^{4}}\bigg[\partial_{y}\bigg(\frac{\varLambda Z}{y}\bigg)+\frac{(2Z+1)\varLambda Z}{y^{2}}\bigg]\,f=\,\frac{b\big(\varLambda(\varLambda Z+Z^{2})\big)(y)}{\lambda^{4}y^{2}}\,f=Gf.

This together with the commutativity
\mathbb{A_{\lambda}}{\rm R}={\rm R}\mathbb{A_{\lambda}}
leads to

\displaystyle\int\mathbb{A_{\lambda}}\mathbb{H_{\lambda}}W_{2}^{0}\cdot LW_{2}^{0}

\displaystyle=-\int\mathbb{H_{\lambda}}W_{2}^{0}\cdot L\mathbb{A_{\lambda}}W_{2}^{0}+\int\mathbb{H_{\lambda}}W_{2}^{0}\cdot GW_{2}^{0},

(5.29)

\displaystyle\int{\rm R}\mathbb{A_{\lambda}}\mathbb{H_{\lambda}}W_{2}^{0}\cdot LW_{2}^{0}

\displaystyle=-\int{\rm R}\mathbb{H_{\lambda}}W_{2}^{0}\cdot L\mathbb{A_{\lambda}}W_{2}^{0}+\int{\rm R}\mathbb{H_{\lambda}}W_{2}^{0}\cdot GW_{2}^{0},

(5.30)

where (
5.29
) is just (
5.24
). Moreover, by a similar computation on
\mathbb{A_{\lambda}}(Lf)+L\mathbb{A_{\lambda}^{\ast}}f
as (
5.28
), and the fact
L(t,r)<0
for any
r>0
, we have

\displaystyle\int\mathbb{H_{\lambda}}W_{2}^{0}\cdot L\mathbb{A_{\lambda}}W_{2}^{0}=\int\mathbb{A_{\lambda}}W_{2}^{0}\cdot\mathbb{A_{\lambda}}\big(L\mathbb{A_{\lambda}}W_{2}^{0}\big)

(5.31)

\displaystyle=\int\mathbb{A_{\lambda}}W_{2}^{0}\cdot\bigg[-L\mathbb{A_{\lambda}^{\ast}}\mathbb{A_{\lambda}}W_{2}^{0}+\bigg(\frac{2Z_{\lambda}+1}{r}L-\partial_{r}L\bigg)\mathbb{A_{\lambda}}W_{2}^{0}\bigg]

\displaystyle=\int\mathbb{A_{\lambda}}W_{2}^{0}\cdot\bigg(-L\mathbb{H_{\lambda}}W_{2}^{0}+\frac{2L}{r}\mathbb{A_{\lambda}}W_{2}^{0}\bigg)=\int\frac{L}{r}\,\big|\mathbb{A_{\lambda}}W_{2}^{0}\big|^{2}\leq 0,

which is the non-positivity (
5.27
). Note here we have used
\mathbb{H_{\lambda}}W_{2}^{0}\cdot X=\mathbb{H_{\lambda}^{\perp}}W_{2}^{0}\cdot X
, for any vector
X
under Frenet basis with the third component
X^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-2.26666pt}{\resizebox{868894}{445645}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.75pt}{$\displaystyle(3)$}}}}}{\raisebox{-2.26666pt}{\resizebox{868894}{445645}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.75pt}{$\textstyle(3)$}}}}}{\raisebox{-2.26665pt}{\resizebox{759247}{445645}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.22499pt}{$\scriptstyle(3)$}}}}}{\raisebox{-2.26666pt}{\resizebox{759248}{445645}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.875pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle(3)$}}}}}}=0
. An analogous argument can be formulated to show the vanishing

\int{\rm R}\mathbb{H_{\lambda}}W_{2}^{0}\cdot L\mathbb{A_{\lambda}}W_{2}^{0}=\int\frac{L}{r}\,{\rm R}\mathbb{A_{\lambda}}W_{2}^{0}\cdot\mathbb{A_{\lambda}}W_{2}^{0}=0,

which is (
5.26
). Combining this with (
5.30
) yields (
5.25
). This completes the proof.

\square

The structural bonus illutrated in Lemma
5.3
will be found essential for the following analysis. We now use it to prove Lemma
5.2
.

Proof of Lemma
5.2
.

We first show the boundedness (
5.21
), and then prove (
5.22
) by computing the time derivatives of each term in (
5.19
), formulating the uncontrollable term, and finally estimating the errors via Appendix
A
.

Step 1
.
Control of
\mathbf{M}(t)
. From the lossy logarithmic bound (
A.5
), we have

\displaystyle\big|\mathbf{M}(t)\big|

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{b}{\lambda^{6}}\bigg(\int|{\it\mathbb{H}}w_{2}^{0}|\,\frac{|w^{\perp}|}{1+y^{4}}+\int|\mathbb{A}w_{2}^{0}|\,\frac{|w_{2}^{0}|}{1+y^{3}}\bigg)

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{b}{\lambda^{6}}\bigg(\int|{\it\mathbb{H}}w_{2}^{0}|^{2}+\int\frac{|\mathbb{A}w_{2}^{0}|^{2}}{y^{2}(1+y^{2})}\bigg)^{\frac{1}{2}}\bigg(\int\frac{|w^{\perp}|^{2}}{1+y^{8}}+\int\frac{1+\left|\log y\right|^{2}}{1+y^{4}}|w_{2}^{0}|^{2}\bigg)^{\frac{1}{2}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{b}{\lambda^{6}}\left|\log b\right|^{C}\bigg(\mathcal{E}_{4}+\frac{b^{4}}{\left|\log b\right|^{2}}\bigg)\lesssim\frac{\delta(b^{\ast})}{\lambda^{6}}\bigg(\mathcal{E}_{4}+\frac{b^{4}}{\left|\log b\right|^{2}}\bigg).

Step 2
.
Computations on time derivatives
. For the first integral in (
5.19
), we apply the equations (
5.7
), (
5.8
) to compute

\displaystyle\partial_{t}\int\mathbb{H_{\lambda}}W_{2}^{0}\cdot GW^{\perp}=\partial_{t}\int W_{2}^{0}\cdot\mathbb{H_{\lambda}}\big(GW^{\perp}\big)

(5.32)

\displaystyle=-\rho_{1}\int{\rm R}\mathbb{H_{\lambda}}W_{2}^{0}\cdot\mathbb{H_{\lambda}^{\perp}}\big(GW^{\perp}\big)-\rho_{2}\int\mathbb{H_{\lambda}}W_{2}^{0}\cdot\mathbb{H_{\lambda}^{\perp}}\big(GW^{\perp}\big)

\displaystyle-\rho_{1}\int\mathbb{H_{\lambda}}W_{2}^{0}\cdot GW_{2}^{0}-\rho_{2}\int{\rm R}\mathbb{H_{\lambda}}W_{2}^{0}\cdot GW_{2}^{0}+\int W_{2}^{0}\cdot\big[\partial_{t},\mathbb{H_{\lambda}}\big]\big(GW^{\perp}\big)

\displaystyle+\int\mathbb{H_{\lambda}}W_{2}^{0}\cdot\big(\partial_{t}G\big)W^{\perp}+\int\mathbb{H_{\lambda}}\mathcal{Q}_{2}^{2}\cdot GW^{\perp}+\int\mathbb{H_{\lambda}}W_{2}^{0}\cdot G\mathcal{Q}_{2}^{1}

\displaystyle-\int{\rm R}\mathbb{H_{\lambda}}F^{\perp}\cdot\mathbb{H_{\lambda}}\big(GW^{\perp}\big)-\int\mathbb{H_{\lambda}}W_{2}^{0}\cdot GF^{\perp}.

The terms involving the commutator
[\partial_{t},\mathbb{H_{\lambda}}]
and
\partial_{t}G
are anticipated tiny errors, so are the ones with
F^{\perp},\mathcal{Q}_{2}^{1}
and
\mathcal{Q}_{2}^{2}
. The attention should be focused on the quadratic terms in
W
with the coefficients
\rho_{1},\rho_{2}
. The third term in (
5.19
) can be treated similarly

\displaystyle\partial_{t}\int{\rm R}\mathbb{H_{\lambda}}W_{2}^{0}\cdot GW^{\perp}=\partial_{t}\int{\rm R}W_{2}^{0}\cdot\mathbb{H_{\lambda}}\big(GW^{\perp}\big)

(5.33)

\displaystyle=\rho_{1}\int\mathbb{H_{\lambda}}W_{2}^{0}\cdot\mathbb{H_{\lambda}^{\perp}}\big(GW^{\perp}\big)-\rho_{2}\int{\rm R}\mathbb{H_{\lambda}}W_{2}^{0}\cdot\mathbb{H_{\lambda}^{\perp}}\big(GW^{\perp}\big)

\displaystyle-\rho_{1}\int{\rm R}\mathbb{H_{\lambda}}W_{2}^{0}\cdot GW_{2}^{0}+\rho_{2}\int\mathbb{H_{\lambda}}W_{2}^{0}\cdot GW_{2}^{0}+\int{\rm R}W_{2}^{0}\cdot\big[\partial_{t},\mathbb{H_{\lambda}}\big]\big(GW^{\perp}\big)

\displaystyle+\int{\rm R}\mathbb{H_{\lambda}}W_{2}^{0}\cdot(\partial_{t}G)W^{\perp}+\int{\rm R}\mathbb{H_{\lambda}}\mathcal{Q}_{2}^{2}\cdot GW^{\perp}+\int{\rm R}\mathbb{H_{\lambda}}W_{2}^{0}\cdot G\mathcal{Q}_{2}^{1}

\displaystyle+\int\mathbb{H_{\lambda}}F^{\perp}\cdot\mathbb{H_{\lambda}}\big(GW^{\perp}\big)-\int{\rm R}\mathbb{H_{\lambda}}W_{2}^{0}\cdot GF^{\perp}.

Next, we treat the second and the last integrals in (
5.19
). Straightforward computations from (
5.7
), (
5.8
) give

\displaystyle\partial_{t}\int{\rm R}\mathbb{A_{\lambda}}W_{2}^{0}\cdot LW_{2}^{0}

(5.34)

\displaystyle=\rho_{1}\int\mathbb{A_{\lambda}}\mathbb{H_{\lambda}}W_{2}^{0}\cdot LW_{2}^{0}-\rho_{2}\int{\rm R}\mathbb{A_{\lambda}}\mathbb{H_{\lambda}}W_{2}^{0}\cdot LW_{2}^{0}

\displaystyle-\rho_{1}\int\mathbb{A_{\lambda}}W_{2}^{0}\cdot L\mathbb{H_{\lambda}}W_{2}^{0}-\rho_{2}\int{\rm R}\mathbb{A_{\lambda}}W_{2}^{0}\cdot L\mathbb{H_{\lambda}}W_{2}^{0}

\displaystyle+\int{\rm R}\big[\partial_{t},\mathbb{A_{\lambda}}\big]W_{2}^{0}\cdot LW_{2}^{0}+\int{\rm R}\mathbb{A_{\lambda}}W_{2}^{0}\cdot(\partial_{t}L)W_{2}^{0}

\displaystyle+\int{\rm R}\mathbb{A_{\lambda}}\mathcal{Q}_{2}^{2}\cdot LW_{2}^{0}+\int{\rm R}\mathbb{A_{\lambda}}W_{2}^{0}\cdot L\mathcal{Q}_{2}^{2}

\displaystyle+\int\mathbb{A_{\lambda}}\mathbb{H_{\lambda}}F^{\perp}\cdot LW_{2}^{0}-\int\mathbb{H_{\lambda}}F^{\perp}\cdot L\mathbb{A_{\lambda}}W_{2}^{0},

where from the vanishing (
5.26
) and the identity (
5.25
), the first two lines on the RHS of (
5.34
) are actually

\displaystyle\rho_{1}\bigg(\int\mathbb{A_{\lambda}}\mathbb{H_{\lambda}}W_{2}^{0}\cdot LW_{2}^{0}-\int\mathbb{H_{\lambda}}W_{2}^{0}\cdot L\mathbb{A_{\lambda}}W_{2}^{0}\bigg)-\rho_{2}\int{\rm R}\mathbb{H_{\lambda}}W_{2}^{0}\cdot GW_{2}^{0}.

We rewrite the expression in the big parenthesis by
1=k_{1}+k_{2}
, separating it into two identical formulas, and then apply (
5.24
) twice on each of them:

\displaystyle\rho_{1}\bigg(\int\mathbb{A_{\lambda}}\mathbb{H_{\lambda}}W_{2}^{0}\cdot LW_{2}^{0}-\int\mathbb{H_{\lambda}}W_{2}^{0}\cdot L\mathbb{A_{\lambda}}W_{2}^{0}\bigg)

\displaystyle=\rho_{1}k_{1}\bigg(\int\mathbb{H_{\lambda}}W_{2}^{0}\cdot GW_{2}^{0}-2\int\mathbb{H_{\lambda}}W_{2}^{0}\cdot L\mathbb{A_{\lambda}}W_{2}^{0}\bigg)

\displaystyle+\rho_{1}k_{2}\bigg(\hskip-1.00006pt-\hskip-1.00006pt\int\mathbb{H_{\lambda}}W_{2}^{0}\cdot GW_{2}^{0}+2\int\mathbb{A_{\lambda}}\mathbb{H_{\lambda}}W_{2}^{0}\cdot LW_{2}^{0}\bigg).

Now by
\Delta k=k_{1}-k_{2}
, we gather the above integrals to get

\displaystyle\partial_{t}\int{\rm R}\mathbb{A_{\lambda}}W_{2}^{0}\cdot LW_{2}^{0}

(5.35)

\displaystyle=\Delta k\rho_{1}\int\mathbb{H_{\lambda}}W_{2}^{0}\cdot GW_{2}^{0}-\rho_{2}\int{\rm R}\mathbb{H_{\lambda}}W_{2}^{0}\cdot GW_{2}^{0}+2\rho_{1}k_{2}\int\mathbb{A_{\lambda}}\mathbb{H_{\lambda}}W_{2}^{0}\cdot LW_{2}^{0}

\displaystyle-2\rho_{1}k_{1}\int\mathbb{H_{\lambda}}W_{2}^{0}\cdot L\mathbb{A_{\lambda}}W_{2}^{0}+\int{\rm R}\big[\partial_{t},\mathbb{A_{\lambda}}\big]W_{2}^{0}\cdot LW_{2}^{0}

\displaystyle+\int{\rm R}\mathbb{A_{\lambda}}W_{2}^{0}\cdot(\partial_{t}L)W_{2}^{0}+\int{\rm R}\mathbb{A_{\lambda}}\mathcal{Q}_{2}^{2}\cdot LW_{2}^{0}+\int{\rm R}\mathbb{A_{\lambda}}W_{2}^{0}\cdot L\mathcal{Q}_{2}^{2}

\displaystyle+\int\mathbb{A_{\lambda}}\mathbb{H_{\lambda}}F^{\perp}\cdot LW_{2}^{0}-\int\mathbb{H_{\lambda}}F^{\perp}\cdot L\mathbb{A_{\lambda}}W_{2}^{0}.

Finally, for the last term of (
5.19
), we repeat the analysis done for (
5.35
) using Lemma
5.3
to obtain

\displaystyle\partial_{t}\int\mathbb{A_{\lambda}}W_{2}^{0}\cdot LW_{2}^{0}

(5.36)

\displaystyle=-\rho_{1}\int{\rm R}\mathbb{H_{\lambda}}W_{2}^{0}\cdot GW_{2}^{0}-\rho_{2}\int\mathbb{H_{\lambda}}W_{2}^{0}\cdot GW_{2}^{0}

\displaystyle+\int\big[\partial_{t},\mathbb{A_{\lambda}}\big]W_{2}^{0}\cdot LW_{2}^{0}+\int\mathbb{A_{\lambda}}W_{2}^{0}\cdot(\partial_{t}L)W_{2}^{0}+\int\mathbb{A_{\lambda}}\mathcal{Q}_{2}^{2}\cdot LW_{2}^{0}

\displaystyle+\int\mathbb{A_{\lambda}}W_{2}^{0}\cdot L\mathcal{Q}_{2}^{2}-\int{\rm R}\mathbb{A_{\lambda}}\mathbb{H_{\lambda}}F^{\perp}\cdot LW_{2}^{0}-\int{\rm R}\mathbb{H_{\lambda}}F^{\perp}\cdot L\mathbb{A_{\lambda}}W_{2}^{0}.

Step 3
.
Numerology on the coefficients
. Injecting (
5.32
), (
5.33
), (
5.35
), (
5.36
) into (
5.19
), we obtain the formula

\displaystyle\frac{d}{dt}\,\mathbf{M}(t)

\displaystyle=C_{1}\int{\rm R}\mathbb{H_{\lambda}}W_{2}^{0}\cdot\mathbb{H_{\lambda}^{\perp}}\big(GW^{\perp}\big)+C_{2}\int\mathbb{H_{\lambda}}W_{2}^{0}\cdot\mathbb{H_{\lambda}^{\perp}}\big(GW^{\perp}\big)

(5.37)

\displaystyle+C_{3}\int{\rm R}\mathbb{H_{\lambda}}W_{2}^{0}\cdot GW_{2}^{0}+C_{4}\int\mathbb{H_{\lambda}}W_{2}^{0}\cdot GW_{2}^{0}

\displaystyle-2\rho_{1}c_{2}k_{1}\int\mathbb{H_{\lambda}}W_{2}^{0}\cdot L\mathbb{A_{\lambda}}W_{2}^{0}+2\rho_{1}c_{2}k_{2}\int\mathbb{A_{\lambda}}\mathbb{H_{\lambda}}W_{2}^{0}\cdot LW_{2}^{0}

\displaystyle+\mathcal{Q}_{4}+\mathcal{Q}_{5}+\mathcal{Q}_{F},

where the coefficients
C_{i}
for
1\leq i\leq 4
are given by

\left\{\begin{aligned} &C_{1}=-(\rho_{1}c_{1}+\rho_{2}c_{3})=-1,\\
&C_{2}=\rho_{1}c_{3}-\rho_{2}c_{1}=0,\\
&C_{3}=-\rho_{1}c_{3}+\rho_{1}c_{4}-\rho_{2}c_{1}-\rho_{2}c_{2}=0,\\
&C_{4}=-\rho_{1}c_{1}+\rho_{1}c_{2}\Delta k+\rho_{2}c_{3}+\rho_{2}c_{4}=1.\end{aligned}\right.

In view of the first two lines of (
5.37
), this formulates the desired uncontrollable term, thanks to the very choice of
c_{i}
(
5.20
). Moreover, the
\mathcal{Q}
terms on the last line of (
5.37
) are given explicitly by (
5.38
), (
5.42
), (
5.47
). We estimate them respectively in the rest of this proof.

Step 4
.
Estimating
\mathcal{Q}_{4}
. By above computation, the
\mathcal{Q}_{4}
term is given by

\displaystyle\mathcal{Q}_{4}

\displaystyle=c_{1}\hskip-1.00006pt\int W_{2}^{0}\cdot\big[\partial_{t},\mathbb{H_{\lambda}}\big]\big(GW^{\perp}\big)+c_{3}\int{\rm R}W_{2}^{0}\cdot\big[\partial_{t},\mathbb{H_{\lambda}}\big]\big(GW^{\perp}\big)

(5.38)

\displaystyle+c_{2}\int{\rm R}\big[\partial_{t},\mathbb{A_{\lambda}}\big]W_{2}^{0}\cdot LW_{2}^{0}-c_{4}\int\big[\partial_{t},\mathbb{A_{\lambda}}\big]W_{2}^{0}\cdot LW_{2}^{0}

\displaystyle+c_{1}\int\mathbb{H_{\lambda}}W_{2}^{0}\cdot\big(\partial_{t}G\big)W^{\perp}+c_{3}\int{\rm R}\mathbb{H_{\lambda}}W_{2}^{0}\cdot\big(\partial_{t}G\big)W^{\perp}

\displaystyle+c_{2}\int{\rm R}\mathbb{A_{\lambda}}W_{2}^{0}\cdot\big(\partial_{t}L\big)W_{2}^{0}-c_{4}\int\mathbb{A_{\lambda}}W_{2}^{0}\cdot\big(\partial_{t}L\big)W_{2}^{0},

which contains the commutators and
\partial_{t}G,\partial_{t}L
. From the explicit formulas of
Z,V
, there holds

\Big|\frac{\varLambda Z}{y}\Big|\lesssim\frac{1}{1+y^{3}},\qquad\Big|\frac{\varLambda V}{y^{2}}\Big|\lesssim\frac{1}{1+y^{4}},

(5.39)

which together with (
5.16
) implies

\left\{\begin{aligned} &{\rm R}\big[\partial_{t},\mathbb{H_{\lambda}}\big]\big(GW^{\perp}\big)=\frac{b^{2}}{\lambda^{8}}\,O\Big(\frac{1}{1+y^{8}}\Big){\rm R}w^{\perp}\\
&L\big[\partial_{t},\mathbb{A_{\lambda}}\big]W_{2}^{0}=\frac{b^{2}}{\lambda^{8}}\,O\Big(\frac{1}{1+y^{8}}\Big)\,w_{2}^{0}.\end{aligned}\right.

(5.40)

Using these with (
A.3
), (
A.29
), we can estimate the first term in (
5.38
) by Cauchy-Schwartz

\displaystyle\Big|\int W_{2}^{0}\cdot\big[\partial_{t},\mathbb{H_{\lambda}}\big]\big(GW^{\perp}\big)\Big|\lesssim\frac{b^{2}}{\lambda^{8}}\int\frac{|w_{2}^{0}|}{1+y^{4}}\cdot\frac{|w^{\perp}|}{1+y^{4}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{b^{2}}{\lambda^{8}}\bigg(\int\frac{\big|w_{2}^{0}\big|^{2}}{(1+y^{4})(1+\left|\log y\right|^{2})}\bigg)^{\frac{1}{2}}\bigg(\int\frac{|w^{\perp}|^{2}}{(1+y^{8})(1+\left|\log y\right|^{2})}\bigg)^{\frac{1}{2}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{b\,\delta(b^{\ast})}{\lambda^{8}}\bigg(\mathcal{E}_{4}+\frac{b^{4}}{\left|\log b\right|^{2}}\bigg).

The extra
b^{2}
given by (
5.40
) ensures the same bounds for the other terms on the first and second lines of (
5.38
). For the rest two lines, by the definition (
5.9
) we see

\left\{\begin{aligned} &\partial_{t}G=\frac{b^{2}}{\lambda^{4}r^{2}}\Big(O(\varLambda V)+O(\varLambda^{2}V)\Big)(y)=\frac{b^{2}}{\lambda^{6}}\,O\Big(\frac{1}{1+y^{4}}\Big),\\
&\partial_{t}L=\frac{b^{2}}{\lambda^{4}r}\Big(O(\varLambda Z)+O(\varLambda^{2}Z)\Big)(y)=\frac{b^{2}}{\lambda^{6}}\,O\Big(\frac{1}{1+y^{3}}\Big),\end{aligned}\right.

which also bring
b^{2}
as the above one, so the estimates are very similar. Applying the interpolation bounds in Lemma
A
fews more times, we obtain

|\mathcal{Q}_{4}|\lesssim\frac{b\,\delta(b^{\ast})}{\lambda^{8}}\bigg(\mathcal{E}_{4}+\frac{b^{4}}{\left|\log b\right|^{2}}\bigg).

(5.41)

Step 5
.
Estimating
\mathcal{Q}_{5}
. We consider the
\mathcal{Q}_{5}
term

\displaystyle\mathcal{Q}_{5}

\displaystyle=c_{1}\int\mathbb{H_{\lambda}}W_{2}^{0}\cdot G\mathcal{Q}_{2}^{1}+c_{3}\int{\rm R}\mathbb{H_{\lambda}}W_{2}^{0}\cdot G\mathcal{Q}_{2}^{1}+c_{2}\int{\rm R}\mathbb{A_{\lambda}}\mathcal{Q}_{2}^{2}\cdot LW_{2}^{0}

(5.42)

\displaystyle-c_{4}\int\mathbb{A_{\lambda}}\mathcal{Q}_{2}^{2}\cdot LW_{2}^{0}+c_{1}\int\mathbb{H_{\lambda}}\mathcal{Q}_{2}^{2}\cdot GW^{\perp}+c_{3}\int{\rm R}\mathbb{H_{\lambda}}\mathcal{Q}_{2}^{2}\cdot GW^{\perp}

\displaystyle+c_{2}\int{\rm R}\mathbb{A_{\lambda}}W_{2}^{0}\cdot L\mathcal{Q}_{2}^{2}-c_{4}\int\mathbb{A_{\lambda}}W_{2}^{0}\cdot L\mathcal{Q}_{2}^{2},

Observing from the definition (
5.7
), (
5.8
) and the commutators (
5.16
), we have the brute identities

\left\{\begin{aligned} &\mathcal{Q}_{2}^{1}=\frac{1}{\lambda^{2}}\Big(O(w_{2}^{1})+O\big(\hat{w}\wedge w_{2}^{0}\big)\Big),\\
&\mathcal{Q}_{2}^{2}=\frac{1}{\lambda^{4}}\bigg[O\Big(\frac{b}{1+y^{4}}\Big){\rm R}w^{\perp}+O\big({\it\mathbb{H}}w_{2}^{1}\big)+O\Big({\it\mathbb{H}}\big(\hat{w}\wedge w_{2}^{0}\big)\Big)\bigg].\end{aligned}\right.

(5.43)

From (
A.8
), (
A.20
), there holds the smallness of
\hat{w}
:

\|\hat{w}\|_{L^{\infty}}\leq\|\tilde{w}_{0}\|_{L^{\infty}}+\|w^{\perp}\|_{L^{\infty}}+\|\gamma\|_{L^{\infty}}\lesssim\delta(b^{\ast}).

(5.44)

Using this and the estimates on
w_{2}^{1}
(
A.30
), we have

\displaystyle\Big|\int\mathbb{H_{\lambda}}W_{2}^{0}\cdot G\mathcal{Q}_{2}^{1}\Big|

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{b}{\lambda^{8}}\int\big|{\it\mathbb{H}}w_{2}^{0}\big|\,\frac{1}{1+y^{4}}\Big(|w_{2}^{1}|+\big|\hat{w}\wedge w_{2}^{0}\big|\Big)

(5.45)

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{b\,\delta(b^{\ast})}{\lambda^{8}}\bigg(\mathcal{E}_{4}+\frac{b^{4}}{\left|\log b\right|^{2}}\bigg).

For the rest terms involving
\mathcal{Q}_{2}^{2}
in (
5.42
), we estimate last term on the first line for instance. From (
5.28
), there holds

\int{\rm R}\mathbb{A_{\lambda}}\mathcal{Q}_{2}^{2}\cdot LW_{2}^{0}=\hskip-1.00006pt\int{\rm R}\mathcal{Q}_{2}^{2}\cdot\mathbb{A_{\lambda}^{\ast}}\big(LW_{2}^{0}\big)=-\hskip-1.00006pt\int{\rm R}\mathcal{Q}_{2}^{2}\cdot L\mathbb{A_{\lambda}}W_{2}^{0}+\hskip-1.00006pt\hskip-1.00006pt\int{\rm R}\mathcal{Q}_{2}^{2}\cdot GW_{2}^{0},

and thus it can be controlled using (
5.43
), (
A.3
), (
A.29
), (
A.33
), we obtain

\displaystyle\Big|\int{\rm R}\mathbb{A_{\lambda}}\mathcal{Q}_{2}^{2}\,\cdot

\displaystyle LW_{2}^{0}\Big|\lesssim\int\big|{\rm R}\mathcal{Q}_{2}^{2}\big|\cdot\Big(\big|L\mathbb{A_{\lambda}}W_{2}^{0}\big|+\big|GW_{2}^{0}\big|\Big)

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{b}{\lambda^{8}}\int\bigg(\frac{b|w^{\perp}|}{1+y^{4}}+\big|{\it\mathbb{H}}w_{2}^{1}\big|+\Big|{\it\mathbb{H}}\big(\hat{w}\wedge w_{2}^{0}\big)\Big|\bigg)\cdot\bigg(\frac{\big|\mathbb{A}w_{2}^{0}\big|}{1+y^{3}}+\frac{\big|w_{2}^{0}\big|}{1+y^{4}}\bigg)

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{b\,\delta(b^{\ast})}{\lambda^{8}}\bigg(\mathcal{E}_{4}+\frac{b^{4}}{\left|\log b\right|^{2}}\bigg).

The terms involving
\mathbb{H_{\lambda}}\mathcal{Q}_{2}^{2}
can be estimated using the self-adjointness of
\mathbb{H_{\lambda}}
and Lemma
A
. Therefore we obtain

|\mathcal{Q}_{5}|\lesssim\frac{b\,\delta(b^{\ast})}{\lambda^{8}}\bigg(\mathcal{E}_{4}+\frac{b^{4}}{\left|\log b\right|^{2}}\bigg).

(5.46)

Step 6
.
Estimating
\mathcal{Q}_{F}
. The
\mathcal{Q}_{F}
term is given by

\displaystyle\mathcal{Q}_{F}=

\displaystyle-c_{1}\int{\rm R}\mathbb{H_{\lambda}}F^{\perp}\cdot\mathbb{H_{\lambda}}\big(GW^{\perp}\big)+c_{3}\int\mathbb{H_{\lambda}}F^{\perp}\cdot\mathbb{H_{\lambda}}\big(GW^{\perp}\big)

(5.47)

\displaystyle+c_{2}\int\mathbb{A_{\lambda}}\mathbb{H_{\lambda}}F^{\perp}\cdot LW_{2}^{0}+c_{4}\int{\rm R}\mathbb{A_{\lambda}}\mathbb{H_{\lambda}}F^{\perp}\cdot LW_{2}^{0}

\displaystyle-c_{1}\int\mathbb{H_{\lambda}}W_{2}^{0}\cdot GF^{\perp}-c_{3}\int{\rm R}\mathbb{H_{\lambda}}W_{2}^{0}\cdot GF^{\perp}

\displaystyle-c_{2}\int\mathbb{H_{\lambda}}F^{\perp}\cdot L\mathbb{A_{\lambda}}W_{2}^{0}-c_{4}\int{\rm R}\mathbb{H_{\lambda}}F^{\perp}\cdot L\mathbb{A_{\lambda}}W_{2}^{0}.

We claim the estimate:

\displaystyle\int\frac{|f^{\perp}|^{2}}{1+y^{8}}+\int\frac{1+\left|\log y\right|^{2}}{1+y^{4}}|{\it\mathbb{H}}f^{\perp}|^{2}

(5.48)

\displaystyle+\int\frac{1+\left|\log y\right|^{2}}{1+y^{2}}|\mathbb{A}{\it\mathbb{H}}f^{\perp}|^{2}\lesssim\frac{\mathcal{E}_{4}}{\log M}+\frac{b^{4}}{\left|\log b\right|^{2}},

and then from (
5.39
), and (
A.3
), (
A.29
), (
A.31
), it follows that

\displaystyle|\mathcal{Q}_{F}|

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{b}{\lambda^{8}}\Bigg(\int\frac{|w^{\perp}|^{2}}{(1+y^{8})(1+\left|\log y\right|^{2})}+\int\frac{|w_{2}^{0}|^{2}}{(1+y^{4})(1+\left|\log y\right|^{2})}

\displaystyle+\int|{\it\mathbb{H}}w_{2}^{0}|^{2}+\int\frac{|\mathbb{A}w_{2}^{0}|^{2}}{(1+y^{2})(1+\left|\log y\right|^{2})}\Bigg)^{\frac{1}{2}}\bigg(\mathcal{E}_{4}+\frac{b^{4}}{\left|\log b\right|^{2}}\bigg)^{\frac{1}{2}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{b}{\lambda^{8}}\bigg(\frac{\mathcal{E}_{4}}{\sqrt{\log M}}+\frac{b^{4}}{\left|\log b\right|^{2}}\bigg),

which is the desired bound for
\mathcal{Q}_{F}
. This together with (
5.41
), (
5.46
) concludes the proof. Recall (
4.23
),
f
consists of three separate terms, namely
\tilde{\varPsi}_{0}
,
\widetilde{{\rm Mod}}
,
\mathcal{R}(t)
, and thus it remains to prove (
5.48
) for each one of them. The estimates for
\tilde{\varPsi}_{0}
follow directly from (
3.55
), (
3.58
), (
3.59
), and we are left to handle the other two.

Step 7
.
Contribution of
\widetilde{{\rm Mod}}
. From (
4.34
), we see

U(t)\lesssim\frac{1}{\sqrt{\log M}}\bigg(\sqrt{\mathcal{E}_{4}}+\frac{b^{4}}{\left|\log b\right|^{2}}\bigg),

(5.49)

which together with (
3.8
), (
3.54
) yields

\widetilde{{\rm Mod}}^{\perp}=U(t)\hskip 1.00006ptO\bigg(\frac{\tilde{w}_{0}}{b}+b\hskip 0.50003pty^{5}\mathbf{1}_{y\leq 1}+\frac{b\hskip 0.50003pty^{3}}{\left|\log b\right|}\mathbf{1}_{1\leq y\leq 6B_{0}}+\frac{1}{y}\mathbf{1}_{y\gtrsim B_{1}}\bigg),

and also

\displaystyle{\it\mathbb{H}}\widetilde{{\rm Mod}}

\displaystyle=\chi_{B_{1}}\Bigg(\frac{a_{s}}{\rho_{1}^{2}+\rho_{2}^{2}}\begin{bmatrix}\rho_{1}\\
\rho_{2}\\
0\end{bmatrix}\varLambda\phi+\frac{b_{s}+b^{2}+a^{2}}{\rho_{1}^{2}+\rho_{2}^{2}}\begin{bmatrix}-\rho_{2}\\
\rho_{1}\\
0\end{bmatrix}\varLambda\phi\Bigg)

(5.50)

\displaystyle+U(t)\hskip 1.00006ptO\bigg(b\hskip 0.50003pty^{3}\mathbf{1}_{y\leq 1}+\frac{b\hskip 0.50003pty}{\left|\log b\right|}\mathbf{1}_{1\leq y\leq 6B_{0}}+\frac{\log y}{y}\mathbf{1}_{1\leq y\leq 2B_{1}}+\frac{1}{y^{3}}\mathbf{1}_{y\gtrsim B_{1}}\bigg)

\displaystyle+U(t)\hskip 1.00006ptO\bigg(\mathbf{1}_{y\leq 1}+\frac{\log y}{y^{2}}\mathbf{1}_{1\leq y\leq 2B_{1}}\bigg)\hskip 0.50003pte_{z}.

Therefore we have

\int\frac{\big|\widetilde{{\rm Mod}}^{\perp}\big|^{2}}{1+y^{8}}+\int\frac{1+\left|\log y\right|^{2}}{1+y^{4}}\big|{\it\mathbb{H}}\widetilde{{\rm Mod}}^{\perp}\big|^{2}\lesssim\frac{\mathcal{E}_{4}}{\log M}+\frac{b^{4}}{\left|\log b\right|^{2}}.

Moreover, the cancellation

AHT_{1}=A\varLambda\phi=0

makes the first profiles in
\tilde{w}_{0}/b
vanish, implying the refined bound

\int\frac{1+\left|\log y\right|^{2}}{1+y^{2}}\big|\mathbb{A}{\it\mathbb{H}}\widetilde{{\rm Mod}}^{\perp}\big|^{2}\lesssim\delta(b^{\ast})\bigg(\mathcal{E}_{4}+\frac{b^{4}}{\left|\log b\right|^{2}}\bigg),

which concludes (
5.48
) for
\widetilde{{\rm Mod}}
.

Step 8
.
Contribution of
\mathcal{R}
. By the definition (
4.24
), we separate
\mathcal{R}
into

\mathcal{R}=\mathcal{R}_{1}+\mathcal{R}_{2},\quad\mbox{where}\quad\left\{\begin{aligned} &\,\mathcal{R}_{1}=w\wedge\Big(\rho_{1}{\it\mathbb{H}}\tilde{w}_{0}-\rho_{2}{\rm R}{\it\mathbb{H}}\tilde{w}_{0}+\varLambda\phi\,\hat{p}\Big),\\
&\,\mathcal{R}_{2}=-\rho_{2}{\rm\hat{J}}\big(w\wedge{\it\mathbb{H}}\tilde{w}_{0}\big)-\rho_{2}w\wedge\big(\tilde{w}_{0}\wedge{\it\mathbb{H}}\tilde{w}_{0}\big)\\
&\phantom{\,\mathcal{R}_{2}=}-\rho_{2}w\wedge\big(w\wedge{\it\mathbb{H}}\tilde{w}_{0}\big)+\varTheta_{s}Z{\rm R}w.\end{aligned}\right.

(5.51)

By the given approximate solution (
3.4
), (
3.5
), (
3.18
), we compute

\displaystyle\rho_{1}{\it\mathbb{H}}\tilde{w}_{0}-\rho_{2}{\rm R}{\it\mathbb{H}}\tilde{w}_{0}+\varLambda\phi\hskip 1.00006pt\hat{p}

\displaystyle=\chi_{B_{1}}\bigg(\hskip-1.00006pt\big(\rho_{1}-\rho_{2}{\rm R}\big){\it\mathbb{H}}w_{0}^{2}+\begin{bmatrix}a+\varTheta_{s}\\
b+\frac{\lambda_{s}}{\lambda}\\
0\end{bmatrix}\hskip-1.00006pt\hskip-1.00006pt\varLambda\phi\hskip-1.00006pt\bigg)

\displaystyle+\Big(\hskip-1.00006pt-2\partial_{y}\chi_{B_{1}}\partial_{y}w_{0}-\Delta\chi_{B_{1}}w_{0}-2(1\hskip-1.00006pt+\hskip-1.00006ptZ)\hskip 0.50003pt\partial_{y}\chi_{B_{1}}\big(e_{y}\wedge w_{0}\big)+(1\hskip-1.00006pt-\hskip-1.00006pt\chi_{B_{1}})\hat{p}\hskip 1.00006pt\varLambda\phi\Big)

\displaystyle=O\bigg(b^{2}y^{3}\mathbf{1}_{y\leq 1}+\frac{b^{\frac{3}{2}}}{\left|\log b\right|}\mathbf{1}_{y\sim B_{1}}+\frac{b}{y}\mathbf{1}_{y\gtrsim B_{1}}\bigg),

which gives the brute force estimate for
{\it\mathbb{H}}\mathcal{R}_{1}
:

\displaystyle\it{\displaystyle\mathbb{H}}\bigg[w\wedge\Big(\rho_{1}{\it\mathbb{H}}\tilde{w}_{0}-\rho_{2}{\rm R}{\it\mathbb{H}}\tilde{w}_{0}+\varLambda\phi\,\hat{p}\Big)\bigg]

(5.52)

\displaystyle\lesssim\bigg(|Hw^{\perp}|+|\Delta\gamma|+\frac{\big|\big(\partial_{y}+\frac{1}{y}\big)w\big|}{1+y^{2}}\bigg)\bigg(b^{2}\hskip 0.50003pty^{3}\mathbf{1}_{y\leq 1}\hskip-1.00006pt+\hskip-1.00006pt\frac{b^{\frac{3}{2}}}{\left|\log b\right|}\mathbf{1}_{y\sim B_{1}}\hskip-1.00006pt+\hskip-1.00006pt\frac{b}{y}\mathbf{1}_{y\gtrsim B_{1}}\bigg)

\displaystyle+\bigg(|\partial_{y}w|+\frac{|w|}{y}\bigg)\bigg(b^{2}\hskip 0.50003pty^{2}\mathbf{1}_{y\leq 1}+\frac{b^{\frac{3}{2}}}{y\left|\log b\right|}\mathbf{1}_{y\sim B_{1}}+\frac{b}{y^{2}}\mathbf{1}_{y\gtrsim B_{1}}\bigg).

This together with interpolation bounds in Appendix
A
yields

\int\frac{|\mathcal{R}_{1}^{\perp}|^{2}}{1+y^{8}}+\int\frac{1+\left|\log y\right|^{2}}{1+y^{4}}|{\it\mathbb{H}}\mathcal{R}_{1}^{\perp}|^{2}+\int\frac{1+\left|\log y\right|^{2}}{1+y^{2}}|\mathbb{A}{\it\mathbb{H}}\mathcal{R}_{1}^{\perp}|^{2}\lesssim b^{2}\bigg(\mathcal{E}_{4}+\frac{b^{4}}{\left|\log b\right|^{2}}\bigg).

Now we treat the terms involving
\mathcal{R}_{2}
. By the construction of
\tilde{w}_{0}
in Lemma
3.2
, we have

\left\{\begin{aligned} &\tilde{w}_{0}=b\hskip 1.00006ptO\Big(y^{3}\mathbf{1}_{y\leq 1}+y\log y\,\mathbf{1}_{1\leq y\leq 2B_{1}}\Big),\\
&{\it\mathbb{H}}\tilde{w}_{0}=b\hskip 1.00006ptO\bigg(y\mathbf{1}_{y\leq 1}+\frac{\log y}{y}\,\mathbf{1}_{1\leq y\leq 2B_{1}}\bigg).\end{aligned}\right.

(5.53)

The bounds of the first two terms in
\mathcal{R}_{2}
(
5.51
) easily follows using Lemma
A
. To control the third one, we apply (
5.44
) to get

\int\frac{\big|w\wedge(w\wedge{\it\mathbb{H}}\tilde{w}_{0})\big|^{2}}{1+y^{8}}\lesssim\delta(b^{\ast})\bigg(\mathcal{E}_{4}+\frac{b^{4}}{\left|\log b\right|^{2}}\bigg).

Moreover, we compute by brute force that for
y\geq 1
,

\displaystyle\big|{\it\mathbb{H}}\big(w\hskip-1.00006pt\wedge\hskip-1.00006pt(w\hskip-1.00006pt\wedge\hskip-1.00006pt{\it\mathbb{H}}\tilde{w}_{0})\big)\big|

\displaystyle\lesssim b\,O\bigg(\frac{\log y}{y}\bigg(|\partial_{y}^{2}w|\hskip 0.50003pt|w|+|\partial_{y}w|^{2}+\frac{|\partial_{y}w|\hskip 0.50003pt|w|}{y}+\frac{|w|^{2}}{y^{2}}\bigg)\mathbf{1}_{1\leq y\leq 2B_{1}}\hskip-1.00006pt\bigg),

which together with (
A.3
), (
A.14
), (
A.16
), (
A.21
) implies

\int_{y\geq 1}\frac{1+\left|\log y\right|^{2}}{1+y^{4}}\big|{\it\mathbb{H}}\big(w\wedge(w\wedge{\it\mathbb{H}}\tilde{w}_{0})\big)\big|^{2}\lesssim b\,\delta(b^{\ast})\bigg(\mathcal{E}_{4}+\frac{b^{4}}{\left|\log b\right|^{2}}\bigg),

The
y\leq 1
case and the estimate for
\mathbb{A}{\it\mathbb{H}}\big(w\wedge(w\wedge{\it\mathbb{H}}\tilde{w}_{0})\big)
can be obtained in the same way. For the last term in
\mathcal{R}_{2}
involving
\varTheta_{s}
, by (
4.34
), we have

\displaystyle|\varTheta_{s}Z{\rm R}w|

\displaystyle\lesssim\big(|a|+U(t)\big)\hskip 0.50003pt|w^{\perp}|,

(5.54)

\displaystyle\big|{\it\mathbb{H}}\big(\varTheta_{s}Z{\rm R}w\big)\big|

\displaystyle\lesssim\big(|a|+U(t)\big)\hskip 0.50003pt\bigg(|\mathbb{H^{\perp}}w|+\frac{|\partial_{y}w^{\perp}|}{1+y^{2}}+\frac{|w^{\perp}|}{y(1+y^{2})}\bigg),

\displaystyle\big|\mathbb{A}{\it\mathbb{H}}\big(\varTheta_{s}Z{\rm R}w\big)\big|

\displaystyle\lesssim\big(|a|+U(t)\big)\hskip 0.50003pt\bigg(|\mathbb{A}\mathbb{H^{\perp}}w|+\frac{|\partial_{y}^{2}w^{\perp}|}{1+y^{2}}+\frac{\partial_{y}w^{\perp}}{y(1+y^{2})}+\frac{|w^{\perp}|}{y^{2}(1+y^{2})}\bigg),

together with (
5.49
), (
A.3
) yields the bounds for
\varTheta_{s}Z{\rm R}w
. In summary, we obtain

\int\frac{|\mathcal{R}_{2}^{\perp}|^{2}}{1+y^{8}}+\hskip-1.00006pt\hskip-1.00006pt\int\hskip-1.00006pt\frac{1+\left|\log y\right|^{2}}{1+y^{4}}|{\it\mathbb{H}}\mathcal{R}_{2}^{\perp}|^{2}+\hskip-1.00006pt\hskip-1.00006pt\int\hskip-1.00006pt\frac{1+\left|\log y\right|^{2}}{1+y^{2}}|\mathbb{A}{\it\mathbb{H}}\mathcal{R}_{2}^{\perp}|^{2}\lesssim b\,\delta(b^{\ast})\bigg(\mathcal{E}_{4}+\frac{b^{4}}{\left|\log b\right|^{2}}\bigg).

This concludes the proof.

\square

5.3
Energy estimate

The aim of this subsection is to prove the following proposition, which is the heart of our analysis.

Proposition 5.1
(Mixed energy/Morawetz estimate)

Assume Lemma
5.2
and Appendix
A
. Then there exists a universal constant
d_{2}\in(0,1)
independent of
M
, such that the following differential inequality holds

\displaystyle\frac{d}{dt}\,\Bigg\{\frac{1}{\lambda^{6}}\bigg[\mathcal{E}_{4}+\delta(b^{\ast})\bigg(\mathcal{E}_{4}+\frac{b^{4}}{\left|\log b\right|^{2}}\bigg)\bigg]\Bigg\}

(5.55)

\displaystyle\leq\frac{b}{\lambda^{8}}\bigg[2\bigg(1-d_{2}+\frac{C}{\sqrt{\log M}}\bigg)\mathcal{E}_{4}+O\bigg(\frac{b^{4}}{\left|\log b\right|^{2}}\bigg)\bigg],

where
\delta(b^{\ast})
is the infinitesimal defined by (
4.12
).

Proof of Proposition
5.1
.

This proof is based on the mixed energy/Morawetz identity (
5.23
). We proceed by first controlling the mixed energy functional, and then estimating each term on the RHS of (
5.23
) respectively.

Step 1
.
Control of the functional
. Recall (
2.38
), (
5.4
), (
5.44
), there holds

\displaystyle{\rm\hat{J}}{\it\mathbb{H}}w_{2}

\displaystyle=({\rm R}+{\rm R}_{\hat{w}})\hskip 0.50003pt{\it\mathbb{H}}\hskip 0.50003pt({\rm R}+{\rm R}_{\hat{w}})\hskip 0.50003pt{\it\mathbb{H}}w

(5.56)

\displaystyle=-\big(1+\delta(b^{\ast})\big)(\mathbb{H^{\perp}})^{2}w^{\perp}+O\bigg(H\bigg(\frac{\partial_{y}\gamma}{1+y^{2}}\bigg)\bigg)+O\Big({\rm R}{\it\mathbb{H}}{\rm R}_{\hat{w}}\big({\it\mathbb{H}}w\big)\Big).

From (
A.16
), (
A.19
), the second term on the RHS of (
5.56
) is
L^{2}
bounded by

\displaystyle\int\Big|O\bigg(H\bigg(\frac{\partial_{y}\gamma}{1+y^{2}}\bigg)\bigg)\Big|^{2}

\displaystyle\lesssim\int\frac{|\partial_{y}^{3}\gamma|^{2}}{1+y^{4}}+\int\frac{\big(\partial_{y}^{2}\gamma-\frac{\partial_{y}\gamma}{y}\big)^{2}}{y^{2}(1+y^{4})}+\int\frac{|\partial_{y}^{2}\gamma|^{2}}{1+y^{6}}+\int\frac{|\partial_{y}\gamma|^{2}}{1+y^{8}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\sum_{0\leq i\leq 3}\int\frac{|\partial_{y}^{i}\gamma|^{2}}{y^{2}(1+y^{6-2i})(1+\left|\log y\right|^{2})}+\int\frac{|A\partial_{y}\gamma|^{2}}{y^{4}(1+\left|\log y\right|^{2})}

\displaystyle\lesssim\,\delta(b^{\ast})\bigg(\mathcal{E}_{4}+\frac{b^{4}}{\left|\log b\right|^{2}}\bigg).

The same
L^{2}
bound holds also for the last term in (
5.56
) by (
A.3
), (
A.7
), (
A.11
), (
A.21
), (
A.22
), (
A.24
). In view of the definition (
4.8
), we have

\int\big|{\rm\hat{J}}_{\lambda}\mathbb{H_{\lambda}}W_{2}\big|^{2}=\frac{1}{\lambda^{6}}\int\big|{\rm\hat{J}}{\it\mathbb{H}}w_{2}\big|^{2}=\frac{1}{\lambda^{6}}\bigg[\mathcal{E}_{4}+\delta(b^{\ast})\bigg(\mathcal{E}_{4}+\frac{b^{4}}{\left|\log b\right|^{2}}\bigg)\bigg].

(5.57)

Moreover, by (
5.21
), the morawetz term does not perturb the bound

\frac{1}{2}\int\big|{\rm\hat{J}}_{\lambda}\mathbb{H_{\lambda}}W_{2}\big|^{2}-\mathbf{M}(t)=\frac{1}{2\lambda^{6}}\bigg[\mathcal{E}_{4}+\delta(b^{\ast})\bigg(\mathcal{E}_{4}+\frac{b^{4}}{\left|\log b\right|^{2}}\bigg)\bigg].

(5.58)

Step 2
.
The quasilinear terms with coefficients
\rho_{1},\rho_{2}
. We consider the first two lines of the RHS of (
5.23
):

\displaystyle-\rho_{1}\int{\rm\hat{J}}_{\lambda}\mathbb{H_{\lambda}}W_{2}\cdot\big({\rm\hat{J}}_{\lambda}\mathbb{H_{\lambda}}\big)^{2}W_{2}+\rho_{2}\int{\rm\hat{J}}_{\lambda}\mathbb{H_{\lambda}}W_{2}\cdot\big({\rm\hat{J}}_{\lambda}\mathbb{H_{\lambda}}\big)^{2}{\rm\hat{J}}_{\lambda}W_{2}

(5.59)

\displaystyle+2\rho_{1}c_{2}k_{1}\int\mathbb{H_{\lambda}}W_{2}^{0}\cdot L\mathbb{A_{\lambda}}W_{2}^{0}-2\rho_{1}c_{2}k_{2}\int\mathbb{A_{\lambda}}\mathbb{H_{\lambda}}W_{2}^{0}\cdot LW_{2}^{0}.

By the scaling (
4.32
) and definition (
5.2
), we see

\displaystyle-\rho_{1}\int{\rm\hat{J}}_{\lambda}\mathbb{H_{\lambda}}W_{2}\cdot\big({\rm\hat{J}}_{\lambda}\mathbb{H_{\lambda}}\big)^{2}W_{2}+\rho_{2}\int{\rm\hat{J}}_{\lambda}\mathbb{H_{\lambda}}W_{2}\cdot\big({\rm\hat{J}}_{\lambda}\mathbb{H_{\lambda}}\big)^{2}{\rm\hat{J}}_{\lambda}W_{2}

(5.60)

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{\lambda^{8}}\bigg[-\rho_{1}\int{\rm\hat{J}}{\it\mathbb{H}}w_{2}\cdot({\rm\hat{J}}{\it\mathbb{H}})^{2}w_{2}+\rho_{2}\int{\rm\hat{J}}^{2}{\it\mathbb{H}}w_{2}\cdot\big({\rm\hat{J}}{\it\mathbb{H}}\big)^{2}{\rm\hat{J}}w_{2}\bigg].

To treat the first term of (
5.59
), we introduce the following lemma, whose proof is given in Appendix
B
.

Lemma 5.4
(Gain of two derivatives)

Assume Appendix
A
. There exists a constant
d_{1}\,\in\,(0,1)
such that for any vector
\varGamma
under the Frenet basis
\big[e_{r},e_{\tau},Q\big]
, there holds the inequality

-\int{\rm\hat{J}}{\it\mathbb{H}}\varGamma\cdot\big({\rm\hat{J}}{\it\mathbb{H}}\big)^{2}\varGamma\leq\,\frac{b(1-d_{1})(|\rho_{1}|+|\rho_{2}|)}{2(\rho_{1}^{2}+\rho_{2}^{2})}\big\|{\rm\hat{J}}{\it\mathbb{H}}\varGamma\big\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+b\,\delta(b^{\ast})\,\big\|{\it\mathbb{H}}\varGamma\big\|_{L^{2}}^{2},

(5.61)

where
\delta(b^{\ast})
is the infinitesimal defined by (
4.12
).

Applying (
5.61
) with
\varGamma=w_{2}
, and also (
A.31
), we have for some constant
d_{2}=d_{2}(b^{\ast})\in(0,1)
independent of
W_{2}
that

\displaystyle-\rho_{1}\int{\rm\hat{J}}{\it\mathbb{H}}w_{2}\cdot({\rm\hat{J}}{\it\mathbb{H}})^{2}w_{2}

\displaystyle\leq b(1-d_{1})\big\|{\rm\hat{J}}{\it\mathbb{H}}w_{2}\big\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+b\,\delta(b^{\ast})\big\|{\it\mathbb{H}}w_{2}\big\|_{L^{2}}^{2}

(5.62)

\displaystyle\leq b(1-d_{2})\mathcal{E}_{4}+b\,\delta(b^{\ast})\bigg(\mathcal{E}_{4}+\frac{b^{4}}{\left|\log b\right|^{2}}\bigg),

where we used
\rho_{1}(|\rho_{1}|+|\rho_{2}|)\leq 2(\rho_{1}^{2}+\rho_{2}^{2})
. Then for the second term (
5.59
), we use (
5.4
) to separate it into

\displaystyle-\int{\rm\hat{J}}^{2}{\it\mathbb{H}}w_{2}\cdot{\it\mathbb{H}}\big({\rm\hat{J}}{\it\mathbb{H}}{\rm\hat{J}}w_{2}\big)

(5.63)

\displaystyle=-\int{\rm R}^{2}{\it\mathbb{H}}w_{2}\cdot{\it\mathbb{H}}\big[({\rm R}{\it\mathbb{H}}{\rm R})w_{2}\big]

\displaystyle-\int{\rm R}^{2}{\it\mathbb{H}}w_{2}\cdot{\it\mathbb{H}}\Big[\Big({\rm R}{\it\mathbb{H}}{\rm R}_{\hat{w}}+{\rm R}_{\hat{w}}{\it\mathbb{H}}{\rm R}+{\rm R}_{\hat{w}}{\it\mathbb{H}}{\rm R}_{\hat{w}}\Big)w_{2}\Big]

\displaystyle-\int\Big({\rm R}{\rm R}_{\hat{w}}\hskip-1.00006pt+\hskip-1.00006pt{\rm R}_{\hat{w}}{\rm R}\hskip-1.00006pt+\hskip-1.00006pt{\rm R}_{\hat{w}}^{2}\Big){\it\mathbb{H}}w_{2}\cdot{\it\mathbb{H}}\Big[\Big({\rm R}{\it\mathbb{H}}{\rm R}\hskip-1.00006pt+\hskip-1.00006pt{\rm R}{\it\mathbb{H}}{\rm R}_{\hat{w}}\hskip-1.00006pt+\hskip-1.00006pt{\rm R}_{\hat{w}}{\it\mathbb{H}}{\rm R}\hskip-1.00006pt+\hskip-1.00006pt{\rm R}_{\hat{w}}{\it\mathbb{H}}{\rm R}_{\hat{w}}\Big)w_{2}\Big].

By (
2.38
), (
2.37
), the first term on the RHS is actually

\displaystyle-\int{\rm R}^{2}{\it\mathbb{H}}w_{2}\cdot{\it\mathbb{H}}\big[({\rm R}{\it\mathbb{H}}{\rm R})w_{2}\big]=\int{\rm R}^{2}{\it\mathbb{H}}w_{2}\cdot{\it\mathbb{H}}(\mathbb{H^{\perp}}w_{2})

(5.64)

\displaystyle=\int\Big(\hskip-1.00006pt-\hskip-1.00006pt\mathbb{H^{\perp}}w_{2}+2(1\hskip-1.00006pt+\hskip-1.00006ptZ)\partial_{y}w_{2}^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-2.26666pt}{\resizebox{868894}{445645}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.75pt}{$\displaystyle(3)$}}}}}{\raisebox{-2.26666pt}{\resizebox{868894}{445645}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.75pt}{$\textstyle(3)$}}}}}{\raisebox{-2.26665pt}{\resizebox{759247}{445645}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.22499pt}{$\scriptstyle(3)$}}}}}{\raisebox{-2.26666pt}{\resizebox{759248}{445645}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.875pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle(3)$}}}}}}\hskip 0.50003pte_{x}\Big)\cdot\bigg[(\mathbb{H^{\perp}})^{2}w_{2}+2(1\hskip-1.00006pt+\hskip-1.00006ptZ)\Big(\partial_{y}\hskip-1.00006pt\hskip-1.00006pt+\hskip-1.00006pt\hskip-1.00006pt\frac{Z}{y}\Big)Hw_{2}^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-2.26666pt}{\resizebox{868894}{445645}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.75pt}{$\displaystyle(1)$}}}}}{\raisebox{-2.26666pt}{\resizebox{868894}{445645}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.75pt}{$\textstyle(1)$}}}}}{\raisebox{-2.26665pt}{\resizebox{759247}{445645}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.22499pt}{$\scriptstyle(1)$}}}}}{\raisebox{-2.26666pt}{\resizebox{759248}{445645}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.875pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle(1)$}}}}}}\hskip 0.50003pte_{z}\bigg]

\displaystyle=-\int|\mathbb{A}\mathbb{H^{\perp}}w_{2}|^{2}+\int 2(1\hskip-1.00006pt+\hskip-1.00006ptZ)\partial_{y}w_{2}^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-2.26666pt}{\resizebox{868894}{445645}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.75pt}{$\displaystyle(3)$}}}}}{\raisebox{-2.26666pt}{\resizebox{868894}{445645}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.75pt}{$\textstyle(3)$}}}}}{\raisebox{-2.26665pt}{\resizebox{759247}{445645}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.22499pt}{$\scriptstyle(3)$}}}}}{\raisebox{-2.26666pt}{\resizebox{759248}{445645}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.875pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle(3)$}}}}}}H^{2}w_{2}^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-2.26666pt}{\resizebox{868894}{445645}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.75pt}{$\displaystyle(1)$}}}}}{\raisebox{-2.26666pt}{\resizebox{868894}{445645}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.75pt}{$\textstyle(1)$}}}}}{\raisebox{-2.26665pt}{\resizebox{759247}{445645}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.22499pt}{$\scriptstyle(1)$}}}}}{\raisebox{-2.26666pt}{\resizebox{759248}{445645}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.875pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle(1)$}}}}}}.

We claim the negative integral
-\int|\mathbb{A}\mathbb{H^{\perp}}w_{2}|^{2}
is the leading term of (
5.63
), and the other terms in (
5.63
) are small errors in the sense that they can be bounded by
-\int|\mathbb{A}\mathbb{H^{\perp}}w_{2}|^{2}
plus
b^{5}\,\delta(b^{\ast})\left|\log b\right|^{-2}
. Indeed, in view of factorization (
2.37
), we observe the LHS of (
5.63
) can be reformulated as

\displaystyle\int{\rm\hat{J}}^{2}{\it\mathbb{H}}w_{2}\cdot{\it\mathbb{H}}\big({\rm\hat{J}}{\it\mathbb{H}}{\rm\hat{J}}w_{2}\big)

\displaystyle=\int\mathbb{A}\big({\rm\hat{J}}^{2}{\it\mathbb{H}}w_{2}\big)\cdot\mathbb{A}\big({\rm\hat{J}}{\it\mathbb{H}}{\rm\hat{J}}w_{2}\big)-\int\big({\rm\hat{J}}^{2}{\it\mathbb{H}}w_{2}\big)^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-2.26666pt}{\resizebox{868894}{445645}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.75pt}{$\displaystyle(1)$}}}}}{\raisebox{-2.26666pt}{\resizebox{868894}{445645}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.75pt}{$\textstyle(1)$}}}}}{\raisebox{-2.26665pt}{\resizebox{759247}{445645}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.22499pt}{$\scriptstyle(1)$}}}}}{\raisebox{-2.26666pt}{\resizebox{759248}{445645}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.875pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle(1)$}}}}}}\cdot 2(1\hskip-1.00006pt+\hskip-1.00006ptZ)\partial_{y}\big({\rm\hat{J}}{\it\mathbb{H}}{\rm\hat{J}}w_{2}\big)^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-2.26666pt}{\resizebox{868894}{445645}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.75pt}{$\displaystyle(3)$}}}}}{\raisebox{-2.26666pt}{\resizebox{868894}{445645}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.75pt}{$\textstyle(3)$}}}}}{\raisebox{-2.26665pt}{\resizebox{759247}{445645}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.22499pt}{$\scriptstyle(3)$}}}}}{\raisebox{-2.26666pt}{\resizebox{759248}{445645}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.875pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle(3)$}}}}}}

\displaystyle+\int\partial_{y}\big({\rm\hat{J}}^{2}{\it\mathbb{H}}w_{2}\big)^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-2.26666pt}{\resizebox{868894}{445645}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.75pt}{$\displaystyle(3)$}}}}}{\raisebox{-2.26666pt}{\resizebox{868894}{445645}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.75pt}{$\textstyle(3)$}}}}}{\raisebox{-2.26665pt}{\resizebox{759247}{445645}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.22499pt}{$\scriptstyle(3)$}}}}}{\raisebox{-2.26666pt}{\resizebox{759248}{445645}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.875pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle(3)$}}}}}}\cdot\partial_{y}\big({\rm\hat{J}}{\it\mathbb{H}}{\rm\hat{J}}w_{2}\big)^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-2.26666pt}{\resizebox{868894}{445645}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.75pt}{$\displaystyle(3)$}}}}}{\raisebox{-2.26666pt}{\resizebox{868894}{445645}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.75pt}{$\textstyle(3)$}}}}}{\raisebox{-2.26665pt}{\resizebox{759247}{445645}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.22499pt}{$\scriptstyle(3)$}}}}}{\raisebox{-2.26666pt}{\resizebox{759248}{445645}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.875pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle(3)$}}}}}}+\int\big({\rm\hat{J}}^{2}{\it\mathbb{H}}w_{2}\big)^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-2.26666pt}{\resizebox{868894}{445645}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.75pt}{$\displaystyle(3)$}}}}}{\raisebox{-2.26666pt}{\resizebox{868894}{445645}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.75pt}{$\textstyle(3)$}}}}}{\raisebox{-2.26665pt}{\resizebox{759247}{445645}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.22499pt}{$\scriptstyle(3)$}}}}}{\raisebox{-2.26666pt}{\resizebox{759248}{445645}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.875pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle(3)$}}}}}}\cdot 2(1\hskip-1.00006pt+\hskip-1.00006ptZ)\Big(\partial_{y}\hskip-1.00006pt\hskip-1.00006pt+\hskip-1.00006pt\hskip-1.00006pt\frac{Z}{y}\Big)\big({\rm\hat{J}}{\it\mathbb{H}}{\rm\hat{J}}w_{2}\big)^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-2.26666pt}{\resizebox{868894}{445645}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.75pt}{$\displaystyle(1)$}}}}}{\raisebox{-2.26666pt}{\resizebox{868894}{445645}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.75pt}{$\textstyle(1)$}}}}}{\raisebox{-2.26665pt}{\resizebox{759247}{445645}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.22499pt}{$\scriptstyle(1)$}}}}}{\raisebox{-2.26666pt}{\resizebox{759248}{445645}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.875pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle(1)$}}}}}}.

The second order spacial derivative from
{\it\mathbb{H}}
can act on either the second derivative of
w_{2}
(
{\it\mathbb{H}}w_{2}
and
{\rm\hat{J}}{\it\mathbb{H}}{\rm\hat{J}}w_{2}
), or
\hat{w}
coming from
{\rm\hat{J}}
(recall
{\rm\hat{J}}=(e_{z}+\hat{w})\wedge
). According to this the estimates can be splited into different types. The most involved situation is that one single derivative acts on
\hat{w}
, and the other acts on
{\it\mathbb{H}}w_{2}
(or
{\rm\hat{J}}{\it\mathbb{H}}{\rm\hat{J}}w_{2}
), where the whole integrand is basically given by
\partial_{y}\hat{w}
times
{\it\mathbb{H}}w_{2}
(or
{\rm\hat{J}}{\it\mathbb{H}}{\rm\hat{J}}w_{2}
) times a third derivative of
w_{2}
. A key observation here is that the third derivative of
w_{2}
actually formulates
\mathbb{A}\mathbb{H^{\perp}}w_{2}
, so that a straight application of Cauchy-Schwartz finishes the estimate. To see this, we estimate for instance the second term on the RHS of (
5.64
). Recalling (
5.2
), we see

w_{2}^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-2.26666pt}{\resizebox{868894}{445645}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.75pt}{$\displaystyle(3)$}}}}}{\raisebox{-2.26666pt}{\resizebox{868894}{445645}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.75pt}{$\textstyle(3)$}}}}}{\raisebox{-2.26665pt}{\resizebox{759247}{445645}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.22499pt}{$\scriptstyle(3)$}}}}}{\raisebox{-2.26666pt}{\resizebox{759248}{445645}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.875pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle(3)$}}}}}}=({\rm\hat{J}}{\it\mathbb{H}}w)^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-2.26666pt}{\resizebox{868894}{445645}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.75pt}{$\displaystyle(3)$}}}}}{\raisebox{-2.26666pt}{\resizebox{868894}{445645}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.75pt}{$\textstyle(3)$}}}}}{\raisebox{-2.26665pt}{\resizebox{759247}{445645}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.22499pt}{$\scriptstyle(3)$}}}}}{\raisebox{-2.26666pt}{\resizebox{759248}{445645}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.875pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle(3)$}}}}}}=\hat{\alpha}H\beta-\hat{\beta}H\alpha.

This together with (
A.3
), (
A.5
) (
A.11
), (
A.15
), and the construction of the approximation solution implies

\displaystyle\int\big|2(1\hskip-1.00006pt+\hskip-1.00006ptZ)\partial_{y}w_{2}^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-2.26666pt}{\resizebox{868894}{445645}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.75pt}{$\displaystyle(3)$}}}}}{\raisebox{-2.26666pt}{\resizebox{868894}{445645}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.75pt}{$\textstyle(3)$}}}}}{\raisebox{-2.26665pt}{\resizebox{759247}{445645}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.22499pt}{$\scriptstyle(3)$}}}}}{\raisebox{-2.26666pt}{\resizebox{759248}{445645}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.875pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle(3)$}}}}}}H^{2}w_{2}^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-2.26666pt}{\resizebox{868894}{445645}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.75pt}{$\displaystyle(1)$}}}}}{\raisebox{-2.26666pt}{\resizebox{868894}{445645}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.75pt}{$\textstyle(1)$}}}}}{\raisebox{-2.26665pt}{\resizebox{759247}{445645}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.22499pt}{$\scriptstyle(1)$}}}}}{\raisebox{-2.26666pt}{\resizebox{759248}{445645}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.875pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle(1)$}}}}}}\big|

\displaystyle=\int\Big|A\bigg[O\Big(\frac{4}{1+y^{2}}\Big)\partial_{y}\big(\hat{\alpha}H\beta-\hat{\beta}H\alpha\big)\bigg]\Big|\cdot\big|AHw_{2}^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-2.26666pt}{\resizebox{868894}{445645}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.75pt}{$\displaystyle(1)$}}}}}{\raisebox{-2.26666pt}{\resizebox{868894}{445645}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.75pt}{$\textstyle(1)$}}}}}{\raisebox{-2.26665pt}{\resizebox{759247}{445645}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.22499pt}{$\scriptstyle(1)$}}}}}{\raisebox{-2.26666pt}{\resizebox{759248}{445645}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.875pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle(1)$}}}}}}\big|

\displaystyle\leq C\bigg(\|\partial_{y}^{2}\hat{w}^{\perp}\|_{L^{\infty}}+\Big\|\frac{\partial_{y}\hat{w}^{\perp}}{y}\Big\|_{L^{\infty}}+\Big\|\frac{\partial_{y}\hat{w}^{\perp}}{1+y^{2}}\Big\|_{L^{\infty}}\bigg)\sum_{i=0}^{4}\int\frac{|\partial_{y}^{i}w^{\perp}|}{1+y^{4-i}}\cdot|\mathbb{A}\mathbb{H^{\perp}}w_{2}|

\displaystyle\leq b\left|\log b\right|^{C_{1}}\sum_{i=0}^{4}\int\frac{|\partial_{y}^{i}w^{\perp}|}{1+y^{4-i}}\cdot|A\mathbb{H^{\perp}}w_{2}|

\displaystyle\leq b\,\delta(b^{\ast})\frac{b^{4}}{\left|\log b\right|^{2}}+o(1)\int|\mathbb{A}\mathbb{H^{\perp}}w_{2}|^{2},

where the
o(1)
is a positive constant that can be chosen sufficiently small. In other situations, we can control the terms directly by either
\|\partial_{y}\hat{w}\|_{L^{\infty}}^{2}b^{4}\left|\log b\right|^{-2}
or
\|\hat{w}\|_{L^{\infty}}\int|\mathbb{A}\mathbb{H^{\perp}}w_{2}|^{2}
, and thus the claim follows. Therefore we obtain

\displaystyle\rho_{2}\int{\rm\hat{J}}{\it\mathbb{H}}w_{2}\cdot\big({\rm\hat{J}}{\it\mathbb{H}}\big)^{2}{\rm\hat{J}}w_{2}\leq-\frac{\rho_{2}}{2}\int\big|\mathbb{A}\mathbb{H^{\perp}}w_{2}|^{2}+b\,\delta(b^{\ast})\bigg(\mathcal{E}_{4}+\frac{b^{4}}{\left|\log b\right|^{2}}\bigg).

(5.65)

Now for the second line of (
5.59
), from the definition of
k_{i}
(
5.18
), the coefficients satisfy
k_{1}(\rho_{1}^{2}-\rho_{2}^{2})\geq 0,k_{2}(\rho_{2}^{2}-\rho_{1}^{2})\geq 0
, and thus

\left\{\begin{aligned} &2\rho_{1}c_{2}k_{1}=\frac{k_{1}(\rho_{1}^{2}-\rho_{2}^{2})}{\Delta k\rho_{1}^{2}+\rho_{2}^{2}}\cdot\frac{4\rho_{1}^{2}}{\rho_{1}^{2}+\rho_{2}^{2}}\in[0,4],\\
&-2\rho_{1}c_{2}k_{2}=\frac{k_{2}(\rho_{2}^{2}-\rho_{1}^{2})}{\Delta k\rho_{1}^{2}+\rho_{2}^{2}}\cdot\frac{4\rho_{1}^{2}}{\rho_{1}^{2}+\rho_{2}^{2}}\in[0,4].\end{aligned}\right.

These together with (
5.27
) ensure the negativity

2\rho_{1}c_{2}k_{1}\int\mathbb{H_{\lambda}}W_{2}^{0}\cdot L\mathbb{A_{\lambda}}W_{2}^{0}\leq 0.

(5.66)

In addition, from the decomposition of
W_{2}^{0}
(
5.6
), we have

\displaystyle\int\mathbb{A_{\lambda}}\mathbb{H_{\lambda}}W_{2}^{0}\cdot LW_{2}^{0}=-\frac{4b}{\lambda^{8}}\bigg(\hskip-1.00006pt-\hskip-1.00006pt\hskip-1.00006pt\int\mathbb{A}\mathbb{H^{\perp}}w_{2}^{1}\cdot\frac{y\hskip 0.50003ptw_{2}^{0}}{(1+y^{2})^{2}}+\int\mathbb{A}\mathbb{H^{\perp}}w_{2}\cdot\frac{y\hskip 0.50003ptw_{2}^{0}}{(1+y^{2})^{2}}\bigg),

where by (
A.29
), (
A.33
), the first integral in the parenthesis is bounded by

\displaystyle\Big|\int\mathbb{A}\mathbb{H^{\perp}}w_{2}^{1}\cdot\frac{y\hskip 0.50003ptw_{2}^{0}}{(1+y^{2})^{2}}\Big|\leq\int|\mathbb{H^{\perp}}w_{2}^{1}|\cdot\Big|\mathbb{A^{\ast}}\bigg(\frac{y\hskip 0.50003ptw_{2}^{0}}{(1+y^{2})^{2}}\bigg)\Big|

\displaystyle\lesssim\int\big|{\rm R}{\it\mathbb{H}}({\rm R}^{2}w_{2}^{1})\big|\cdot\bigg(\frac{|\partial_{y}w_{2}^{0}|}{1+y^{3}}+\frac{|w_{2}^{0}|}{y(1+y^{3})}\bigg)\lesssim\delta(b^{\ast})\bigg(\mathcal{E}_{4}+\frac{b^{4}}{\left|\log b\right|^{2}}\bigg).

Moreover, from the positivity of
\rho_{2}
and
b
, the second integral is bounded by

\int\mathbb{A}\mathbb{H^{\perp}}w_{2}\cdot\frac{y\hskip 0.50003ptw_{2}^{0}}{(1+y^{2})^{2}}\leq\frac{\rho_{2}}{16b}\int\big|\mathbb{A}\mathbb{H^{\perp}}w_{2}\big|^{2}+O(b)\bigg(\mathcal{E}_{4}+\frac{b^{4}}{\left|\log b\right|^{2}}\bigg).

Putting these bounds together, we see

-2\rho_{1}c_{2}k_{2}\int\mathbb{A_{\lambda}}\mathbb{H_{\lambda}}W_{2}^{0}\cdot LW_{2}^{0}\leq\frac{1}{\lambda^{8}}\bigg[\frac{\rho_{2}}{4}\int\big|\mathbb{A}\mathbb{H^{\perp}}w_{2}\big|^{2}+b\,\delta(b^{\ast})\bigg(\mathcal{E}_{4}+\frac{b^{4}}{\left|\log b\right|^{2}}\bigg)\bigg].

(5.67)

Finally, collecting together (
5.60
), (
5.62
), (
5.65
), (
5.66
), (
5.67
), we obtain the control of the quadratic terms in (
5.23
): There holds

\displaystyle-\rho_{1}\int{\rm\hat{J}}_{\lambda}\mathbb{H_{\lambda}}W_{2}\cdot\big({\rm\hat{J}}_{\lambda}\mathbb{H_{\lambda}}\big)^{2}W_{2}+\rho_{2}\int{\rm\hat{J}}_{\lambda}\mathbb{H_{\lambda}}W_{2}\cdot\big({\rm\hat{J}}_{\lambda}\mathbb{H_{\lambda}}\big)^{2}{\rm\hat{J}}_{\lambda}W_{2}

(5.68)

\displaystyle+2\rho_{1}c_{2}k_{1}\int\mathbb{H_{\lambda}}W_{2}^{0}\cdot L\mathbb{A_{\lambda}}W_{2}^{0}-2\rho_{1}c_{2}k_{2}\int\mathbb{A_{\lambda}}\mathbb{H_{\lambda}}W_{2}^{0}\cdot LW_{2}^{0}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{b\big(1-d_{2}\big)}{\lambda^{8}}\bigg(\mathcal{E}_{4}+\frac{b^{4}}{\left|\log b\right|^{2}}\bigg),

for some constant
d_{2}\in(0,1)
. Here we note that the integral
\int\big|\mathbb{A}\mathbb{H^{\perp}}w_{2}\big|^{2}
in (
5.67
) has been absorbed by the one in (
5.65
) with negative sign.

Step 3
.
The
\mathcal{Q}_{3}
term
. According to the definition (
5.11
), we separate
\mathcal{Q}_{3}
into four parts

\mathcal{Q}_{3}=\mathcal{Q}_{3}^{1}+\mathcal{Q}_{3}^{2}+\mathcal{Q}_{3}^{3}+\mathcal{Q}_{3}^{4},

where

\displaystyle\mathcal{Q}_{3}^{1}

\displaystyle=-\Big(b+\frac{\lambda_{s}}{\lambda}\Big)\int{\rm R}\mathbb{H_{\lambda}}W_{2}^{0}\cdot\bigg[-\mathbb{H_{\lambda}^{\perp}}\bigg(\frac{(\varLambda V)_{\lambda}}{\lambda^{2}r^{2}}W^{\perp}\bigg)+\frac{(\varLambda V)_{\lambda}}{\lambda^{2}r^{2}}{\rm R}W_{2}^{0}\,\bigg],

(5.69)

\displaystyle\mathcal{Q}_{3}^{2}

\displaystyle=\int{\rm R}\mathbb{H_{\lambda}}W_{2}^{0}\cdot\bigg({\rm R}\mathbb{H_{\lambda}}{\rm R}\big[\partial_{t},\mathbb{H_{\lambda}}\big]\gamma\,e_{z}+{\rm R}_{\hat{w}}\mathbb{H_{\lambda}}{\rm R}\big[\partial_{t},\mathbb{H_{\lambda}}\big]W

\displaystyle+\partial_{t}\widehat{W}\wedge\mathbb{H_{\lambda}}W_{2}^{0}+{\rm R}_{\hat{w}}\big[\partial_{t},\mathbb{H_{\lambda}}\big]W_{2}^{0}+\big[\partial_{t},{\rm\hat{J}}_{\lambda}\mathbb{H_{\lambda}}\big]W_{2}^{1}\bigg),

\displaystyle\mathcal{Q}_{3}^{3}

\displaystyle=\int{\rm R}\mathbb{H_{\lambda}}W_{2}^{1}\cdot\Big({\rm\hat{J}}_{\lambda}\mathbb{H_{\lambda}}{\rm R}\big[\partial_{t},\mathbb{H_{\lambda}}\big]W+\big[\partial_{t},{\rm\hat{J}}_{\lambda}\mathbb{H_{\lambda}}\big]W_{2}\Big),

\displaystyle\mathcal{Q}_{3}^{4}

\displaystyle=\int{\rm R}_{\hat{w}}\mathbb{H_{\lambda}}W_{2}\cdot\Big({\rm\hat{J}}_{\lambda}\mathbb{H_{\lambda}}{\rm R}\big[\partial_{t},\mathbb{H_{\lambda}}\big]W+\big[\partial_{t},{\rm\hat{J}}_{\lambda}\mathbb{H_{\lambda}}\big]W_{2}\Big).

We estimate each one of these respectively. From the modulation equations (
4.34
), and the rough bound of the uncontrollable term (
5.12
), we have

|\mathcal{Q}_{3}^{1}|\lesssim\frac{O(b^{3})}{\lambda^{8}}\bigg(\mathcal{E}_{4}+\frac{b^{4}}{\left|\log b\right|^{2}}\bigg)\lesssim\frac{b\,\delta(b^{\ast})}{\lambda^{8}}\bigg(\mathcal{E}_{4}+\frac{b^{4}}{\left|\log b\right|^{2}}\bigg).

(5.70)

To treat
\mathcal{Q}_{3}^{2}
, we apply (
5.15
), (
4.34
) to get the brute estimate

\big[\partial_{t},\mathbb{H_{\lambda}}\big]W=\frac{b}{\lambda^{4}}O\bigg(\frac{w^{\perp}}{1+y^{4}}+\frac{\partial_{y}\gamma\,e_{x}}{1+y^{2}}+\frac{1}{1+y^{2}}\Big(\partial_{y}\alpha+\frac{\alpha}{y}\Big)e_{z}\bigg).

(5.71)

Now we consider the
L^{2}
bound of each term in the big parenthesis. By (
5.71
), (
A.16
), the first term in the brace of
\mathcal{Q}_{3}^{2}
is

\int\big|{\rm R}\mathbb{H_{\lambda}}{\rm R}\big[\partial_{t},\mathbb{H_{\lambda}}\big]\gamma\hskip 0.50003pte_{z}\big|^{2}\lesssim\frac{b^{2}}{\lambda^{10}}\int\Big|H\bigg(\frac{\partial_{y}\gamma}{1+y^{2}}\bigg)\Big|^{2}\lesssim\frac{b^{2}\,\delta(b^{\ast})}{\lambda^{10}}\bigg(\mathcal{E}_{4}+\frac{b^{4}}{\left|\log b\right|^{2}}\bigg).

Similarly from (
5.15
), (
5.44
), (
A.3
) the second term in the brace is bounded by

\displaystyle\int\Big|{\rm R}_{\hat{w}}\mathbb{H_{\lambda}}{\rm R}\big[\partial_{t},\mathbb{H_{\lambda}}\big]W\Big|^{2}

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{b^{2}\,\delta(b^{\ast})}{\lambda^{10}}\int\bigg[\Big|{\it\mathbb{H}}\bigg(\frac{w^{\perp}}{1+y^{4}}\bigg)\Big|^{2}+\Big|H\bigg(\frac{\partial_{y}\gamma}{1+y^{2}}\bigg)\Big|^{2}\bigg]
