\displaystyle=e_{z}\cdot(e_{z}+\hat{w})\wedge{\it\mathbb{H}}\varGamma=e_{z}\cdot\hat{w}\wedge{\it\mathbb{H}}\varGamma,

\displaystyle e_{x}\cdot{\it\mathbb{H}}\varGamma

\displaystyle=e_{y}\cdot(e_{z}\wedge{\it\mathbb{H}}\varGamma)=e_{y}\cdot{\rm\hat{J}}{\it\mathbb{H}}\varGamma-e_{y}\cdot{\rm R}_{\hat{w}}{\it\mathbb{H}}\varGamma,

\displaystyle=-e_{x}\cdot(e_{z}\wedge{\it\mathbb{H}}\varGamma)=-e_{x}\cdot{\rm\hat{J}}{\it\mathbb{H}}\varGamma+e_{y}\cdot{\rm R}_{\hat{w}}{\it\mathbb{H}}\varGamma,

from which the fourth term on the RHS of (

) is bounded by

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle-b\hskip-1.00006pt\hskip-1.00006pt\int\hskip-1.00006pt\frac{Z(1\hskip-1.00006pt+\hskip-1.00006ptZ)}{y}\big(e_{z}\cdot\tilde{\varPhi}_{0,1}\hskip-1.00006pt\wedge\hskip-1.00006pt{\it\mathbb{H}}\varGamma\big)\big(e_{y}\cdot{\rm\hat{J}}{\it\mathbb{H}}\varGamma\big)+\hskip-1.00006pt\int\hskip-1.00006ptO\bigg(\frac{|\tilde{w}_{0}^{2}|+|w|}{y(1+y^{2})}\bigg)\,|{\rm\hat{J}}{\it\mathbb{H}}\varGamma|^{2}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle b\hskip-1.00006pt\int\frac{Z(1\hskip-1.00006pt+\hskip-1.00006ptZ)}{y}\,\tilde{\varPhi}_{0,1}^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-2.26666pt}{\resizebox{868894}{445645}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.75pt}{$\displaystyle(1)$}}}}}{\raisebox{-2.26666pt}{\resizebox{868894}{445645}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.75pt}{$\textstyle(1)$}}}}}{\raisebox{-2.26665pt}{\resizebox{759247}{445645}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.22499pt}{$\scriptstyle(1)$}}}}}{\raisebox{-2.26666pt}{\resizebox{759248}{445645}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.875pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle(1)$}}}}}}\,\big(e_{x}\cdot{\rm\hat{J}}{\it\mathbb{H}}\varGamma\big)\big(e_{y}\cdot{\rm\hat{J}}{\it\mathbb{H}}\varGamma\big)+b\hskip-1.00006pt\int\frac{Z(1\hskip-1.00006pt+\hskip-1.00006ptZ)}{y}\,\tilde{\varPhi}_{0,1}^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-2.26666pt}{\resizebox{868894}{445645}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.75pt}{$\displaystyle(2)$}}}}}{\raisebox{-2.26666pt}{\resizebox{868894}{445645}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.75pt}{$\textstyle(2)$}}}}}{\raisebox{-2.26665pt}{\resizebox{759247}{445645}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.22499pt}{$\scriptstyle(2)$}}}}}{\raisebox{-2.26666pt}{\resizebox{759248}{445645}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.875pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle(2)$}}}}}}\,\big(e_{y}\cdot{\rm\hat{J}}{\it\mathbb{H}}\varGamma\big)^{2}

\displaystyle+O\bigg(b\hskip 0.50003pt\bigg\|\frac{\chi_{B_{1}}T_{1}}{y(1+y^{2})}\bigg\|_{L^{\infty}}\|\hat{w}\|_{L^{\infty}}\hskip-1.00006pt\bigg)\,\|{\rm\hat{J}}{\it\mathbb{H}}\varGamma\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+O\bigg(\,\bigg\|\frac{|\tilde{w}_{0}^{2}|+|w|}{y(1+y^{2})}\bigg\|_{L^{\infty}}\bigg)\,\|{\rm\hat{J}}{\it\mathbb{H}}\varGamma\|_{L^{2}}^{2}

\displaystyle-\int{\rm\hat{J}}{\it\mathbb{H}}\varGamma\cdot({\rm\hat{J}}{\it\mathbb{H}})^{2}\varGamma

(B.9)

\displaystyle\leq b\int(1\hskip-1.00006pt+\hskip-1.00006ptZ)A\hskip 0.50003pt\tilde{\varPhi}_{0,1}^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-2.26666pt}{\resizebox{868894}{445645}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.75pt}{$\displaystyle(2)$}}}}}{\raisebox{-2.26666pt}{\resizebox{868894}{445645}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.75pt}{$\textstyle(2)$}}}}}{\raisebox{-2.26665pt}{\resizebox{759247}{445645}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.22499pt}{$\scriptstyle(2)$}}}}}{\raisebox{-2.26666pt}{\resizebox{759248}{445645}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.875pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle(2)$}}}}}}\Big[\big(e_{y}\cdot{\rm\hat{J}}{\it\mathbb{H}}\varGamma\big)^{2}-\big(e_{x}\cdot{\rm\hat{J}}{\it\mathbb{H}}\varGamma\big)^{2}-\big(e_{z}\cdot{\rm\hat{J}}{\it\mathbb{H}}\varGamma\big)^{2}\Big]

\displaystyle+2b\int(1\hskip-1.00006pt+\hskip-1.00006ptZ)A\hskip 0.50003pt\tilde{\varPhi}_{0,1}^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-2.26666pt}{\resizebox{868894}{445645}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.75pt}{$\displaystyle(1)$}}}}}{\raisebox{-2.26666pt}{\resizebox{868894}{445645}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.75pt}{$\textstyle(1)$}}}}}{\raisebox{-2.26665pt}{\resizebox{759247}{445645}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.22499pt}{$\scriptstyle(1)$}}}}}{\raisebox{-2.26666pt}{\resizebox{759248}{445645}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.875pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle(1)$}}}}}}\big(e_{x}\cdot{\rm\hat{J}}{\it\mathbb{H}}\varGamma\big)\big(e_{y}\cdot{\rm\hat{J}}{\it\mathbb{H}}\varGamma\big)

\displaystyle+b\int\frac{V}{y^{2}}\,\tilde{\varPhi}_{0,1}^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-2.26666pt}{\resizebox{868894}{445645}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.75pt}{$\displaystyle(2)$}}}}}{\raisebox{-2.26666pt}{\resizebox{868894}{445645}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.75pt}{$\textstyle(2)$}}}}}{\raisebox{-2.26665pt}{\resizebox{759247}{445645}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.22499pt}{$\scriptstyle(2)$}}}}}{\raisebox{-2.26666pt}{\resizebox{759248}{445645}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.875pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle(2)$}}}}}}\big(e_{x}\cdot{\rm\hat{J}}{\it\mathbb{H}}\varGamma\big)\big(e_{z}\cdot{\rm\hat{J}}{\it\mathbb{H}}\varGamma\big)-b\int\frac{V}{y^{2}}\,\tilde{\varPhi}_{0,1}^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-2.26666pt}{\resizebox{868894}{445645}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.75pt}{$\displaystyle(1)$}}}}}{\raisebox{-2.26666pt}{\resizebox{868894}{445645}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.75pt}{$\textstyle(1)$}}}}}{\raisebox{-2.26665pt}{\resizebox{759247}{445645}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.22499pt}{$\scriptstyle(1)$}}}}}{\raisebox{-2.26666pt}{\resizebox{759248}{445645}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.875pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle(1)$}}}}}}\big(e_{y}\cdot{\rm\hat{J}}{\it\mathbb{H}}\varGamma\big)\big(e_{z}\cdot{\rm\hat{J}}{\it\mathbb{H}}\varGamma\big).

Step 3
.
Upper bound on the quadratic terms
. Recalling (
2.29
), for
\tilde{T}_{1}=\chi_{B_{1}}T_{1}
, we claim that there exists a constant
d_{1}\in(0,1)
such that

\forall y>0,\quad 0\leq(1\hskip-1.00006pt+\hskip-1.00006ptZ)A\tilde{T}_{1}\leq\frac{1}{2}(1-d_{1}).

(B.10)

We prove this inequality for
T_{1}
, but the same result follows for
\tilde{T}_{1}
immediately. First we note from (
2.30
) that

\frac{1}{y\varLambda\phi}\partial_{y}\big(y\varLambda\phi\,AT_{1}\big)=A^{\ast}(AT_{1})=\varLambda\phi,

which yields the explicit expression of
(1\hskip-1.00006pt+\hskip-1.00006ptZ)AT_{1}
:

(1\hskip-1.00006pt+\hskip-1.00006ptZ)AT_{1}=\frac{1+Z}{y\varLambda\phi}\int_{0}^{y}\tau\varLambda\phi^{2}(\tau)d\tau=\frac{2\log(1+y^{2})}{y^{2}}-\frac{2}{1+y^{2}}.

(B.11)

To show (
B.10
), we define the functions

f_{1}(y)=2\log(1+y)-\frac{2y}{1+y},\quad f_{2}(y)=f_{1}(y)-\frac{y}{2}.

By direct computations, we see

f_{1}(0)=f_{2}(0)=0,\quad\mbox{and}\quad\forall\hskip 0.50003pty>0,\quad f_{1}^{\prime}(y)>0\geq f_{2}^{\prime}(y),

where
f_{2}^{\prime}(y)=0
holds for
y=1
only. This actually implies

\forall y>0,\quad 0<\frac{2\log(1+y^{2})}{y^{2}}-\frac{2}{1+y^{2}}<\frac{1}{2},

which together with (
B.11
) yields (
B.10
). Consequently, the quadratic terms in (
B.9
) can be estimated. By the explicit formula of
\varPhi_{0,1}
(
3.22
), we have

\displaystyle b\int(1\hskip-1.00006pt+\hskip-1.00006ptZ)A\hskip 0.50003pt\tilde{\varPhi}_{0,1}^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-2.26666pt}{\resizebox{868894}{445645}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.75pt}{$\displaystyle(2)$}}}}}{\raisebox{-2.26666pt}{\resizebox{868894}{445645}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.75pt}{$\textstyle(2)$}}}}}{\raisebox{-2.26665pt}{\resizebox{759247}{445645}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.22499pt}{$\scriptstyle(2)$}}}}}{\raisebox{-2.26666pt}{\resizebox{759248}{445645}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.875pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle(2)$}}}}}}\Big[\big(e_{y}\cdot{\rm\hat{J}}{\it\mathbb{H}}\varGamma\big)^{2}-\big(e_{x}\cdot{\rm\hat{J}}{\it\mathbb{H}}\varGamma\big)^{2}-\big(e_{z}\cdot{\rm\hat{J}}{\it\mathbb{H}}\varGamma\big)^{2}\Big]

(B.12)

\displaystyle\leq\frac{b|\rho_{1}|}{\rho_{1}^{2}+\rho_{2}^{2}}\,\big\|(1\hskip-1.00006pt+\hskip-1.00006ptZ)A\tilde{T}_{1}\big\|_{L^{\infty}}\int|{\rm\hat{J}}{\it\mathbb{H}}\varGamma\big|^{2}\leq\frac{b(1-d_{1})|\rho_{1}|}{2(\rho_{1}^{2}+\rho_{2}^{2})}\,\|{\rm\hat{J}}{\it\mathbb{H}}\varGamma\|_{L^{2}}^{2},

and also

\displaystyle 2b\int(1\hskip-1.00006pt+\hskip-1.00006ptZ)A\hskip 0.50003pt\tilde{\varPhi}_{0,1}^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-2.26666pt}{\resizebox{868894}{445645}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.75pt}{$\displaystyle(1)$}}}}}{\raisebox{-2.26666pt}{\resizebox{868894}{445645}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.75pt}{$\textstyle(1)$}}}}}{\raisebox{-2.26665pt}{\resizebox{759247}{445645}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.22499pt}{$\scriptstyle(1)$}}}}}{\raisebox{-2.26666pt}{\resizebox{759248}{445645}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.875pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle(1)$}}}}}}\big(e_{x}\cdot{\rm\hat{J}}{\it\mathbb{H}}\varGamma\big)\big(e_{y}\cdot{\rm\hat{J}}{\it\mathbb{H}}\varGamma\big)

(B.13)

\displaystyle\leq\frac{2b|\rho_{2}|}{\rho_{1}^{2}+\rho_{2}^{2}}\,\big\|(1\hskip-1.00006pt+\hskip-1.00006ptZ)A\tilde{T}_{1}\big\|_{L^{\infty}}\int|{\rm\hat{J}}{\it\mathbb{H}}\varGamma|^{2}\leq\frac{b(1-d_{1})|\rho_{2}|}{2(\rho_{1}^{2}+\rho_{2}^{2})}\,\|{\rm\hat{J}}{\it\mathbb{H}}\varGamma\|_{L^{2}}^{2}.

For the last line of (
B.9
), from the smallness of
w
(
A.8
), (
A.20
), we observe

|e_{z}\cdot{\rm\hat{J}}{\it\mathbb{H}}\varGamma|=|e_{z}\cdot\hat{w}\wedge{\it\mathbb{H}}\varGamma|\leq|\hat{w}|\,|{\it\mathbb{H}}\varGamma|\lesssim\delta(b^{\ast})|{\it\mathbb{H}}\varGamma|,

and hence

\displaystyle b\int\frac{V}{y^{2}}\,\tilde{\varPhi}_{0,1}^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-2.26666pt}{\resizebox{868894}{445645}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.75pt}{$\displaystyle(2)$}}}}}{\raisebox{-2.26666pt}{\resizebox{868894}{445645}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.75pt}{$\textstyle(2)$}}}}}{\raisebox{-2.26665pt}{\resizebox{759247}{445645}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.22499pt}{$\scriptstyle(2)$}}}}}{\raisebox{-2.26666pt}{\resizebox{759248}{445645}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.875pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle(2)$}}}}}}\big(e_{x}\cdot{\rm\hat{J}}{\it\mathbb{H}}\varGamma\big)\big(e_{z}\cdot{\rm\hat{J}}{\it\mathbb{H}}\varGamma\big)-b\int\frac{V}{y^{2}}\,\tilde{\varPhi}_{0,1}^{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-2.26666pt}{\resizebox{868894}{445645}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.75pt}{$\displaystyle(1)$}}}}}{\raisebox{-2.26666pt}{\resizebox{868894}{445645}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.75pt}{$\textstyle(1)$}}}}}{\raisebox{-2.26665pt}{\resizebox{759247}{445645}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.22499pt}{$\scriptstyle(1)$}}}}}{\raisebox{-2.26666pt}{\resizebox{759248}{445645}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.875pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle(1)$}}}}}}\big(e_{y}\cdot{\rm\hat{J}}{\it\mathbb{H}}\varGamma\big)\big(e_{z}\cdot{\rm\hat{J}}{\it\mathbb{H}}\varGamma\big)

(B.14)

\displaystyle\lesssim b\,\delta(b^{\ast})\bigg\|\frac{V}{y^{2}}\,\tilde{T}_{1}\bigg\|_{L^{\infty}}\int|{\rm\hat{J}}{\it\mathbb{H}}\varGamma|\cdot|{\it\mathbb{H}}\varGamma|\lesssim b\,\delta(b^{\ast})\|{\it\mathbb{H}}\varGamma\|_{L^{2}}^{2}.

Eventually, we inject (
B.12
), (
B.13
), (
B.14
) into (
B.9
), and then (
5.61
) follows.

\square
